异型庙举例
中文

4. 异型庙举例

庙宇是崇祀建筑，与礼制建筑相近，所以形制比较呆板、保守、程式化，而且形式也比较封闭。不过，在楠溪江，乡土文化中人文气息浓郁，村落中建筑大多外向开敞，自然明朗。因此，也有一些庙宇，形制和形式接受了世俗性乡土建筑的影响，有所变化。

（1）人文主义对庙宇呆板、封闭性格的克服，突出地表现在苍坡村的仁济庙上。仁济庙是祭祀平水圣王的庙。据明初宋濂《温州横山周公庙碑》所记：

神讳凯、字公武，姓周氏。世居临海郡之横阳。……及司马氏平吴，与陆机兄弟入洛。张华荐之，神知晋室将乱，独辞不就。时临海属邑……地皆濒海，海水沸腾，蛇龙杂居之民罹其毒。神还自洛，乃白于邑长，随其地形，凿壅塞而疏之，遂使三江东注于海。水性既顺，其土作乂。永康中，三江逆流，飓风挟怒潮为孽，邑将陆沉，民咸惧为鱼。神奋然曰，吾将以身平之。即援弓发矢，大呼，衡潮而入，水忽裂开，电光中见神乘白龙东去，……莫知所在矣。俄而水势平，江祸乃绝。

……（按：以后屡屡显灵，除治河伏水等等之外，亦有救驾保土等其他神异）神初封于唐，为平水显应公，寻升王爵，赐衮冕赤舄。宋累加通天护国仁济之号，从祀郊坛，兼赐仁济为庙额。（按：元代复加尊号，入明之后降为横山周公之神）……仍命守土臣岁修祀事。

苍坡村的仁济庙位于宽阔的东池与西池之间。西池是由兼作堤防的寨墙拦水蓄成的，用于抗旱，也附会风水之说，用来克"火"。再请了这位平水圣王来坐镇，那就无虞水火旱涝了。仁济庙三面临水，左右两侧水面宽阔，正面是连接两池的大约两米多宽的水渠，有一道石板桥架在门前。临水的三面都设五间宽阔的敞廊，贴边置美人靠。于是这庙宇排除了崇祀建筑的封闭和呆板，不再与楠溪江乡土建筑的主调格格不入。它的风格亲切平易，与以东、西池为主体的公园和谐一致，真正成了公园的一部分，充满了生活气息。

村人们常常在它的敞廊里凭栏闲坐，享受无边的风月。从东廊观赏望兄亭，从西廊观赏寨门，角度最美，水色如玉，倒影似画，一片空明透净。庙前就是兼作寨墙的堤坝，宽14米，三棵数人合抱的古柏，虬枝鳞干，浓荫蔽日，给仁济庙罩上一层祥和的安谧气氛。

庙里和庙外一样，给人意外的喜悦。门屋和正殿都是五开间，与两侧廊庑一起围着一个小小的水池院，池里荷香轻溢。门屋，正殿、廊庑，都完全对水池敞开，形成一个统一的、然而很有变化的空间。

据《苍坡李氏宗谱》，仁济庙是十世祖伯钧公于宋孝宗淳熙八年（1180年）辞官返乡后造的，但现存的已不是宋代原构。三棵柏树是宋孝宗淳熙戊戌（1178年）筑堤时九世祖西斋公手植的，至今已经历了八百多年岁月，依然苍劲蓬勃。

（2）芙蓉峰下的广福寺，村民称它岩下寺。据岩上寺《峙岩崇果寺大隐智深禅师修建殿碑》记载，两寺同时启基于晋，赐额于宋。北宋仁宗皇祐二年（1050年）建成，明成化丙申（1476年）天台智者大师圭公募资重修。但据《两源陈氏宗谱·岩下广福寺》载：

岩下广福寺乃吾祖桂公舍建也，屡被火灾，屡施鼎建。正统间又遭火毁，公于天顺元年（1457年）捐稻五百石重建佛殿，又捐田拾亩为本寺香火。又捐资建寺前大桥，其石桥记。

不过，现存的建筑是晚近才重建的。

它的柱网很乱。正殿三间，左右又有些房间，都是草架，总面阔为26.0米，进深9.0米。左右厢房两层。前面只有院墙和随墙门，门外一堵砖照壁。照壁两侧各有古柏一棵，修直而高，传说是初建庙宇时种下的，有八百年的历史了。庙的形制很简单，近年又重建了五间后殿，总宽18.1米，深8.0米。很简陋。三间神橱供奉着卢氏娘娘、陈十四娘娘和其他五位娘娘，都是乡土神祇。正殿里早就没有了佛像，三间神橱还在。乡民们敬神，为的是解决生活中的实际问题，他们觉得本乡本土出身的娘娘们亲切贴心，远比西天佛祖可以信赖。

前院的侧厢，楼上楼下是禅房和客房，由于远离村落，有些香客需要留宿。原有一个不小的香积厨，现在已经荒废。后院的两厢没有恢复。

广福寺的选址很好。它位于峡谷口上，前面是比较平缓的坡地，一直伸展到里岙村、下园村，再远便是芙蓉村和岩头村。登山的道路在寺的南侧开始盘旋拾级而上。上去1公里左右是崇果寺，俗称岩上寺，现在已经没有了。路和寺之间是一条溪涧，涧底怪石突兀，水声清越。有一座拱桥过涧通向广福寺。广福寺是上山下山的路人歇脚之所。它的纵轴线正对着芙蓉峰，一进山门，抬头望去，三块赤红色的悬崖恰如芙蓉花绽开在屋脊之上，峥嵘奇丽，为庙宇生色。

广福寺的特殊之处在于，它的形式和风格完全和民间居住建筑一样，反映出民俗文化对崇祀建筑的影响。它虽然是三合院式，但外向开敞，两层楼的左右厢是悬山式的屋顶，有腰檐，向前的山墙错落有致，挑出的山檐和腰檐投下深深的影子在粉壁上，原木的构架也在粉壁上画出生动随意的图案。正殿正脊两端和北厢正脊前端都有很灵动的、很写实的、又很华丽有装饰性的吻，显然是早年的遗物，在重建现有的庙宇时拿来按上的。

楠溪江多雨，芙蓉峰多云。朝云夕雨，广福寺前团团烟雾横飞，时浓时淡。粉壁青瓦，隐约于浓烟薄雾之中，缥缥缈缈，朦朦胧胧，飘逸脱俗，却没有崇祀建筑那种严肃的与人隔离之感。

（3）另一座风格平易、小巧可爱的庙，是西岸村西北角山坡上的娘娘庙。它本是一座关帝庙，用来镇伏"反弓水"的。它很小，只有三开间的正殿和右侧一间香积厨。正殿总宽8.0米，进深6.0米。前院很窄，成为天井。推行计划生育之后，正殿里搬走了关公，供上了送子娘娘和两位乡土性的娘娘，两位功曹站在正厅中央，他们本来是关平和周仓，关公被请走之后，却把他们留了下来，稍加改变，转业服侍送子娘娘。由于地形复杂，小小的庙宇，形体却变化丰富。正门偏在左边，进门还得在狭隘的天井里横着上几步台阶。

左侧墙开门，木构件把粉墙衬托得格外素白，而粉墙又把大块蛮石砌的墙裙衬托得非常强劲有力。墙裙既不直又不平，明显地向外撇脚，大起大伏，大凹大凸，雕塑感极强。它四周都是高高低低的坡坎，落差大而又陡峭，大多用蛮石砌挡土墙。庙周围参参差差长着些老树，浓绿的树荫和一堵堵的蛮石墙，把白色的小庙映照得明亮夺目。小庙紧凑多变的轮廓，表现得很强烈。它野性十足，却又不失妩媚，乡民们对感性美的追求，给它以浓厚的人文性，而没有崇祀建筑的庄严性格。

崇祀杂神的庙宇普遍具有的人文性，反映出泛灵论的杂神崇拜本身的人文性。实用主义的崇祀，祈求的是现实生活中的福祉，而不是关心某种纯精神性的、抽象的哲理，或者某种玄远的、难以捉摸的空幻，如镜中花、水中月。因此，楠溪江的许多崇祀建筑，在整个乡土建筑系统中，能够相处得很和谐，远胜于宗祠。

English

4. Examples of Atypical temples

temples are sacrificial architecture, close to ritual architecture, so form relatively rigid, conservative, stylized, and form also relatively enclosed. However, in Nanxi River,乡土 culture rich in humanistic atmosphere; village buildings mostly outward open, natural and bright. Therefore, some temples, form and style accepted influence of secular乡土 architecture, changed.

(1) Humanism overcoming temple's rigid, enclosed character, prominently manifested in Cangpo village renji temple. renji temple worships Pingshui Holy King. According to early Ming Song Lian's "Wenzhou Hengshan Zhou Gong temple Stele":

Deity named Kai, style Gongwu, surname Zhou. Ancestral home Hengyang of Linhai Commandery. ... When Sima clan pacified Wu, with Lu Ji brothers entered Luoyang. Zhang Hua recommended him; deity knew Jin dynasty about to fall, alone declined. At that time Linhai subordinate counties ...

all coastal, seawater boiling, people living with dragons and snakes suffered poison. Deity returned from Luoyang, then reported to county magistrate, according to terrain, chiseled blockages and dredged, thus made three rivers flow east into sea. Water nature then obedient, land then cultivated.

Yongkang era, three rivers reversed flow, hurricane with angry tide disaster, county about to sink, people all feared becoming fish. Deity bravely said, I will level it with my body. Then drew bow shot arrow, shouted, entered against tide; water suddenly split; in lightning saw deity ride white dragon eastward, ... unknown where went.

Soon water leveled, river disaster ended. ... (note: later repeatedly manifested, besides river control water subjugation etc., also rescued emperor protected land etc. other miracles) Deity first enfeoffed in Tang, as Pingshui Xianying Duke; soon raised to king rank, bestowed ceremonial robe and red shoes.

Song repeatedly added Tongtian Huguo Renji title, sacrificed at suburban altars, also bestowed Renji as temple name. (note: Yuan again added honorific title; after entering Ming demoted to Hengshan Zhou Gong deity) ... still ordered local officials perform annual sacrifices.

Cangpo village renji temple located between broad East Pool and West Pool. West Pool formed by village wall also serving as dike blocking water, used for drought resistance, also conforms to fengshui theory, used to overcome "fire." Then inviting this Pingshui Holy King to sit in charge, then no worry about water-fire drought-flood.

renji temple three sides face water; left/right sides water surface broad; front is about 2m wide water channel connecting two pools, a stone slab bridge before gate. Three water-facing sides all set five-bay broad open corridors, edge set beauty's-rest railings.

Thus this temple eliminated sacrificial architecture's enclosure and rigidity, no longer incompatible with Nanxi River乡土 architecture's main tone. Its style friendly and plain, harmonious with park centered on East/West Pools, truly became part of park, full of life atmosphere.

villagers often sit by railings in its open corridors, enjoying boundless scenery. From east corridor view Wangxiong Pavilion, from west corridor view village gate, angle most beautiful; water color like jade, reflection like painting,一片 clear transparent.

Before temple is dike also serving as village wall, 14m wide; three ancient cypresses several people arm-span, twisted branches scaled trunks, dense shade covers sun, giving renji temple a layer of auspicious tranquil atmosphere.

Inside temple same as outside, gives unexpected delight. Gate house and main hall both five bays, together with two side corridors surround a small pool courtyard; pool lotus fragrance lightly overflows. Gate house, main hall, corridors, all completely open to pool, forming unified yet varied space.

According to "Cangpo Li clan Genealogy," renji temple built by 10th generation ancestor Bojun Gong after resigning and returning home in Song Xiaozong Chunxi eight year (1180); but existing not Song original structure. Three cypresses planted by 9th generation ancestor Xizhai Gong when building dike in Song Xiaozong Chunxi Wuxu (1178),至今经历 over 800 years, still vigorous and thriving.

(2) Guangfu temple below Furong Peak, villagers call it Yanxia temple. According to Yan-shang temple "Zhi Yan Chongguo temple Dayin Zhishen Chan Master Building Hall Stele" record, two temples simultaneously founded in Jin, bestowed name in Song.

Northern Song Renzong Huangyou two year (1050) completed; Ming Chenghua Bingshen (1476) Tiantai Wise Master Gui Gong raised funds rebuilt. But according to "Liangyuan Chen clan Genealogy · Yanxia Guangfu temple":

Yanxia Guangfu temple was our ancestor Gui Gong donated to build; repeatedly suffered fire, repeatedly rebuilt. Zhengtong era again fire destroyed; Gong in Tianshun first year (1457) donated 500 shi rice rebuilt Buddha hall, also donated 10 mu farmland for temple incense. Also donated funds build bridge before temple, its stone bridge record.

However, existing buildings rebuilt in recent times.

Its column grid very irregular. Main hall three bays, left/right some rooms, all hidden frames; total facade 26.0m, depth 9.0m. Left/right wing rooms two stories. Front only courtyard wall and wall-attached gate; outside gate a brick screen wall.

Screen wall both sides each ancient cypress, straight and tall, legend planted when first built temple, 800 years history. temple form very simple; recent years rebuilt five-bay rear hall, total width 18.1m, depth 8.0m. Very crude.

Three-bay shrine cabinets enshrine Lu clan Niangniang, Chen Shisi Niangniang and other five Niangniang, all local deities. Main hall long no Buddha images; three shrine cabinets remain. villagers worship gods to solve practical life problems; they feel local-born Niangniang friendly and close, far more trustworthy than Western Heaven Buddha.

Front courtyard side wings, upstairs/downstairs meditation rooms and guest rooms; due to far from village, some pilgrims need lodging. Originally had a不小 incense kitchen, now abandoned. Rear courtyard two wings not restored.

Guangfu temple site selection very good. It located at canyon mouth; front is relatively gentle slope, extending to Li'ao village, Xiayuan village, further is Furong village and Yantou village. Mountain climbing road starts south of temple winding upward. About 1km up is Chongguo temple, commonly called Yan-shang temple, now gone.

Between road and temple a stream; stream bed strange rocks abrupt, water sound clear. A stone arch bridge crosses stream to Guangfu temple. Guangfu temple is rest place for people going up/down mountain.

Its longitudinal axis directly faces Furong Peak; entering gate, looking up, three crimson-red cliffs just like hibiscus flower blooming above roof ridge, towering and beautiful, enhancing temple.

Guangfu temple's special feature: its form and style completely same as folk residential architecture, reflecting folk culture's influence on sacrificial architecture.

Although three-courtyard style, but outward open; two-story left/right wings overhanging gable roofs, have waist eaves; forward gable walls irregular; projecting gable eaves and waist eaves cast deep shadows on white plaster walls; raw timber frames also draw vivid casual patterns on white walls.

Main hall main ridge both ends and north wing main ridge front ends both have very lively, realistic, ornate decorative chi (roof ornaments), obviously early relics, installed when rebuilding existing temple.

Nanxi River rainy, Furong Peak cloudy. Morning clouds evening rain, Guangfu temple before it clouds of smoke横飞, sometimes thick sometimes thin. White plaster walls blue tiles, faintly in thick smoke thin mist, ethereal, hazy, elegant and transcendent, yet no sacrificial architecture's serious alienation feeling.

(3) Another plain-style, small and lovely temple, is Niangniang temple on northwest corner hillside of Xi'an village. It originally was a Guandi temple, used to suppress "reverse-bow water." It very small, only three-bay main hall and right side one incense kitchen. Main hall total width 8.0m, depth 6.0m.

Front courtyard very narrow, becomes skylight courtyard.

After family planning implemented, main hall removed Guan Gong, enshrined Child-Giving Niangniang and two local Niangniang; two merit officers stand in main hall center, they originally Guan Ping and Zhou Cang; after Guan Gong invited away, left them, slightly changed, transferred to serve Child-Giving Niangniang.

Due to complex terrain, small temple, form yet varied rich. Main gate偏 left; entering must cross sideways up several steps in narrow skylight courtyard. Left side wall door; timber components make white plaster wall格外 plain white; and white plaster wall makes large rough-stone wall base格外 strong and powerful.

Wall base neither straight nor flat, obviously outward splayed, big rises big falls, big concaves big convexes, sculptural feeling极强. Its surroundings all高低 slope banks, drop large and steep, mostly rough-stone retaining walls.

Around temple irregularly grow old trees; dense green shade and rough-stone walls make white small temple bright and eye-catching. Small temple compact varied outline, expressed very strongly.

It wild十足, yet not lacking charm; villagers' pursuit of sensory beauty, gives it strong humanistic character, without sacrificial architecture's solemn personality.

temples worshipping miscellaneous gods generally have humanistic character, reflecting animist miscellaneous god worship itself's humanistic character. Pragmatist worship, prays for real-life blessings, not concerned with some pure spiritual abstract philosophy, or some remote elusive illusion, like flower in mirror, moon in water.

Therefore, many Nanxi River sacrificial buildings, in entire乡土 architecture system, can coexist harmoniously, far better than ancestral halls.

本篇术语 Key Terms

14
书院和读书楼
中文

十、文教建筑

楠溪江人，历经宋、元、明、清四朝"科甲簪缨，珠贯蝉联"，而且出了著名的理学家和诗文作者。文风之盛，文运之隆，在村落体素上的表现之一，就是文教建筑的普及和类型的多样化。最常见的有义塾、书院、读书楼、文昌阁、戏台和其他以教化为目的的各种建筑物。

1. 书院和读书楼

（1）乾隆《永嘉县志·学校》说，永嘉的"乡间社学，本古党庠术序，亦较他州为多"。

学校的作用，一是读书准备考功名，二是教化。《茗川胡氏大宗祠谱·田川胡氏义塾规》里表达了宗族在兴办学校时的愿望："是以愿人文蔚起，高拔五桂之芳，门第常新，足兆三槐之瑞。……愿沼芹叠采，云路同登。"而在地方长官和乡绅看来，学校的意义更在于"此吏治所首重，民风所视以转移也"（乾隆《永嘉县志·学校》）。所以乾隆年间，为了平定民风强悍的楠溪江地区的动乱，县丞何森就在枫林办了一所楠溪义学(见乾隆《永嘉府志》)。学校是传播儒学正统、捍卫封建宗法制的基地，是"化民成俗"的工具。

地方学校有多种层次。社学、义塾，一般是学习识字、计数，也读经书准备考试的初级学校。书院则在准备科举之外，还有讲学和其他比较高级的文化学习。

宋、明两代，楠溪江确实有过相当高级的书院。最早的书院之一，溪口（古名菰田）村的东山书院，是南宋进士，曾任太子讲读的著名理学家戴蒙辞官之后创办的。由于成绩斐然，朝廷赐额"明文"。明代主持朱垟村白岩书院的朱广文和花坛村凤南书院的朱墨矓（名道魁，以字行），都是当地著名学者，著作宏富。墨矓公的学生王瓒中弘治九年（1496年）进士一甲二名，曾任两京国子祭酒、礼部侍郎，也有著作。传说明孝宗因此亲书"溪山第一"匾额赐给朱墨矓。豫章村的石马书院，也得本村退休的明代中书公胡宗韫来论学、题诗。这种讲授高层次学术的书院是少数的。

楠溪江中游几乎村村有学校，有些村落还不止有一所，例如花坛村除凤南书院外还有西园书院，芙蓉村在村中心有芙蓉书院，在"司马第"有书塾。这些学校虽然也叫书院，大约从明代中叶之后，随着楠溪江经济文化的衰退，都不过是初级的义学而已。在康熙《温州府志》里，溪口村的明文书院竟称作菰田塾，"所以启童蒙也"。

（2）书院建筑并没有特定的形制。后期的明文书院，即菰田塾，与当地的民居完全一样。地段南北长约24米，北端略呈箭矢形，宽度约19米，南端约宽15米。建筑物"工"字形，正脊走南北向。正座五开间，南北两端前后出轩，形成东西院落。建筑物四面开敞。对院落设廊。两个院落都有对外的门，东北门通村内，西南门通村口。有楼层。两轩的屋顶向西作歇山，檐角高翘，略有公共建筑的特色，从西侧村口望去，轮廓很生动。它的各个房间的使用情况不明。由于义塾的活动简单，各个房间大约也不会有特殊的功用。明间有太师壁，应该是供奉至圣先师或者朱子的位置。

芙蓉书院的形制很正规、庄重。它位于村中心，主要街道如意街的南侧，紧靠芙蓉池。它是封闭的内院式的，外墙东西长约52米，顺如意街延伸，南北宽约12.8米。由东向西，依次排列着泮池、仪门、明伦堂和讲堂。仪门前有旗杆一对，明伦堂前有3.2米宽、6.4米长的杏坛。明伦堂三开间，进深9米，后壁中央有神橱，是供奉孔子的。讲堂深也是9米，为扩大中央部分空间，将中楹梁架向两侧移开，所以开间不很整齐。讲堂后壁向一个很狭的采光天井并开两扇窗子。梁架的移动和开这两扇窗子，都是为了方便讲堂里学子们读书。

书院的南侧，顺南墙展开一所花园，宽约12米，长度大略与书院相等。花园里有土石混合假山和一条水渠，树木茂密。花园西端是书院山长住宅，三开间，进深6米，总宽度只有8米，净居住面积大约48平方米。有一道小门通向讲堂。

岩头村的水亭祠，本来是一所书院，《岩头金氏宗谱·宗祠》记载，嘉靖年间，桂林公"原创水亭为子孙课业计，自兹文学振兴，叨膺科第，至今胶序蝉联，绳绳不绝"。桂林公卒后，族众把它改成桂林公的特祠。

这座书院的规模是楠溪江最大的。它也是内院式，外墙东西长约65米，南北宽约25.4米。现存只有正堂一座，在西端，七开间，总面宽25.4米，进深13.4米。明间前半部宽约6米，梁架高大，空间宏敞。正堂前是一个大水池，南北宽与正堂总面阔几乎相等，只在两端沿墙各有一条甬路通向末间。池的东西宽度大约21.4米，正中有一道石板桥跨过水池直达正堂明间大门。桥的中央有一座亭子，现在已经毁去，还留下4米×4米的石板基座。水池之东，除东端的雕砖墙随墙门还在之外，建筑物都已经完全没有了。

故老传闻，原来这里从东到西依次还有照壁、泮池、月台和一个亭子。光绪《永嘉县志》里有一幅明正德四年（1509年）勒石的旧学宫图，明伦堂前也有水池和桥，桥之前是仪门，再前为儒学门。所传岩头水亭祠泮池后的亭子，很可能是仪门。

这所书院的东墙外和南墙外是宽约3-4米的水渠，向东再向北流向不远的进宦湖和镇南湖。渠东是村子的风水山汤山，山头有与书院同时建造的文峰塔。塔不高，据残石推算，不及5米。

这座书院和芙蓉书院，规模与村里的大宗祠匹敌，形制和风格也与宗祠相仿。文教建筑与礼制建筑一样，在村子里象征着上层雅言文化的力量。一般的义塾，等级低，采用的是民间居住建筑的形制与风格。建筑上的差别，反映出乡土文化内部的矛盾。

（3）书院在村里的位置没有一定的规制。芙蓉书院在芙蓉村的公共中心，但大多数的书院大约都在村子边缘风景优美的地方。例如明代造的豫章村石马书院，位于"渠口寨山之麓，后枕高岳石壁，下临溪流深渊"（见《豫章胡氏宗谱·古迹》）。塘湾村的姚姓书院，造在屏风岩侧，"岩后百武许有小瀑布，……下有重磨岩"（见《棠川郑氏宗谱·志地景》）。因此，书院常常列入楠溪江村落的"十景"或者"八景"。例如溪口村的"蒙公书塾"就是"合溪十景"之一。《十景诗》里写道：

宋第名儒系泽长，东山传有戴公庄；

湾中带草空绿，垄上龙鳞松尚苍。

鹤垟村的书院叫"环翠楼"，鹤垟八景里有"环翠书声"，诗曰：

幽阁峻嶒碧树荣，琅琅中有读书声；

半空掷地金钱解，五夜朝天玉佩鸣。

两首诗里写的书院环境都很清静，花木茂盛，大有利于潜心读书。花坛村的西园书院，"其中牡丹最盛"，"前有莲池"，每逢佳日，文士们前去观赏赋诗，书院起了公共花园的作用。楠溪江村落的建筑，早就有了明晰的环境意识。

（4）书院的建设和管理，一般由宗族负责，乡绅们常常是主持人。《茗川胡氏大宗祠谱·东山书塾记》生动而详尽地记述了这种情况：

茗屿胡氏旧有读书楼，在居之东，极其幽静。……尝延师以诲子弟，由是族属衣冠济济，咸知以礼律身。……（按：后读书楼废，文运遂衰）嘉靖癸丑（1553年）仲冬日，源泉、乔西二公谋于众曰：先世修周孔之业，吾宗绳绳，颇知大义。向也礼教不及前人，盖由家学失传之故耳。今宜续建精舍，以陶后进，庶几书香不泯，愿克肖者听。皆曰善。于是改卜地于东山之屏，厥土燥刚，厥土孔良。遂勾木植，庀工藏物，期年而舍成。廊庑壮丽，傍峻垣墉，望之岿然。名之曰东山书塾。量出田租若干石，以为累岁延师教育之费。

书院居然"廊庑壮丽，望之岿然"，可见是村子里很重要的建筑。

有一些乡绅，在家里构屋作书塾，课读族中子弟，或者读书自娱，如豫章村胡弼在南宋绍兴年间造的"给事厅"。岩头村则有桂林公在嘉靖年间改造的"森秀轩"，位于塔湖庙的南侧，本来是庙的香积厨，但位置优美，前临镇南湖，后傍右军池，所以桂林公把它改成了书斋。桂林公《题森秀轩·其二》诗中有二联：

门植垂堤陶公柳，院开洗砚右军池。

谈棋石磴风频至，烹茗松轩月上时。

轩中生活的文化品位很高。轩很素朴，三开间，总面宽10.7米，进深7.5米。环境清幽，确是读书的好地方，则轩也就"何陋之有"了。

English

X. Cultural-Educational Architecture

Nanxi River people, through Song, Yuan, Ming, Qing four dynasties "examination success and officialdom, pearls strung together," and produced famous Neo-Confucian philosophers and poetry-prose authors.

Literary style flourishing, literary fortune rising, one manifestation in village fabric is普及 and type diversification of cultural-educational buildings. Most common are charity schools (yi shu), academies (shu yuan), reading buildings, wenchang pavilions, stages, and other buildings for enlightenment purposes.

1. Academies and Reading Buildings

(1) Qianlong "Yongjia County Annals · Schools" says, Yongjia's "village community schools, originally ancient party-village schools, also more than other prefectures."

School's role: one, study to prepare for examinations; two, enlightenment. "Mingchuan Hu clan Main Hall Genealogy · Tianchuan Hu clan Charity School Rules" expressed clan's wish when founding school: "Therefore wish human culture flourishing, high pulling five-cassia fragrance, family gate always new, sufficient to omen three-huai auspice. ...

Wish marsh celery repeatedly gathered, cloud road together ascended." In local officials and gentry's view, school's significance more在于 "this is first priority of governance, folk customs look to it to transform" (Qianlong "Yongjia County Annals · Schools").

Therefore Qianlong era, to pacify Nanxi River area's strong folk customs unrest, county assistant He Sen founded Nanxi Charity School at Fenglin (see Qianlong "Yongjia Prefecture Annals"). School is base for spreading Confucian orthodoxy, defending feudal patriarchal system, is tool for "transforming people and forming customs."

Local schools have multiple levels. Community schools, charity schools, generally primary schools learning literacy, counting, also reading classics preparing for exams. Academies besides preparing for civil service exams, also have lectures and other more advanced cultural learning.

Song, Ming dynasties, Nanxi River indeed had quite advanced academies. One of earliest academies, Dongshan academy at Xikou (ancient name Gutian) village, founded by Southern Song jinshi, famous Neo-Confucian philosopher Dai Meng who served as crown prince lecturer, after resigning.

Due to outstanding achievements, court bestowed name "Mingwen." Ming dynasty Zhu Guangwen who led Baiyan academy at Zhuyang village, and Zhu Molong (name Daokui, known by style) of Fengnan academy at Huatan village, both local famous scholars,著作 rich.

Molong Gong's student Wang Zan passed Hongzhi nine year (1496) jinshi first rank second, served as two-capitals Imperial academy Chancellor, Vice Minister of Rites, also had works. Legend Ming Xiaozong therefore personally wrote "Xishan No.1" plaque bestowed to Zhu Molong.

Shima academy at Yuzhang village, also had retired Ming dynasty Zhongshu Gong Hu Zongyun from same village come to discuss learning, compose poems. Such academies teaching high-level scholarship are少数.

Nanxi River middle almost every village has school; some villages more than one, e.g.

Huatan village besides Fengnan academy also Xiyuan academy; Furong village has Furong academy at village center, and a school at "Sima Di." These schools although also called academies, approximately after mid-Ming, with Nanxi River economic-cultural decline, all不过 primary charity schools.

In Kangxi "Wenzhou Prefecture Annals," Xikou village's Mingwen academy竟 called Gutian School, "to enlighten children."

(2) academy architecture has no specific form. Later Mingwen academy, i.e. Gutian School, completely same as local residences. Plot north-south about 24m long, north end slightly arrow-shaped, width about 19m, south end about 15m wide. Building "I" shape, main ridge runs north-south.

Main block five bays, north/south ends front/rear protruding porches (xuan), forming east/west courtyards. Building四面 open. corridors face courtyards. Both courtyards have external doors; northeast door to village interior, southwest door to village entrance. Has upper floor.

Two porches' roofs westward hip-and-gable, eave corners high翘, slightly public building feature; from west village entrance view, outline very lively. Its various rooms' usage unknown. Due to charity school activities simple, various rooms probably no special function.

Central bay has taishi wall, should be position for worshipping Supreme Sage First Teacher or Zhu Xi.

Furong academy's form very regular, solemn. It located at village center, south side of main street ruyi street, close to Furong Pool. It is enclosed courtyard style; outer wall east-west about 52m long, extending along ruyi street; north-south about 12.8m wide.

From east to west,依次 arranged Pan Pool (scholar's pool), Yi Gate (ceremony gate), minglun hall (Ethics Hall), and Lecture Hall. Before Yi Gate a pair of flagpoles; before minglun hall a 3.2m wide, 6.4m long Apricot Altar (Xingtan). minglun hall three bays, depth 9m; rear wall center has shrine cabinet, for worshipping Confucius.

Lecture Hall depth also 9m; to expand central space, moved central bay beam frames to both sides, so bays not very regular. Lecture Hall rear wall toward a very narrow lighting skylight courtyard and opens two windows. beam frame movement and opening these two windows, both for convenience of students studying in lecture hall.

academy's south side, along south wall展开 a garden, about 12m wide, length roughly equal to academy. Garden has earth-stone mixed rockery and a water channel, trees dense. Garden west end is academy headmaster residence, three bays, depth 6m, total width only 8m, net living area about 48 sq m. Has a small door to lecture hall.

Yantou village's Shuiting temple, originally an academy; "Yantou Jin clan Genealogy · ancestral halls" records, Jiajing era, Guilin Gong "originally created water pavilion for descendants' study purpose, from then literature revitalized, received examination success,至今 school succession continuous, endless." After Guilin Gong died, clan changed it into Guilin Gong's special shrine.

This academy's scale is largest in Nanxi River. It also courtyard style; outer wall east-west about 65m long, north-south about 25.4m wide. Existing only one main hall, at west end, seven bays, total facade 25.4m, depth 13.4m. Central bay front half about 6m wide, beam frame tall, space spacious.

Before main hall is a large pool; north-south width almost equal to main hall total facade, only at both ends along walls each a corridor path to end bays. Pool east-west width about 21.4m; center has a stone slab bridge crossing pool directly to main hall central bay gate.

bridge center has a pavilion, now destroyed, still leaves 4m×4m stone slab base. East of pool, except east end carved brick wall-attached gate still exists, buildings completely gone. Elders rumor, originally here from east to west依次 also had screen wall, Pan Pool, moon platform, and a pavilion.

Guangxu "Yongjia County Annals" has an old school palace diagram carved in Ming Zhengde four year (1509); before minglun hall also pool and bridge, before bridge is Yi Gate, further before is Confucian School Gate. Rumored Yantou Shuiting temple pavilion after Pan Pool, likely Yi Gate.

This academy's east and south outer walls outside are 3-4m wide water channel, east then north flows to nearby Jinhuan Lake and Zhennan Lake. East of channel is village's fengshui mountain Tang Mountain; mountain top has wenfeng pagoda built same time as academy. Pagoda not tall, according to remnant stone推算, less than 5m.

This academy and Furong academy, scale rival village's main ancestral hall, form and style also similar to ancestral hall. Cultural-educational architecture like ritual architecture, in village symbolizes upper elite culture's power. Ordinary charity schools, low grade, adopt folk residential architecture form and style. Architectural difference, reflects contradiction within乡土 culture.

(3) academy's position in village has no fixed regulation. Furong academy at Furong village public center; but most academies approximately at village edge scenic places. E.g.

Ming-built Shima academy at Yuzhang village, located "foot of Qukou Zhai Mountain, back rests high peak stone wall, below faces stream deep abyss" (see "Yuzhang Hu clan Genealogy · Ancient Sites"). Tangwan village Yao clan academy, built beside Pingfeng Rock, "behind rock about 100 paces small waterfall, ...

below has Chongmo Rock" (see "Tangchuan Zheng clan Genealogy · Local Scenery"). Therefore, academies often listed in Nanxi River villages' "Ten Scenes" or "Eight Scenes." E.g. Xikou village's "Mengong School" is one of "Hexi Ten Scenes." "Ten Scenes Poem" writes:

Song dynasty famous scholar lineage long, East Mountain has Dai Gong manor;

Bay belt grass empty green, ridge dragon-scale pine still dark.

Heyang village's academy called "Huancui Tower" (Jade-Green Surrounding Tower); Heyang Eight Scenes includes "Huancui Reading Sound," poem:

Secluded tower steep green trees flourishing, ringing中有 reading sound;

Half sky throws ground gold coins解, five nights toward heaven jade pendants鸣.

Both poems describe academy environment very quiet, flowers and trees lush, greatly beneficial to concentrated study. Huatan village's Xiyuan academy, "among them peonies most flourishing," "front has lotus pool"; every fine day, scholars go to appreciate and compose poems, academy served as public garden. Nanxi River village architecture, already had clear environmental consciousness.

(4) academy's construction and management, generally by clan responsible; gentry often hosts. "Mingchuan Hu clan Main Hall Genealogy · Dongshan School Record" vividly and detailedly records this situation:

Mingyu Hu clan formerly had reading building, east of residence, extremely quiet. ... once invited teacher to teach descendants, thus clan members officials many, all knew to discipline self with ritual. ...

(note: later reading building abandoned, literary fortune declined) Jiajing Guichou (1553) mid-winter day, Yuanquan, qiaoxi two gong discussed with all: ancestors cultivated Zhou-Kong learning, our clan continuous, quite know great righteousness. formerly ritual education not equal predecessors, because family learning lost.

Now should continue build refined house, to cultivate later generation, hopefully book fragrance not extinguished, those willing to emulate listen. All said good. Then divined land at Dong Mountain screen, soil dry and firm, soil very good. Then procured timber, prepared labor materials, within year house completed.

corridors and wings magnificent, beside high walls, looking imposing. Named Dongshan School. Measured out several shi farmland rent, as annual teacher education expense.

academy actually "corridors magnificent, looking imposing," visible is very important building in village.

Some gentry, built house at home as school, taught clan children, or read for self-entertainment, e.g. Yuzhang village Hu Bi's Southern Song Shaoxing era "Geishi Ting" (Auditor's Hall).

Yantou village has Guilin Gong's Jiajing era renovated "Senxiu Xuan" (Luxuriant-Elegant Studio), located south of Tahu temple, originally temple's incense kitchen, but location beautiful, front faces Zhennan Lake, back borders Youjun Pool, so Guilin Gong changed it into study.

Guilin Gong's "Inscription on Senxiu Xuan · Second" poem has two couplets:

Gate plants embankment Tao Gong willows, courtyard opens ink-washing Youjun pool.

Playing chess stone steps wind frequently arrives, brewing tea pine studio moon rises time.

Studio life's cultural taste very high. Studio very plain, three bays, total facade 10.7m, depth 7.5m. Environment quiet, indeed good place for study; then studio also "how can it be humble."

本篇术语 Key Terms

15
文昌阁和文峰塔
中文

2. 文昌阁和文峰塔

和书院关系最密切的另一类文教建筑就是文昌阁和文峰塔。

（1）文昌阁祭祀"文昌帝君"。文昌本是北斗七星里的"司禄"，后来道教徒把它和梓潼大帝合一成为文昌帝君，"主文昌宫事"。事关功名，受到普遍祭祀。嘉庆六年（1801年）五月，清仁宗颁上谕："文昌帝君主著海内崇奉与关圣大帝相同，宜列入祀典，同光文治。"文昌阁的重要性大大增加。光绪《永嘉县志·建置·坛庙》里说：文昌庙"各乡皆有，书不胜书"。

在楠溪江，廊下、花坛、岩头、水云各村原来都有文昌阁。它们一般与书院有密切关系，如岩头村的文昌阁，创建于乾隆五年庚申（1740年），在塔湖庙北侧，面临智水湖，与南侧的森秀轩遥应，与水亭祠书院相隔一条流水潺潺的水渠。有的文昌阁兼作书院，如廊下村的。

与书院一样，文昌阁也大都造于风景优美地方。往往也成为村子的"十景"或者"八景"之一，吟咏的对象。花坛村的《十景诗》里有《文昌登眺》二首，其一：

杰阁凌云起，溪山入眼奇。……游身图画里，俯仰展须眉。

其二：

……竹疏风弄影，花暗鸟鸣阴。古树浮波静，空潭落月深。……

关于文昌阁选址的慎重，可于《珍溪朱氏合族副谱·改建文昌阁记》里见到。清初雍正年间，廊下村的乡绅们集资造了一座文昌阁，"然其地卑下，树木蔽障"，邑庠生朱闻轩觉得不满意。有一天，他邀朋友一齐到桂松岭上闲步，到了"广可亩馀"的一处山坳，"见潭水潆洄而涵影，秀峰耸拔以连云；文笔插其右，斗山踞其左；山川环绕，若绣若绮。因喜不胜曰：此真文昌阁基也，可以安神灵而聚风气矣。"于是在乾隆二年（1737年）将原来的文昌阁拆迁到这块新址重建。潭水涵影，秀峰连云，这新址的环境十分清丽。而文笔、斗山，又是文运之所寄；安神聚气，合乎堪舆风水的要求。所以《记》里说，这位闻轩公的两个儿子都中了举人。

创建文昌阁，是宗族的大事，关系到整个宗族的兴衰，因此成为乡绅们重大的善举之一。《珍川朱氏合谱·慎轩公传》里说这位朱光润捐重赀"创建文阁，……经营杰构，鸟革翚飞。"这是一幢两层的建筑，两庑用作书院讲堂。它的环境十分秀丽，"珍川之胜，于此称第一焉。"

这幢文昌阁是"鸟革翚飞"的杰构，所用的大约是歇山顶，在当地是最高的建筑等级。

（2）文峰塔从堪舆风水方面影响村落的文运，是被雅言文化接纳了的民俗文化。清人高见南著《相宅经纂》里说："凡都、省、州、县、乡村，文人不利，不发科甲者，可于甲、巽、丙丁四字方位上择其吉地，立一文笔尖峰，只要高过别山，即发科甲。或于山上立文笔，或于平地建高塔，皆为文笔峰。"楠溪江两岸山峦起伏，山体都是火山流纹岩，经过亿万年的侵蚀，许多村落能够在四近找到仿佛圆锥形的岩峰，可以认定为"文笔峰"，如蓬溪、豫章、廊下诸村，所以建造文峰塔的村子不多。在中游的村子里，大约只有岩头村和塘湾村各有一座。《棠川（按：即塘湾）郑氏宗谱·志地景》中有《十景诗》，其一为《巽吉山》，诗云：

耸然特立一高峰，恰位东南秀气锺；

巽吉更加崇宝塔，文风焕发笔潜龙。

可证塘湾村东南方巽吉山上原有文峰塔。

岩头村的文峰塔是明代嘉靖年间桂林公兴建的，与水亭祠书院同时。它位于汤山上，而汤山在村子的东南，也是巽方。山低，不能"高过别山"，所以造文峰塔来抢风水。

现在这两座塔都已经彻底毁坏。岩头村文峰塔还剩下几块残石，计檐部二块，塔身二块，平坐一块，基座一块。由残石看，这是一座小小的实心的灰白色大理石塔，六角形，楼阁式，每层各有三面有火焰式龛，中刻坐佛一尊。上下分为若干段，每段是一块整石，连挑檐也是从整块石头上凿出来的。从基座看，底层塔身每边长只有29.5厘米。现存两块塔身一块边长25厘米，一块22.5厘米，高度分别为118厘米和113厘米。两块檐部高分别为12厘米及14厘米。若塔为7层，则总高大约在2.5米左右。

文峰塔常有一个墨沼或者砚池与它搭配。塔影落入水中，称为"文笔蘸墨"，这是大有利于文运的风水。岩头村的文峰塔投影于水亭祠书院里的池中，就是这种风水。天然的文笔峰也一样。如豫章村和蓬溪村就都有墨沼砚池来映照文笔峰。《豫章胡氏宗谱》说到村口的墨沼："文笔峰倒影如笔尖之蘸水，秀气所锺，可使仕宦迭出，科第连登，文笔代不乏人。"

芙蓉村原来有一座文昌庙，在东南角巽位上，也为的是有利科甲。庙很小，正屋三间，院墙一周而已。现在已经残破不堪。

English

2. wenchang pavilion and wenfeng pagoda

Another type of cultural-educational architecture most closely related to academies is wenchang pavilion and wenfeng pagoda.

(1) wenchang pavilion worships "Wenchang Emperor." Wenchang originally was "Salu" (in charge of official salary) among Big Dipper seven stars; later Daoists merged it with Zitong Great Emperor into Wenchang Emperor, "governs Wenchang Palace affairs." Related to official success, universally worshipped.

Jiaqing six year (1801) May, Qing Renzong issued edict: "Wenchang Emperor's domestic worship same as Guansheng Emperor, should be列入 sacrificial canon, together illuminate cultural governance." wenchang pavilion's importance greatly increased.

Guangxu "Yongjia County Annals · Establishment · Altars-temples" says: Wenchang temple "every township has, too many to record."

In Nanxi River, Langxia, Huatan, Yantou, Shuiyun villages originally all had wenchang pavilion. They generally closely related to academies; e.g.

Yantou village's wenchang pavilion, founded in Qianlong five year Gengshen (1740), north of Tahu temple, facing Zhishui Lake, echoing south side Senxiu Xuan, separated from Shuiting temple academy by a gurgling water channel. Some wenchang pavilions兼作 academies, e.g. Langxia village's.

Like academies, wenchang pavilions mostly built at scenic places. Often also become village's "Ten Scenes" or "Eight Scenes" one, object of poetry. Huatan village's "Ten Scenes Poem" has "Wenchang Ascent-View" two poems, first:

Outstanding pavilion rises to clouds, streams-mountains enter eyes strange. ... Travel body in painting, looking up-down展 beard-brows.

Second:

... bamboo sparse wind plays shadow, flower dark bird sings shade. Ancient tree floats wave quiet, empty pool falls moon deep. ...

About wenchang pavilion site selection prudence, can be seen in "Zhenxi Zhu clan Combined Genealogy · Reconstruction of wenchang pavilion Record." Early Qing Yongzheng era, Langxia village gentry raised funds built a wenchang pavilion, "however its site low, trees block"; county student Zhu Wenxuan felt unsatisfied.

One day, he invited friends together stroll on Guisong Ridge, arrived at a mountain col "wide about one mu," "saw pool water winding and reflecting image, elegant peaks towering to clouds; writing brush inserts right, Di Mountain crouches left; mountains-rivers surround, like embroidery like brocade.

Therefore overjoyed said: this truly wenchang pavilion foundation, can settle deity and gather fengshui." Thus in Qianlong two year (1737) moved original wenchang pavilion to this new site rebuilt. Pool water reflecting image, elegant peaks to clouds — new site environment very clear-beautiful.

And Writing Brush, Di Mountain,又是 literary fortune's sustenance; settle deity gather qi, conforms to fengshui requirements. Therefore "Record" says, this Wenxuan Gong's two sons both became juren.

Founding wenchang pavilion, is clan major event, related to entire clan's rise-fall, therefore became one of gentry's major charitable acts. "Zhenchuan Zhu clan Combined Genealogy · Shenxuan Gong Biography" says this Zhu Guangrun donated heavy funds "founded Wen Pavilion, ...

managed outstanding structure, bird-leather phoenix-flying." This is a two-story building; two side wings used as academy lecture halls. Its environment very beautiful, "Zhenchuan's scenery, here ranks first."

This wenchang pavilion is "bird-leather phoenix-flying" outstanding structure; used approximately hip-and-gable roof, locally highest architectural grade.

(2) wenfeng pagoda influences village's literary fortune from fengshui geomancy, is folk culture accepted by elite culture.

Qing person Gao Jiannan's "House-Physiognomy Classic Compilation" says: "All capitals, provinces, prefectures, counties, villages, where scholars unfavorable, not producing examination success, can choose auspicious site in jia, xun, bing-ding four positions, erect a writing-brush sharp peak; as long as higher than other mountains,即 produces examination success.

Or erect writing brush on mountain, or build tall tower on flat land, all are writing-brush peaks." Nanxi River both banks mountains undulating; mountain bodies all volcanic rhyolite; after hundreds of millions years erosion, many villages can find near conical rock peaks nearby, can be认定 as "writing-brush peaks," e.g.

Pengxi, Yuzhang, Langxia villages; therefore villages building wenfeng pagoda few. In middle-stream villages, approximately only Yantou village and Tangwan village each one. "Tangchuan (i.e. Tangwan) Zheng clan Genealogy · Local Scenery" has "Ten Scenes Poem," one is "Xunji Mountain," poem:

Towering stands one high peak, exactly southeast elegant qi gathered;

Xunji additionally adds precious pagoda, literary style radiates brush hidden dragon.

Can prove Tangwan village southeast Xunji Mountain originally had wenfeng pagoda.

Yantou village's wenfeng pagoda built by Guilin Gong in Ming Jiajing era, same time as Shuiting temple academy. It located on Tang Mountain; Tang Mountain southeast of village, also xun position. Mountain low, cannot "higher than other mountains," so built wenfeng pagoda to seize fengshui.

Now both pagodas completely destroyed. Yantou village wenfeng pagoda still leaves several remnant stones: eave parts two, tower body two, flat seat one, base one. From remnant stones, this is a small solid gray-white marble pagoda, hexagonal, pavilion-style; each layer three sides have flame-style niches, center carved seated Buddha one.

Upper-lower divided into several segments; each segment one whole stone; even projecting eaves chiseled from whole stone. From base, bottom layer tower body each side only 29.5cm long. Existing two tower bodies one side 25cm, one 22.5cm; heights 118cm and 113cm respectively. Two eave parts heights 12cm and 14cm respectively.

If pagoda 7 layers, total height approximately 2.5m.

wenfeng pagoda often has an ink pool or inkstone pool paired with it. Pagoda shadow falls into water, called "writing brush dips ink," this is fengshui greatly beneficial to literary fortune. Yantou village's wenfeng pagoda projects into pool in Shuiting temple academy, is this fengshui. Natural writing-brush peaks same. E.g.

Yuzhang village and Pengxi village both have ink pools to reflect writing-brush peaks. "Yuzhang Hu clan Genealogy" says village entrance ink pool: "Writing-brush peak inverted image like brush tip dipping water; elegant qi gathered, can使 officials successive, examination success continuous, writing-brush generation not lacking people."

Furong village originally had a Wenchang temple, at southeast corner xun position, also for benefiting examination success. temple very small, main house three bays, courtyard wall一周 only. Now already ruined beyond repair.

小品建筑
中文

3. 小品建筑

楠溪江村落，大多有些小品建筑，如牌坊、亭、台和过街门之类。它们往往也有重要的文化教育意义。光绪《永嘉县志·古迹》说到坊表："古人志厥宅里，树之风声，可法可传，政教攸系。"所以牌坊一般造得很考究，位置在宗祠或村门附近，成为村落的礼制中心的一部分。

（1）花坛村的牌坊最多，有十二座，它们是：

"乌府"：明英宗正统年间建，在敦睦祠前，为兵科给事中朱良暹立。

"黄门"：同上。

"奕世簪缨"：明嘉靖进士朱腆立，与上二牌坊成组，合成敦睦祠的前导部分。

"乡贤"：明正德温州知府何文渊为学正思宁公立。

"宪台"：建于明弘治乙丑（1505年），温州知府李端，永嘉知县刘经为工部给事中朱良以立。在宪台祠堂前。（今祠堂已改小学）

"锺秀"：为举人朱腆立。（朱腆于嘉靖年间中进士）

"公直淳良"：明嘉靖丙寅（1566年）冬永嘉知县程文著为朱双溪立。

"翕和"：乡绅西华王公为朱莲溪立。

"溪山第一"：乡进士知凤阳县令朱义川立，朱腆书匾。

"为公宣力"：邑令伍公为朱小山立。

"鸢飞鱼跃"：中书舍人周令为朱幽独立，在西书院院内。

"松柏寒贞"：明崇祯二年（1629年）巡按史徐吉公为节妇邵孺人立。

这十二座牌楼排列在花坛村正面，即南面的横贯大路上，造成节奏分明、层次丰富的景观。这是楠溪江最独特的村落景观之一。可惜目前已经只剩下一座宪台牌楼和"溪山第一坊"了。

除了"松柏寒贞"外，其馀牌楼都是为表彰学人而建的。他们或是中了进士、举人，或是当了高官，或是在本乡有很盛的令誉，或是为乡里做了大好事。总之，都是足以荣耀宗族，"可法可传"的。

"溪山第一"传说是明孝宗应翰林院编修、两京国子祭酒、礼部侍郎王瓒的请求，题给朱墨矓。朱是花坛凤南书院的山长，王瓒的老师。现存的"溪山第一"坊位于大路的西端，其实是一座过街门。它的东侧，路北是初建于宋代淳祐己酉（1249年），扩建于明代成化的朱氏大宗祠，名桂芳堂；路南是一座兼作三官庙的茶亭。三者形成一个建筑群，是大路的起点，全村主要的礼制中心。

"宪台"牌楼在宪台祠前，为三间式，高5.95米，通宽6.28米，风格雄浑庄重。中央两棵柱子是石质方形的，两端则各有一对圆形木柱，前后并列。牌楼用斗栱，结构很特别。在明间的大额枋上先用两攒平身科斗栱前后各出两跳托住一根月梁式的秀美枋子。枋子上再有三层下昂托住挑檐檩。枋子的两端延长为次间的挑檐檩。两端的各两对圆木柱就支承这挑檐檩。

岩头村的进士牌楼和这座宪台牌楼风格相同，是明世宗赐给大理寺左寺右寺副，后来迁任瑞州知府金昭的。它造在金氏大宗祠正门前左侧，与正门成直角，但与岩头村北门进来的大街平行。进士牌楼四柱三楼，通面宽8.46米，明间宽4.60米。柱高4.40米，总高7.63米。尺度和体量都比花坛村宪台牌楼大，非常雄伟壮观，挺拔刚劲。中央两棵柱子石质方形，外侧两棵木质方形，柱子前后都有抱鼓石。明间大额枋之上有两攒平身科斗栱，也是出两跳托住一根月梁形的枋子，上面再有三层下昂，前后下昂后尾交叉托住脊檩。

次间有一攒平身科斗栱，单翘双昂。柱头科斗栱形制复杂而不正规，有45°斜出，多装饰性处理。三个顶子都是悬山式，两侧的出山大约1.65米。有一对比较细一点的方木柱前后并列支承出山的檩子。

花坛村的宪台牌楼和岩头村的进士牌楼，都造于明代中叶，形制和风格一致。它们都有柱子的侧脚，都有起结构作用的下昂和丁头栱，都用形式柔美的月梁和由生起及升头木形成的檐口和正脊的完整曲线。这些做法都是宋代建筑中常见的，而在明、清以来的官式建筑中早已绝迹。

花坛村敦睦祠（乌府）的大门和苍坡村的寨门，结构的形式和做法与这两座牌楼大致一样。

（2）岩头村的正门是北门，原名"仁道门"，是为纪念始迁祖刘进之（1095-1166年）公正处事，广施仁德，多次赈济灾民的义举而造的，早已毁坏不存。枫林镇则还完整地保存着明代成化年间建造的"徐尹沛尚义之门"，是供奉明孝宗旌表徐氏兄弟友爱的诏书的，所以俗称圣旨门。溪口村有"明文里门"，本来是南宋时造来悬挂宋光宗赐给戴蒙主持的书院的题额的。现存的显然不是原构。这三座门都成了村子公共中心的组成部分。封建的伦理教化和价值观就通过建筑环境而悄然向人心沁透。

圣旨门三开间，两层楼阁式，上为歇山顶，下有腰檐。它在枫林镇主街的北侧，一条垂直于主街的次街在它的门洞下向北笔直而去，但不长。隔着主街，对面是一个水池，叫圣旨门湖，湖里有长方形亭子一座。这个小小建筑群成了枫林镇的休闲中心，终日都有人坐在圣旨门门洞里两侧的台基上和湖中亭子里聊天。

明文里门在溪口主街的东侧，也是一个丁字路口。它比较小，只有三开间，单层，悬山顶。形制的级别比较低，可能不是原物。过门不远便是公园的大水池。

（3）楠溪江还有一对重要的小品建筑。田夫野老们远近传诵，流露着农家朴实的感情。这就是苍坡村的望兄亭和方巷村的送弟阁。

苍坡村李氏七世祖有秋山、嘉木兄弟二人。据《宗谱》记载，南宋建炎二年（1128年），哥哥秋山迁居大约二里外小溪对岸的方巷。兄弟情谊深重，往来频繁，"会桃李之芳园，叙天伦之乐事"。于是兄弟各造一座亭子，遥遥相望，寄托依恋之情。

望兄亭造在苍坡村2.5米高的寨墙上，正方形，边长5.0米。四棵金柱，十二棵檐柱，在四角组成四组柱子的集簇。檐柱间设美人靠。屋顶为歇山式，梁架有简单的斗栱。它北临东湖，南望远处方巷村的送弟阁，视野宽阔。它与西侧的仁济庙一起成为苍坡村重要的公共中心，并与仁济庙、大宗祠、寨门和寨墙上几棵参天的古柏构成了苍坡村正面很活泼灵动的轮廓线，这是楠溪江村落最动人的建筑景观之一。亭子微微侧转，呈现出最多变的形体，在这幅图画中起着突出的作用。

送弟阁形制与望兄亭相同，傍小溪上石板桥，遥望情意绵绵的望兄亭，正好观赏苍坡村景致的正面。

这两座小品建筑虽然已经不是宋代原构，但风格依然浑厚。它们的美人靠上经常坐着休闲的人，评古论今，兄弟友爱的故事，像春风化雨一样沁入乡民们的心田。望兄亭上一副楹联："礼重人伦明古训；亭传佳话继家风"，道出了这对小建筑的人文意义。

楠溪江不少村落还有一些流连风景，读书吟咏的亭榭。最有名的是鹤垟村的临清楼。《鹤垟八景诗》之一写临清楼：

危楼高架碧溪遥，鸟帽登吟白发年。……

幽窗帘卷看山坐，宝篆香残枕瀑眠。

在这楼里过着的是高级的文化生活。可惜这类文化建筑已经毁坏无馀了。

English

3. Small Architecture

Nanxi River villages mostly have some small architecture, such as memorial archways (paifang), pavilions, terraces, and street-crossing gates. They often also have important cultural-educational significance.

Guangxu "Yongjia County Annals · Ancient Sites" says about memorial archways: "Ancients marked their residences, established their reputation, worthy of emulation and transmission, related to governance-education." Therefore memorial archways generally built very refined, located near ancestral halls or village gates, becoming part of village's ritual center.

(1) Huatan village has most memorial archways, twelve of them:

"Wu Fu" (Black Office): Ming Yingzong Zhengtong era built, before Dunmu ancestral hall, for Bingke Jishizhong Zhu Liangxian.

"Huang Men" (Yellow Gate): same as above.

"Yi Shi Zan Ying" (Generations of Officialdom): Ming Jiajing jinshi Zhu Dian erected, grouped with above two archways, forming Dunmu ancestral hall's front approach.

"Xiang Xian" (village Worthy): Ming Zhengde Wenzhou Prefect He Wenyuan for Xuezheng Si Ning Gong.

"Xian Tai" (Censorate Terrace): built Ming Hongzhi Yichou (1505), Wenzhou Prefect Li Duan, Yongjia County Magistrate Liu Jing for Gongbu Jishizhong Zhu Liangyi. Before Xiantai ancestral hall. (Now hall converted to primary school)

"Zhong Xiu" (Gathered Elegance): for juren Zhu Dian. (Zhu Dian became jinshi in Jiajing era)

"Gong Zhi Chun Liang" (Public-Upright Honest-Good): Ming Jiajing Bingyin (1566) winter Yongjia County Magistrate Cheng Wenzhu for Zhu Shuangxi.

"Xi He" (Harmony): gentry Xihua Wang Gong for Zhu Lianxi.

"Xi Shan Di Yi" (Stream-Mountain First): village jinshi Fengyang County Magistrate Zhu Yichuan erected, Zhu Dian wrote plaque.

"Wei Gong Xuan Li" (Serving the Public): county magistrate Wu Gong for Zhu Xiaoshan.

"Yuan Fei Yu Yue" (Kites Fly Fish Leap): Zhongshu Sheren Zhou Ling for Zhu Youdu, in West academy courtyard.

"Song Bai Han Zhen" (Pine-Cypress Cold Chastity): Ming Chongzhen two year (1629) Censor Xu Ji Gong for chaste widow Madam Shao.

These twelve archways arranged on Huatan village front, i.e. south side transverse main road, creating rhythm-clear, layer-rich landscape. This is one of Nanxi River's most unique village landscapes. Unfortunately currently only one Xiantai archway and "Xishan Diyi Fang" remain.

Except "Song Bai Han Zhen," remaining archways all built to praise scholars. They either passed jinshi, juren, or held high office, or had great reputation in village, or did great good for village. In short, all sufficient to glorify clan, "worthy of emulation and transmission."

"Xishan Diyi" legend says Ming Xiaozong, at request of Hanlin academy Compiler, Two-Capitals Imperial academy Chancellor, Vice Minister of Rites Wang Zan, bestowed to Zhu Molong. Zhu was Huatan Fengnan academy headmaster, Wang Zan's teacher. Existing "Xishan Diyi" arch located at west end of main road, actually a street-crossing gate.

Its east side, north of road is Zhu clan Main ancestral hall, name Guifang Hall, initially built Song Chunyou Jiyou (1249), expanded Ming Chenghua; south of road is a tea pavilion also serving as Sanguan temple. Three form a building complex, is start of main road, village's main ritual center.

"Xiantai" archway before Xiantai ancestral hall, three-bay style, height 5.95m, total width 6.28m, style powerful-solemn. Central two columns stone square; both ends each pair of round wooden columns, front-back并列. archway uses dougong, structure very special.

On central bay's large eave beam first use two sets of ping-shen-ke dougong front-back each two jumps supporting a moon-beam-style elegant beam. On beam again three layers of xia-ang supporting挑檐 purlin. Beam both ends extend as side bays'挑檐 purlin. Both ends' each two pairs of round wooden columns support this挑檐 purlin.

Yantou village's jinshi archway and this Xiantai archway same style; was Ming Shizong bestowed to Dali temple Left-Right temple Deputy, later transferred Ruizhou Prefect Jin Zhao. It built left of Jin clan Main ancestral hall main gate front, at right angle to main gate, but parallel to street entering from Yantou village north gate.

jinshi archway four columns three floors, total facade 8.46m, central bay 4.60m. Column height 4.40m, total height 7.63m. Scale and volume larger than Huatan Xiantai archway, very majestic-spectacular, upright-strong. Central two columns stone square; outer two wooden square; columns front-back both have drum-embracing stones.

Central bay large eave beam above has two sets ping-shen-ke dougong, also two jumps supporting a moon-beam-shaped beam; above again three layers xia-ang, front-back xia-ang rear tails cross-supporting ridge purlin. Side bay has one set ping-shen-ke dougong, single qiao double ang.

Column-head dougong form complex and irregular, has 45° oblique projection, mostly decorative treatment. Three roofs all overhanging-gable style; both sides' mountain projection about 1.65m. Has a pair of thinner square wooden columns front-back并列 supporting mountain-projection purlins.

Huatan Xiantai archway and Yantou jinshi archway, both built mid-Ming, form and style consistent. They both have column ce-jiao (slanting), both have structural xia-ang and ding-tou-gong, both use gracefully-shaped moon beams and complete eave-ridge curves formed by sheng-qi and sheng-tou-mu. These practices common in Song architecture, but早已绝迹 in Ming-Qing official-style architecture.

Huatan Dunmu ancestral hall (Wu Fu) gate and Cangpo village stockade gate, structural form and practice roughly same as these two archways.

(2) Yantou village's main gate is north gate, originally named "Ren Dao Men" (Benevolence-Way Gate), built to commemorate founding ancestor Liu Jinzhi (1095-1166) for just handling affairs, widely practicing benevolence, repeatedly relieving disaster victims; already destroyed.

Fenglin Town still completely preserves "Xu Yinpei Shangyi Zhi Men" built Ming Chenghua era, for enshrining Ming Xiaozong's edict praising Xu brothers' fraternal love, so commonly called Shengzhi Gate (Imperial Edict Gate).

Xikou village has "Mingwen Li Men" (Mingwen village Gate), originally Southern Song built to hang Song Guangzong's bestowed plaque for Dai Meng's academy. Existing obviously not original structure. These three gates all became part of village public center.

Feudal ethical enlightenment and values quietly permeate people's hearts through built environment.

Shengzhi Gate three bays, two-story pavilion style, upper hip-and-gable roof, lower has waist eave. It north of Fenglin Town main street; a secondary street perpendicular to main street goes straight north under its gate opening, but not long. Across main street, opposite is a pool, called Shengzhi Gate Lake; lake has a rectangular pavilion.

This small building complex became Fenglin Town's leisure center; all day people sit on both side platforms in Shengzhi Gate opening and in lake pavilion chatting.

Mingwen Li Gate east of Xikou main street, also a T-junction. It relatively small, only three bays, single story, overhanging-gable roof. form grade relatively low, may not be original. Past gate not far is park's large pool.

(3) Nanxi River also has a pair of important small architecture. Farmers and elders传诵 far and near, revealing rustic simple feelings. These are Cangpo village's Wangxiong Pavilion (Elder-Brother-Gazing Pavilion) and Fangxiang village's Songdi Ge (Younger-Brother-Seeing-Off Pavilion).

Cangpo village Li clan seventh generation ancestors had Qiushan, Jiamu brothers. According to "Genealogy," Southern Song Jianyan two year (1128), elder brother Qiushan moved to Fangxiang across small stream about two li away.

Brothers deep affection, frequent往来, "gather in peach-plum fragrant garden, recount family-joy affairs." Therefore brothers each built a pavilion, facing each other from afar, expressing attachment.

Wangxiong Pavilion built on Cangpo village 2.5m high stockade wall, square, side 5.0m. Four gold columns, twelve eave columns, at four corners form four groups of column clusters. Between eave columns set meiren-kao (beauty-rest railing). roof hip-and-gable style; beam frame has simple dougong.

It north faces East Lake, south gazes at distant Fangxiang village's Songdi Ge, wide view.

It together with west side renji temple becomes Cangpo village important public center; and with renji temple, main ancestral hall, stockade gate, and several towering ancient cypresses on stockade wall forms Cangpo village front very lively dynamic silhouette — one of Nanxi River villages' most moving architectural landscapes.

Pavilion slightly turned, presents most varied form, plays prominent role in this picture.

Songdi Ge form same as Wangxiong Pavilion, beside small stream stone slab bridge, gazing at affectionate Wangxiong Pavilion, exactly viewing Cangpo village scenery front.

These two small architecture although not Song original structures, style still powerful. Their meiren-kao often sit leisure people, commenting past-present; brotherly love story, like spring breeze transforming rain, permeates villagers' hearts.

Wangxiong Pavilion has a couplet: "Ritual values human relations clarifies ancient teachings; Pavilion transmits fine tales continues family tradition," expresses these small buildings' humanistic significance.

Many Nanxi River villages also have pavilions for enjoying scenery, reading and composing. Most famous is Heyang village's Linqing Tower. "Heyang Eight Scenes Poem" one writes Linqing Tower:

Towering tower high above green stream distant, bamboo-hat reciting white-hair years. ...

Secluded curtain rolled viewing mountain sitting, precious incense exhausted pillow waterfall sleeping.

In this tower lived advanced cultural life. Unfortunately such cultural buildings already destroyed without trace.

本篇术语 Key Terms

17
戏台
中文

4. 戏台

南宋以来，浙东沿海一带就流行南曲戏文，称温州杂剧或永嘉杂剧。明祝允明《猥谈》说："南戏出于宣和之后，南渡之际，谓之温州杂剧。"徐渭《南词叙录》说："南戏始于宋光宗朝，……或云宣和间已滥觞，其盛行则自南渡，号曰永嘉杂剧。"

杂剧，如温州人高明写的《琵琶记》虽是文人作品，但与民俗文化有很深的关系。据光绪《永嘉县志·风土志·民风》载，温州人从隋唐以来就"尚歌舞"，"至于上元灯火，端午竞渡，争奇炫新，靡财奢费，略不顾惜。士女游观，靓妆华服，阗城溢郭。"明人陆容《菽园杂记》说到成化间浙江地方戏流行盛况时说："嘉兴之海盐，绍兴之余姚，宁波之慈溪，台州之黄岩，温州之永嘉，皆有习倡优者，名曰戏文子弟，虽良家子不耻为之。"清代道光年间，温州各城镇直到山乡都设乱弹馆，招农民子弟学戏。在永嘉县就有枫林、永强、楠溪、武石、港头浦、峰头、乌牛七座戏馆。其中至少有枫林、港头浦和楠溪三座戏馆在楠溪江中游。

地方戏盛行，需要很多演出场所，大多戏台在宗祠和庙宇里，演出常借岁年头祭酬神的机会。

不附属于宗祠和庙宇的独立戏台很少，只水云村有一座，造于清光绪壬辰（1892年），叫做赤水亭。赤水亭位于村子前沿循等高线蜿蜒而行的道路的外侧，正好在一道落差将近两米的断坎上。它的平面呈"凸"字形，由一个长边10.2米，短边9.6米的凉亭和一个4.3米见方的戏台组成。亭子贴近道路而相隔一条沟，有一条一米多长的石板桥相通，桥头还有一方拦阻猪狗的石板。亭子平日是村民休闲的场所，美人靠上，终日袒胸跳足，纵论上下古今，稻黍桑麻，悠然亲切。每逢演戏，亭子就成了后台。

戏台面向村外，距溪流大约18.0米。利用地形的落差，戏台面正好高于台前空地一米半左右。观众就在这空地看戏。戏台与亭子之间有一道太师壁，在亭子一侧设神龛供奉三官大帝。左右有"出将"、"入相"的上下场门。亭子有美人靠，戏台则只有沿边缘的二十多厘米高的小栏杆，以免妨碍看戏。两部分各有一个歇山顶，正脊互相平行，结构用勾连搭。天花上和太师壁上布满鲜艳的彩画，以故事题材为主，大多和戏文有关。图画之多，色彩之华丽，在楠溪江绝无仅有。所有柱子都有漆写的楹联，是里人清代孝廉陈子万撰的。亭子上的有：

观鱼槛外桃花浪；

系马阶前柳叶风。

戏台上的有：

听空中馨欬如歌乎？

是活现情形非戏也。

一面是潇洒闲适，词句纤丽；一面也并不板起脸来作道学说教。这座与休闲亭子结合的演出场所，气氛和宗祠、庙宇里的戏台，多少是有些差别的。

English

4. Stage (Xitai)

Since Southern Song, eastern Zhejiang coastal area popular southern-tune drama, called Wenzhou zaju or Yongjia zaju. Ming Zhu Yunming "Wei Tan" says: "Southern drama arose after Xuanhe, during southern migration, called Wenzhou zaju." Xu Wei "Southern Narrative Record" says: "Southern drama began Song Guangzong reign, ...

some say Xuanhe era already originated, its prevalence from southern migration, called Yongjia zaju."

Zaju, such as Wenzhou person Gao Ming's "Pipa Ji" (Tale of the Pipa), although literati work, has deep relation with folk culture.

According to Guangxu "Yongjia County Annals · Local Customs · folk customs," Wenzhou people since Sui-Tang "valued song-dance," "as for Lantern festival lights, Dragon Boat festival boat races, competing novelty, extravagant spending, no regard.

Men-women sightseeing, dressed up, filling city." Ming person Lu Rong "Shuyuan Miscellaneous Notes" speaking of Chenghua era Zhejiang local drama prevalence: "Jiaxing's Haiyan, Shaoxing's Yuyao, Ningbo's Cixi, Taizhou's Huangyan, Wenzhou's Yongjia, all have those practicing performers, called drama-wen disciples; even good-family children not ashamed to do so." Qing Daoguang era, Wenzhou towns even mountain villages all set luantan (chaotic-tune) schools, recruiting farmer children to learn drama.

In Yongjia County there were Fenglin, Yongqiang, Nanxi, Wushi, Gangtoupu, Fengtou, Wuniu seven drama schools. Among them at least Fenglin, Gangtoupu, and Nanxi three drama schools in Nanxi River middle stream.

Local drama prevalence, needed many performance venues; most stages in ancestral halls and temples, performances often took opportunity of annual festivals and deity-repaying sacrifices.

Independent stages not attached to ancestral halls and temples few; only Shuiyun village has one, built Qing Guangxu Renchen (1892), called Chishui Pavilion (Red-Water Pavilion). Chishui Pavilion located outside of road winding along contour line at village front, exactly on a nearly two-meter drop cliff.

Its plan "convex" shape, composed of a long-side 10.2m, short-side 9.6m leisure pavilion and a 4.3m square stage. Pavilion close to road but separated by a ditch; has a one-meter-plus stone slab bridge connecting; bridgehead also has a stone slab blocking pigs-dogs.

Pavilion平日 villagers' leisure place; on meiren-kao, all day bare-chested bare-foot, discussing past-present, rice-mulberry, leisurely-intimate. Every drama performance, pavilion becomes backstage.

Stage faces outside village, about 18.0m from stream. Using terrain drop, stage surface exactly about 1.5m higher than front open ground. Audience watches drama on this open ground. Between stage and pavilion a taishi wall; on pavilion side set shrine enshrining Three Great Officials (Sanguan Dadi).

Left-right have "Chu Jiang" (Exit General), "Ru Xiang" (Enter Minister) entrance-exit doors. Pavilion has meiren-kao; stage only has 20-plus cm high small railing along edge, to avoid obstructing viewing. Both parts each have a hip-and-gable roof; main ridges parallel each other; structure uses interlocking-join (goulian-da).

ceiling and taishi wall covered with bright colored paintings, mainly story themes, mostly related to drama texts. Number of paintings, gorgeousness of color, unparalleled in Nanxi River. All columns have lacquer-written couplets, composed by local Qing xiaolian Chen Ziwan. Pavilion's:

Watching fish beyond railing peach-blossom waves;

Tethering horse before steps willow-leaf wind.

Stage's:

Listening to空中 coughing like song?

Is living-present situation not drama.

One side leisurely-elegant, words delicate; other side also not sternly moralizing. This performance venue combined with leisure pavilion, atmosphere somewhat different from stages in ancestral halls and temples.

18
防御工程
中文

十一、其他建筑类型

楠溪江的建筑类型很丰富，一一胪列缕述，实在没有可能。但有些类型，虽然不如住宅、宗庙的重要，不写却会严重失楠溪江建筑面貌之真。在这一章里，择要写一写防御工程、路亭和商业建筑，以免缺失过多。

1. 防御工程

楠溪江的村落里，大多数民居和一些公共建筑是外向开放的，村子的公共中心、绿地、池塘和街巷系统也是外向开放的。它们向人坦露胸怀，像乡民一样纯真、笃诚，随时招呼邻里乡亲甚至过路人到家里闲话尝新。陌生人走进这样的村庄，不会觉得被人用警惕的眼光打量着，严密防范。

然而，这些笼罩在安祥和气氛之下的村落，有不少，尤其是河谷平原里比较富庶的，都用厚厚的石筑寨墙包围起来，精心地设计了它的防御功能。

（1）一个单姓的血缘村落，为了在异己的自然力量和社会力量之前求生存、求发展；在宗族关系干预之下，内部的对抗可能比较缓和。但村落的内聚力愈强，就愈会以一个宗族的整体来处理村落之间的事务，甚至个别村民之间的争端会酿成村落和村落之间的严重冲突。宗族关系又是封建的君臣关系的基础，所以每当国家改朝换代的时候，偏僻的楠溪江山村里也会有忠臣义士，演出悲壮的故事。至于整个封建时代都会有的农民起义或者草寇野匪，当然也不会见到进士牌楼就揖让而去。

即使在"科名鹊起"的南宋，温州太守杨简在《劝农文》里也说，温州的大患之一是"风俗好争"。元、明以后，风尚渐漓，到乾隆年间，《永嘉县志·风俗》里引《旧志》说：当地"其俗剽悍，其货纤靡，其士风任气而矜节"。老百姓剽悍好争，读书人任气矜节，这个"弦歌之声不绝于耳"的楠溪江，就常常免不了要有激烈的冲突。

楠溪江比较大的动乱，最早是北宋末年方腊义军的到来。方腊事败之后，本地人俞道安又率起义农民奋战了几年。

楠溪江的第一次大规模战争是元兵抗元。这次战争的英雄是芙蓉村的陈虞之。《两源陈氏宗谱》载："虞之公字云翁，登宋咸淳乙丑（1265年）进士第。始为扬州教授，淮东帅幹转两浙漕幹，除刑工二部架阁文字，迁秘书省校勘兼国史院编修，辟广王府记室参军。积借承议郎。元兵至温，公与族侄规公率子侄乡人千馀迎战于绿嶂洋，退登芙蓉岩，拒守三载。戊寅闻帝昺溺死，公自刎，岩遂溃。规公被执，不屈而亡。时从死者八百馀人，被掳者五十馀人。

"同时，廊下村也有太常寺簿，咸淳四年戊辰（1268年）进士朱猛善和一位省元，率乡勇一千多人筑城抗元，"然其起事仓卒，力不能支，于是奔溃逃遗，众乃瓦解"（见《珍川朱氏宗谱》）。芙蓉村和廊下村以及其他不少村子都被元兵焚惨。

元代，楠溪江山寇蜂起，有些村落聚兵自卫，结果称霸一方。元末，方国珍军曾经影响楠溪上游毗邻乐清的廊下、花坛一带。本地也有一些"互相攻杀"的武装力量，"焚劫最惨"。

明清易代之际，楠溪江有"不轨之徒，出没其间"。康熙十四年（1675年），三藩之一的耿精忠部与清兵曾在楠溪江大战。战后，清兵把许多村落"烧荡一空"。同时，又有"白巾"在楠溪江中游和珍溪上游骚扰，"惨毒万状，民不聊生"。太平军也曾经到珍溪一带，各村组织乡勇，"义旗四出，奏凯而旋"。

组织乡勇义兵，抗元抗清，抵御太平天国，剿灭山寇，都靠宗族的力量。宗族也使用这些军事力量为争田、争水、争柴山而挑起村落之间的大规模械斗。正在他们"协力"抵御太平军期间，枫林村和岩头村就为了争柴山（按：名为"金山头刀税事"）而反复械斗，杀人放火。岩头村的许多建筑物，包括三列堂皇的"进士第"被完全焚毁。芙蓉和岩头、芙蓉和溪南、塘湾和泰石等，都因争柴山或溪水而成了世仇，甚至把仇恨写进《宗谱》，嘱子孙永不忘记。

（2）由于不断发生各种各样的尖锐斗争，楠溪江有些村落，尤其是比较富裕有力的，造起了坚固的寨墙，完善的防御工程。目前保存得最完整的是芙蓉村、廊下村和坦下村的。其次是岩头村、苍坡村、花坛村的。许多村落的寨墙都只剩下断壁残垣。苍坡村的南墙、岩头村的东墙本都是蓄水的堤，当地叫做"垟"。前者可能造于南宋，后者造于明中叶。寨墙筑于何时失记。有些村的墙兼作抗洪的堤，如西岸、港头、周宅、渡头、坦下、廊下、花坛和塘湾的。有些村子背后以陡峭的山为屏障，只在前面筑墙，如坦下、塘湾和廊下村。大多数的围墙都综合这样多种的功能。《棠川郑氏宗谱·新城记》说塘湾村筑城的经过：

此地左有和龙、合冈，右有天岩、翼吉。四山环绕，名胜纷呈。所惜者独缺其前一面未能蔽御江风，以固我囿者也。于是乙未之冬，三时之暇，朝鸣、朝望诸翁因有筑城之议。此议一倡，举地乐从，老者鸠工，壮者累石，同力合作，不数月而告厥成功。额其门曰通德，即取郑公讲学之芳名焉。又其东曰惠门，即王右军所谓惠风和畅之义焉。总记之曰新城，即筑自近日之意焉。是城也，可以为屏藩，可以为锁钥，可以为村坊之风脉，可以为洪水之堤防。一举告成，百端藉利，殆有合乎王公设险以守其国之道欤？

这城筑于光绪二十一年(1895年)，正是塘湾与南邻泰石村械斗高潮时期。

因为寨墙有切实的防御作用，所以它是村落明确的边界，寨墙之外没有散落的住宅。寨墙所包的范围，因而通常大于当时的村落，有的大了许多，如花坛村的南部和廊下村的西部，都有大片的空地，仍作农业生产之用，而后才陆续变为宅基地。芙蓉村的南部和北部，各有内外两道寨墙，两墙之间很少房屋。

兼作抗洪或蓄水之用的墙很厚，而且高。岩头村的东墙，最高处达3.5米，厚10~13米。苍坡村的南墙高2.5米，厚14米。为了防御，常常在作为堤坝的墙体之上再加一道0.5米左右的女儿墙，或者叫胸墙。港头、周宅它和渡头临江一侧有一堵两阶的堤坝，第一阶的外侧有一米多高的胸墙，它与第二阶之间形成了一条巷道，仿佛有两道墙。纯粹为防御用的寨墙稍薄一点，矮一点。高度一般是1.8~2.0米，底脚厚1.2~1.5米，顶端厚0.6~0.8米。

墙是用大块蛮石砌的，下部石块比较大，往上渐渐减小。石块的长度平均在0.3米左右。大多数的墙上设方形孔洞，宽约15~18厘米，高约20厘米左右，距地多为65厘米上下，少数在1米以上。间距差别很大，近的不过3~4米，或者6~7米。这些孔洞是用来瞭望和施放火铳用的。

最雄伟的是廊下村的寨墙，它全长1500米，从西南到东北，保护村子的半边，另外一半则是山坡。墙外珍溪急泻而过，所以它兼作防洪的堤坝。外壁陡峭，从河床起算，高达8米，下部坡脚很大。内壁分三阶，每阶宽大约2米有余，高约1米，抗洪或者作战的时候，人员和装备可以在各阶上顺墙快速运动。

（3）寨墙辟门，方便出入。每村必有一座正门，称作"溪门"，形制比较庄重。芙蓉村东门是一座两层的楼阁，三开间，上覆歇山顶。木构架有斗栱，单翘双昂，昂作象鼻式，曲线很柔和。门的现状很通敞，次间设美人靠，明间没有台基，阙作车行道。但从柱子及地面痕迹看，明间原有闸门，楼梯曾傍北侧次间的前檐，则可能有过前檐墙。当初它也是有很强的防御性的。

苍坡村的溪门朝南，在东、西两池之间。这是一座牌楼式的门，三开间，中央一间前面设台阶，可以穿过，左右次间各为高台基，不通行。门的木构架与岩头进士牌楼、花坛宪台牌楼和敦睦祠大门三座明代建筑物大致相同，很可能也是明代的遗物。它的斗栱宏伟壮硕，双翘双昂，上层的昂前托挑檐檩，后尾压在脊檩的下面，是重要的斜向构件。下层的昂与上层的昂之间，在外侧垫着一个斗，在内侧则没有，所以二者不平行。所有的构件，关系十分明确。虹梁曲栱，形体非常流畅。比例匀称，构图饱满紧凑，这是一件木构建筑的珍品。

这座门本身的防御性不很强，但它的前面是一口半月形的水池，后面又是宽阔的东池和西池，只有一条石板桥在两池之间通向村里，所以，从整体看，它易守难攻，防御性还是不弱的。

现存的大多数寨门是石券门，在门洞里或门洞上造谯亭。例如廊下村的东北门和西南门，岭下村、坦下村和上烘头村的寨门，等等。石券门洞在相当于券的圆心位置设一根横梁，下面装板门，中央立门杠关死。廊下村的两座门的设计特别巧妙：东北角的寨墙向内曲折形成一个方形的凹袋，深12.0米，宽5.0米。东北门的石券辟在它的西侧，门洞宽约3.6米。这凹袋类似瓮城，攻门的敌人三面被围，而且很难直冲券门。凹袋之前是陡坡，陡坡之下是宽阔的珍溪，只有矴步可走，攻门因而更加困难。西南门略微简单一点，没有凹袋，不过西寨墙在这里左右错了几米，石券门向北辟在这错开的地方，守门人可以打击攻门人的背后。门前也是陡坡和溪流。

（4）谯亭大都是方形的，四棵金柱、十二棵檐柱。歇山顶。檐柱间设美人靠，开敞和易，本身没有防御性。如今有不少的改为三官庙，在后檐墙上砌神龛。岭下村和坦下村寨门的谯亭都很优美，飞檐远扬如展翼，美人靠、挂落等细节繁简得体，风格很典雅。优美典雅的谯亭有些是防御系统里的重要角色，如坦下的谯亭，可以瞭望村前的大片开阔地。廊下东北门的谯亭，正对着门前的矴步，监视过来的人。

芙蓉村的南门，东皋村的北门，渡头村的东门，都在门外有亭子，形式与谯亭一样，但大约都没有防御的功能了。它们轻巧的木构架和飘洒的飞檐，与粗犷沉重的蛮石砌筑的券门在一起，强烈的对比之下，画面很触目动人。

次要的寨门，或者只有一个券洞，或者横置一根石梁，都很简单。但有一些更简单的豁口，防御设计却很细致。溪南村的北墙，正对着世仇芙蓉村，中段有一个通达溪边的豁口。口西侧的石墙向前拐出，再向东延伸几米，正好掩住豁口，又与东侧的石墙形成了一个向东的开口，于是，进这个豁口就要经两次转折，而向前拐出掩住豁口的墙上还有监望和放火铳的孔洞。花坛村东门的设计更加周到。寨墙在临近溪边留了一个4.8米宽的豁口，在它的内侧大约5.5米处，正对豁口造了一面10.2米长的照壁；照壁上有几个瞭望和放火铳的孔洞，正好封锁住豁口。

照壁前还有一个大致半月形的水池，深可及腰，以阻滞敌人对照壁的进攻。照壁的北面有一座谯亭，已经改成三官庙。亭内用砖和灰塑做的神龛和木作藻井，非常华丽精致，色彩也很鲜艳，在同类建筑中独一无二。它们构图饱满，比例匀称，是上好的建筑艺术作品。

时过境迁，村落的寨墙现在完全没有了防御作用，那些没有防洪蓄水功能的寨墙，年久失修，大多倒坍，或者因修路、扩村而拆除了。开敞的村落边界和村内开敞的街巷、住宅一起，给人一种祥和安谧的气氛，教人忘记楠溪江在古老的日子里，不但有过文化水平很高的耕读生活，也有过仇恨。残存下来的寨墙遗迹，斑斑驳驳，曲曲折折，只增加村落景物的古意。有些段落，甚至诗情画意，盎然成趣。例如下园村的寨门，位于高坡之上，坡下有三个串连的水池，门、墙、池、路，一色用蛮石垒成，连作整体一片，隙地里生长着浓密的大树。鲜衣美饰的姑娘们挎着鹅兜，到池边浣洗，映照着苍劲的寨门，古今交融，很能引发人的思绪。

English

XI. Other Architectural Types

Nanxi River's architectural types very rich; listing and describing all one by one, truly impossible. But some types, although less important than residences and ancestral halls, not writing would seriously lose truth of Nanxi River architectural appearance. In this chapter, selectively write defense works, road pavilions, and commercial architecture, to avoid excessive omission.

1. Defense Works

In Nanxi River villages, most residences and some public buildings outwardly open; village public centers, green spaces, ponds, and street-lane systems also outwardly open. They expose their bosoms to people, like villagers pure, sincere,随时招呼 neighbors and relatives even passersby to home chat and taste new.

Strangers entering such village, will not feel watched with vigilant eyes, tightly guarded.

However, these villages shrouded in peaceful atmosphere, many, especially richer ones in river-valley plains, all enclosed by thick stone-built stockade walls, carefully designed their defensive function.

(1) A single-surname blood-relation village, to seek survival and development before alien natural and social forces; under clan relation intervention, internal confrontation may be relatively缓和.

But village's cohesion stronger, more will handle inter-village affairs as one clan whole; even individual villagers' disputes may brew into serious conflicts between villages.

clan relation又是 basis of feudal lord-minister relation; therefore whenever dynasty changes, remote Nanxi River mountain villages also have loyal ministers and righteous scholars, performing tragic stories. As for peasant uprisings or bandits throughout feudal era, of course will not see jinshi archway and politely leave.

Even in Southern Song with "examination names rising like magpies," Wenzhou Governor Yang Jian in "Persuade Farmers Text" also said, one of Wenzhou's great troubles is "custom fond of strife." Yuan-Ming after, custom gradually deteriorated; to Qianlong era, "Yongjia County Annals · Customs" quotes "Old Annals": local "custom fierce, goods delicate, scholar style willful and principled." Common people fierce-fond of strife, scholars willful-principled; this Nanxi River with "string-song sound never ceasing," often inevitably has fierce conflicts.

Nanxi River's relatively large unrest, earliest was late Northern Song Fang La rebel army arrival. After Fang La defeat, local Yu Dao'an again led uprising peasants fought several years.

Nanxi River's first large-scale war was resisting Yuan. This war's hero was Furong village's Chen Yuzhi. "Liangyuan Chen clan Genealogy" records: "Yuzhi Gong style Yunweng, passed Song Xianchun Yichou (1265) jinshi.

First Yangzhou professor, Huaidong commander secretary transferred Liangzhe transport secretary, appointed Xing-Gong two departments archive clerk, promoted Secretariat proofreader兼 National History compiler, recruited Guangwang Palace recorder. Accumulated to Chengyilang.

Yuan army arrived Wenzhou, Gong and clan nephew Gui Gong led nephews-villagers over 1000 fought at Lüzhang Ocean, retreated to Furong Rock, defended three years. Wuyin heard Emperor Bing drowned, Gong自刎, rock then fell. Gui Gong captured, unyielding died.

Those died with him over 800, captured over 50." Meanwhile, Langxia village also had Court of Imperial sacrifices registrar, Xianchun four year Wuchen (1268) jinshi Zhu Mengshan and a provincial first, led village braves over 1000 built city resisting Yuan, "however their uprising hasty, strength unable to support, then fled scattered, crowd collapsed" (see "Zhenchuan Zhu clan Genealogy").

Furong village and Langxia village and many other villages all burned by Yuan army.

Yuan dynasty, Nanxi River mountain bandits swarmed; some villages gathered troops self-defense, resulting dominating one area. Late Yuan, Fang Guozhen army once influenced Nanxi upper stream adjacent Yueqing's Langxia, Huatan area. Local also had some "mutually attack-kill" armed forces, "burn-rob most cruel."

Ming-Qing transition, Nanxi River had "unruly persons, appearing-disappearing among." Kangxi fourteen year (1675), one of Three Feudatories Geng Jingzhong's troops and Qing troops once fought large battle in Nanxi River. After battle, Qing troops "burned-swept empty" many villages.

Meanwhile, also "White Turban" harassed Nanxi River middle and Zhenxi upper, "cruel beyond description, people no livelihood." Taiping Army also once arrived Zhenxi area; villages organized village braves, "righteous flags everywhere, returned victorious."

Organizing village braves righteous troops, resisting Yuan-Qing, resisting Taiping, exterminating bandits, all relied on clan power. clans also used these military forces to provoke large-scale village fights over farmland, water, firewood mountains.

While they "cooperated" resisting Taiping, Fenglin village and Yantou village repeatedly fought over firewood mountain (called "Jinshantou knife-tax affair"), killing-arson. Many Yantou village buildings, including three grand "jinshi Di" completely burned.

Furong-Yantou, Furong-Xinan, Tangwan-Taishi etc., all became hereditary enemies over firewood mountain or stream water, even wrote hatred into "Genealogy," instructing descendants never forget.

(2) Due to continuous various sharp struggles, some Nanxi River villages, especially richer-powerful ones, built solid stockade walls, complete defense works. Currently most completely preserved are Furong village, Langxia village, and tanxia village. Next are Yantou village, Cangpo village, Huatan village.

Many villages' stockade walls only broken remnants left. Cangpo village's south wall, Yantou village's east wall originally both water-storage dikes, locally called "yang." former possibly built Southern Song, latter mid-Ming. Stockade wall construction time lost record. Some villages' walls兼作 flood-control dikes, e.g.

Xi'an, Gangtou, Zhouzhai, Dutou, Tanxia, Langxia, Huatan, Tangwan. Some villages behind use steep mountain as barrier, only build wall in front, e.g. Tanxia, Tangwan, Langxia. Most enclosure walls综合 such multiple functions. "Tangchuan Zheng clan Genealogy · New City Record" describes Tangwan village wall construction:

This place left has Helong, Hegang; right has Tianyan, Yiji. Four mountains surround, scenic spots abound. Regrettably only missing front side unable to block river wind, to secure our enclosure. Therefore Yiwei winter, three seasons leisure, Chaoming, Chaoweng elders thus had wall-building discussion.

This proposal once raised, whole land gladly followed; elders organized labor, strong men piled stones, worked together, within months completed. Named gate Tongde, taking Zheng Gong lecturing's fine name. Its east called Hui Gate, taking Wang Youjun's "gentle breeze pleasant" meaning. Overall called New City, meaning built recently.

This city, can be screen, can be lock-key, can be village's fengshui vein, can be flood dike. One act completed, hundred benefits rely, perhaps conforms to kings setting strategic passes to defend their state way?

This city built Guangxu twenty-one year (1895), exactly Tangwan and south neighbor Taishi village fight climax period.

Because stockade wall has practical defensive role, it is village's clear boundary; outside stockade wall no scattered residences. stockade wall enclosed area, therefore usually larger than village then, some much larger, e.g.

Huatan village's south and Langxia village's west, both have large open land, still agricultural use, later gradually became residential plots. Furong village's south and north, each has inner-outer two stockade walls, between two walls few houses.

Walls兼作 flood-control or water-storage very thick and high. Yantou village's east wall, highest point 3.5m, thick 10-13m. Cangpo village's south wall 2.5m high, 14m thick. For defense, often add about 0.5m parapet wall, or breast wall, on dike body.

Gangtou, Zhouzhai, Dutou river side has a two-step dike; first step outside has over 1m breast wall, between it and second step forms a lane, as if two walls. Purely defensive stockade wall slightly thinner, lower. Height generally 1.8-2.0m, base thick 1.2-1.5m, top thick 0.6-0.8m.

Walls built with large rough stones; lower stones larger, upward gradually smaller. Stone length average about 0.3m. Most walls set square holes, width about 15-18cm, height about 20cm, above ground mostly 65cm,少数 over 1m. Spacing varies greatly, close only 3-4m, or 6-7m. These holes for lookout and firing matchlocks.

Most majestic is Langxia village's stockade wall, total length 1500m, from southwest to northeast, protects half village, other half mountain slope. Outside wall Zhenxi stream rushes past, so it兼作 flood dike. Outer wall steep, from riverbed calculated, up to 8m high, lower footing large.

Inner wall divided three steps; each step about 2m+ wide, about 1m high; during flood or battle, personnel and equipment can move quickly along wall on each step.

(3) stockade wall opens gates for access. Each village必有 one main gate, called "Stream Gate" (Xi Men), form relatively solemn. Furong village east gate is a two-story tower, three bays, hip-and-gable roof. Timber frame has dougong, single qiao double ang, ang elephant-trunk style, curve very soft.

Gate current state very open; side bays have meiren-kao; central bay no platform, used as carriage passage. But from column and ground traces, central bay originally had sluice gate; staircase once beside north side bay front eave, then possibly had front eave wall. Originally it also had strong defensiveness.

Cangpo village's stream gate faces south, between east-west two pools. This is a paifang-style gate, three bays; central bay front has steps, can pass through; left-right side bays each high platform, not passable.

Gate's timber frame roughly same as Yantou jinshi archway, Huatan Xiantai archway, and Dunmu ancestral hall gate three Ming buildings, likely also Ming remnant. Its dougong grand-robust, double qiao double ang; upper ang front supports eave purlin, rear tail presses under ridge purlin, important diagonal member.

Between lower ang and upper ang, outside pads a dou, inside none, so two not parallel. All members, relations very clear. Rainbow beams curved brackets, forms very fluent. Proportion balanced, composition full-compact; this is a timber architecture treasure.

This gate itself defensiveness not very strong, but its front is a half-moon pool, behind又是 broad east pool and west pool, only a stone slab bridge between two pools leads to village; therefore overall, easy to defend difficult to attack, defensiveness still not weak.

Existing most stockade gates are stone arch gates; in gate opening or above gate built watch pavilion (qiao ting). E.g. Langxia village's northeast gate and southwest gate, Lingxia village, tanxia village, Shanghongtou village stockade gates, etc.

Stone arch opening at arch center position sets a crossbeam; below installs plank door; center sets door bar locked. Langxia village's two gates design especially clever: northeast corner stockade wall inward bends forming a square recessed pocket, depth 12.0m, width 5.0m.

Northeast gate's stone arch opened on its west side, gate opening about 3.6m wide. This recessed pocket similar to barbican (weng cheng); attacking enemy surrounded on three sides, and difficult to charge arch gate.

Before recessed pocket is steep slope; below steep slope is broad Zhenxi stream, only stepping stones (ding bu) can walk; attacking gate thus more difficult.

Southwest gate slightly simpler, no recessed pocket; but west stockade wall here staggered several meters, stone arch gate north opened at this staggered place; gate defenders can strike attacker's back. Before gate also steep slope and stream.

(4) Watch pavilions mostly square, four gold columns, twelve eave columns. Hip-and-gable roof. Between eave columns set meiren-kao, open-easy, itself no defensiveness. Now many changed to Sanguan temple, back eave wall built shrine.

Lingxia village and tanxia village stockade gate watch pavilions both very elegant; flying eaves far spread like wings; meiren-kao, hanging lattice etc. details appropriate, style very refined. Elegant watch pavilions some are important roles in defense system, e.g. Tanxia's watch pavilion, can lookout large open area before village.

Langxia northeast gate's watch pavilion, directly facing stepping stones before gate, monitors incoming people.

Furong village's south gate, Donggao village's north gate, Dutou village's east gate, all have pavilions outside gate, form same as watch pavilion, but approximately no defensive function anymore. Their light timber frames and flowing flying eaves, together with rough-heavy rough-stone arch gates, under strong contrast, picture very striking-moving.

Secondary stockade gates, either only one arch opening, or one stone beam across, all simple. But some simpler gaps, defensive design very detailed. Xinan village's north wall, directly facing hereditary enemy Furong village, middle section has a gap to stream.

Gap west side stone wall forward bends, then extends east several meters, exactly covers gap, and with east side stone wall forms an east-facing opening; therefore entering this gap requires two turns; and forward-bent covering gap wall also has lookout and matchlock holes. Huatan village east gate design more thorough.

stockade wall near stream leaves a 4.8m wide gap; at its inside about 5.5m, directly facing gap built a 10.2m long screen wall; screen wall has several lookout and matchlock holes, exactly blocks gap. Before screen wall also a roughly half-moon pool, waist-deep, to阻滞 enemy attack on screen wall.

Screen wall north has a watch pavilion, already changed to Sanguan temple. Pavilion interior brick-plaster shrine and timber caisson ceiling, very gorgeous-refined, colors also bright, unique among similar buildings. Their composition full, proportion balanced, are excellent architectural art works.

Times changed, village stockade walls now completely no defensive role; those without flood-water-storage function, years disrepair, mostly collapsed, or removed due to road-building village-expansion.

Open village boundary and村内 open streets-lanes residences together, give people peaceful atmosphere, teaching people forget Nanxi River in old days, not only had high-cultural-level farming-reading life, but also had hatred. Remaining stockade wall ruins, mottled, winding, only increase village scenery's ancient charm.

Some sections, even poetic-painterly, full of interest. E.g. Xiayuan village's stockade gate, located on high slope; below slope three connected pools; gate, wall, pool, road, all rough-stone built, integrated as one whole; gaps grow dense big trees.

Colorfully-dressed girls carrying goose baskets, go to pool wash, reflecting vigorous stockade gate; ancient-modern blend, very evocative.

路亭
中文

2. 路亭

楠溪江流域，除了中游以下的主流和几个大支流可以通小船和竹筏之外，全部交通，就只靠蜿蜒在山岭之上、沟壑之间的石子小路了。荷重负担，跋涉在这些小路上，异常艰辛。为了给旅人和樵子们歇脚，乡民们沿路造起了一座座凉亭。亭子大多是集资兴建的，由宗祠组织，少量由个人捐造，为父母积德祈福，叫做"孝子亭"。

山路上，亭子之间相距大约五里，选址多近清冽的泉水。亭边通常植树，浓荫覆盖，夏季可供路人一个凉爽的休息。楠溪江流域至少有过一千多座路亭，现在还有274座，其中有不少是明、清两代遗构。从路口乡的十二盘到渠口乡的泰石村，一路亭子保存得很完好，其中有11座造于明代，方形的石柱上还隐约可见"明万历己卯冬月建"或"大明崇祯七年八月建"等铭刻。

路边的亭子，一般比较简单，有些不过一间，硬山搁檩。大一点的有三间。河谷平川上，尤其邻近村落的，亭子多为正方形，面阔在4~6米左右，四棵金柱，十二棵檐柱，上覆歇山顶，形式比较精致，不过形制和形式变化不多。岩头村北双浚头的一座，用悬山顶，溪南村的一座，用粉墙封住前檐，留一个方形的门，但两侧仍然敞开。在碧绿的田野里，它的粉墙耀眼地明亮。西源村有一座八柱的重檐八角攒尖亭，造于清顺治十七年（1660年）。碧莲镇也有一座重檐八角攒尖亭，多一圈内槽柱子，一共十六棵，造于清道光十七年（1837年）。

在越雁荡山脉去乐清县的山路边，有圆形和六角形的亭子各一座。上烘头村的路亭和埭头村至水云村中途的名为"肃王殿"的路亭，背靠山脚，前檐有两层，上层进深大于下层，坐实在山坡上，且有侧门直通山坡。这些是变化比较大的。那座肃王殿飞檐飘洒，大方而灵动。从它的廊下远望山脚的田畴村落，眼界空阔。

亭子里设美人靠或者栏杆凳，给疲累的过客休息，也自然成了他们辨认道路、计算脚程的标志。暴雨的黄昏，烈日的正午，那仿佛微笑着迎候归客的亭子，能给攀跻而来的路人多少亲切的慰藉。有些近村的亭子，每年从夏初端午到秋末重阳由村人供应药茶和暑药。柱子上挂着一串串金黄的草鞋，有大有小，赶路人坐落下来，翻过脚板，看草鞋破了，自己伸手取一双来换上。有些亭子设锅灶，旁边堆足柴草，赶远脚的人，用竹筒带着糙米和干菜，打一勺山泉水，点上火慢慢煮着，吃饱了，几袋烟一抽，便又精神焕发。岩头村南门口的乘风亭，贴一副对联，写的是：

茶待多情客；饭留有义人。

殷勤的主人称过客为"多情"和"有义"的人，心地多么仁厚。小小的路亭，从它们的建造到它们的设施，问饥问渴、问冷问热，乡人们互相间的关怀和体贴，滋润心田。亭子默默无言，丝雨中、夕阳下，它们给楠溪江的山水带上了温暖的笑容，行人们在这笑容中认得了父老乡亲。这就是乡风民俗。

几乎所有路亭都兼作三官庙，用砖墙封上后檐，设神龛。在重要的水源，也往往有一座兼作三官庙的凉亭。溪南村南边的泉源井，渡头村东门外的大口井，西岸村东北角的瓠瓜井，芙蓉村西门外一里左右的水渠头等等，都有这样的凉亭。因为三官里有一位水官大禹，要祈求他保佑村里清泉常流。

村庄妇女们洗涤的泉头渠边，往往也有凉亭。妇女们到水边来，多带着孩子，她们劳作的时候，孩子们可以在亭子里游戏。而且楠溪江夏季多骤然而来的暴雨，亭子是妇孺们避雨的场所。芙蓉村南门外，渠水很旺，浣衣女很多，虽然不是水源，也有一座兼作三官庙的凉亭。这些凉亭，地形、树木、井泉都很优美，成为小小的风景点。

浙东各地常见的风雨桥，在楠溪江却不多。北部的黄南村有一座，跨在穿村而过的急流上，五开间，连系着溪涧两岸。这也是一种路亭，成了村子的公共中心。农事间隙，人们常到这里聚谈家常。

English

2. road pavilions (Lu Ting)

Nanxi River basin, except main stream below middle and several large tributaries navigable by small boats and bamboo rafts, all transportation relied solely on stone-paved paths winding over mountain ridges and between gullies. Carrying heavy loads, trekking these paths, extremely arduous.

To give travelers and woodcutters rest, villagers built pavilions along roads. Pavilions mostly collectively funded, organized by ancestral halls; few individually donated, for parents' merit-blessing, called "Filial Son Pavilions."

On mountain roads, pavilions about five li apart; sites mostly near clear springs. Pavilion sides usually planted trees, dense shade covering, summer provides cool rest for passersby. Nanxi River basin at least had over 1000 road pavilions; now still 274, many Ming-Qing remnants.

From Lukou Township's Shi'erpan to Qukou Township's Taishi village, pavilions along road well preserved; among them 11 built Ming dynasty; square stone columns still faintly show "Ming Wanli Jimao winter month built" or "Ming Chongzhen seven year eighth month built" inscriptions.

Roadside pavilions generally relatively simple; some no more than one bay, flush-gable purlin-on-wall. Larger ones three bays. In river-valley plains, especially near villages, pavilions mostly square, facade about 4-6m, four gold columns, twelve eave columns, hip-and-gable roof, form relatively refined, but form-type variation not much.

One north of Yantou village at Shuangjuntou uses overhanging-gable roof; one at Xinan village uses whitewashed wall sealing front eave, leaving a square door, but both sides still open. In green fields, its whitewashed wall dazzlingly bright.

Xiyuan village has an eight-column double-eave octagonal pyramidal pavilion, built Qing Shunzhi seventeen year (1660). Bilian Town also has a double-eave octagonal pyramidal pavilion, one more ring of inner columns, total sixteen, built Qing Daoguang seventeen year (1837).

On mountain road crossing Yandang range to Yueqing County, one round and one hexagonal pavilion each.

Shanghongtou village's road pavilion and one midway from Daitou village to Shuiyun village named "Su Wang Dian" (Solemn King Hall), back against mountain foot, front eave two layers, upper layer depth greater than lower, firmly seated on hillside, and has side door directly to hillside. These are relatively large variations.

That Su Wang Dian flying eaves flowing, generous and lively. From its corridor gazing at foot-of-mountain fields-villages, view wide.

Pavilions set meiren-kao or railing benches, for tired passersby rest, naturally became their road-identifying, distance-calculating markers. Stormy dusk, scorching noon, that pavilion as if smiling welcoming returning guests, can give climbing travelers much intimate comfort.

Some near-village pavilions, yearly from early-summer Dragon Boat festival to late-autumn Double Ninth, villagers supply medicinal tea and summer-heat medicine. Columns hang strings of golden straw sandals, large and small; travelers sit down, turn over soles, see straw sandals worn, reach out take a pair to change.

Some pavilions set stove-pot, beside piled enough firewood; long-distance travelers, carrying brown rice and dried vegetables in bamboo tubes, scoop mountain spring water, light fire slowly cook; eat full, smoke a few pipes, then refreshed again. Yantou village south gate's Chengfeng Pavilion (Riding-Wind Pavilion), pasted a couplet:

Tea awaits affectionate guests;

Rice leaves for righteous persons.

Attentive hosts call passersby "affectionate" and "righteous" persons, how kind-hearted. Small road pavilions, from their construction to their facilities, asking hunger-thirst, asking cold-heat, villagers' mutual care and consideration, nourish hearts.

Pavilions silently wordless; in drizzle, in sunset, they bring warm smile to Nanxi River's mountains-waters; travelers in this smile recognize fathers-elder. This is village custom-folkway.

Almost all road pavilions also serve as Sanguan temple (Three Officials temple), using brick wall sealing back eave, setting shrine. At important water sources, also often a pavilion also serving as Sanguan temple.

Xinan village south's Quanyuan well, Dutou village east gate's large mouth well, Xi'an village northeast's Hugua well, Furong village west gate about one li's water channel head etc., all have such pavilions. Because among Three Officials there is a Water Official Yu the Great, pray him bless village clear springs always flow.

village women's washing spring-head channel sides, often also have pavilions. Women come to water, mostly bring children; while they work, children can play in pavilion. And Nanxi River summer often sudden rainstorms; pavilion is women-children's rain-shelter place.

Furong village south gate outside, channel water abundant, washing women many, although not water source, also has a pavilion also serving as Sanguan temple. These pavilions, terrain, trees, wells-springs all beautiful, become small scenic spots.

wind-rain bridges common in eastern Zhejiang, but few in Nanxi River. Northern Huangnan village has one, spanning rapid stream through village, five bays, connecting stream banks. This is also a type of road pavilion, became village public center. Between farm work, people often gather here chat family matters.
