商业建筑
中文

3. 商业建筑

在历史漫长的农业社会里，自然经济在楠溪江占统治地位，商品交换很不发达，没有什么商业建筑。几副货郎担就满足了多少乡民对小商品的需求。

到了清代，商品经济渐渐发展。楠溪江边少数几个村落里产生了手工业。利用江流航运，以山货土产换取温州商品的贸易也活跃起来，于是楠溪江中游出现了一些初级的商业中心，其中比较重要的有枫林、岩头、渠口、花坛等村子。这些商业中心有几十里的服务半径，周围许多村子依靠它们的货郎担，不再发展自己的商业。

这些村子大多在水陆交通线上，路程合适，是山货土产和温州商品的集散地。它们大多也是从沿海的永嘉或者乐清到山区的仙居或者缙云去的道路必经之地。乐清产海盐，向浙中、浙西，甚至皖南、赣北等内陆地区去的盐贩，在横穿楠溪江流域的几条道路上络绎不绝。于是，位置适当的村子，除了商店之外，还有了旅店、饭店、茶馆等等。到了清代末年，尤其是民国年间，有些村子里，如枫林和岩头，形成了紧凑集中的商业街道。

在这些村子里，最早出现了商业建筑。

商业建筑的形成，最初受到自然经济条件下根深蒂固的重农轻商思想的阻滞。《苍坡李氏宗谱》里，明文规定街面上不许开店，小买卖只能在家里做。后来，有些人家，在沿重要街道的厢房的前端或者外侧，开一个洞口做买卖。再后来，把这前端或者外侧翻改成店面。店面其实很简单，无非是前脸敞开，开关全用排板，临街设一个柜台。有一些店面，柜台相当于一段槛墙，台面以上用排板。

家里售货，临街开洞口，翻造店面，这三种类型在商业很不发达的芙蓉村、塘湾村等主要街道上都有。

岩头、枫林、东皋、廊下等初级商业中心的早期商店，形制仍然很简单，而且千篇一律。它们造在村中心的主要街道上，尤其是街道的交会点上，临街而建，面阔一、二、三开间的都有。店面敞开，柜台贴近前沿，顾客在街上买东西。二、三开间的店铺，也有让顾客进入店堂的，把柜台从前沿返过来，延伸到店堂里就行了。关闭店面时，每间用六块或八块排板，也还有用柜台作槛墙，只在台面以上装排板的。稍大一点的店铺，把店号刻在木质的匾上，挂在店面枋子正中，或者刻在木牌上，竖立在曲尺形柜台的里端，正对外面。一般没有专门的幌子或者"童叟无欺"之类的牌子。

这种早期商铺仍然是店宅合一。大多是后半间里住人，前后或者用货柜隔开，或者用板壁。许多店铺是两层的，但楼层低矮，裸露着椽子和屋瓦，夏季闷热，冬季寒冷，通常并不住人，只用作储藏。有些手工业品小店，如卖秤的、卖圆木家具的、卖篾编的，等等，店面的一部分甚至大部分是作坊。因为买卖大都是预订，并不需要陈列或者堆放成品。产品都是传统式样，很少创新，连样品都不必要。

旅店、饭店、茶馆都并没有、也不必要发展出自己特殊的形制。廊下村前街的西端，在几间店面的楼上有一家饭店，岩头村东门、献义门里，有一座茶馆，都不过是比较宽敞的空间而已。茶馆的形式很像路亭，但尺寸大一些。

岩头村的中心，旧进士街（今名金苍路）南端与横街相交的丁字路口上，有三座"苏式店面"，两座占据转角，一座在横街南侧，斜对着西北角上的一座。它们是整个楠溪江中游农村里最华丽的店铺。两层楼，楼上的槛墙外满满镶贴着复杂精巧的木栏杆式的装饰。上层略略挑出于下层，槛墙下作垂莲柱，雕饰很细致。把角的两座，充分利用它们的位置，向进士街和横街两面敞开。这三座店铺的风格显然是外来的，与楠溪江建筑的朴实不相协调，但这种对乡土建筑单纯的统一性的破坏，恰恰是商品经济的商业文化的特点。

进士街是岩头村最重要的一条街道。它的北端是由仁道门、金氏大宗祠、进士牌楼和贞节牌坊形成的全村最重要的礼制中心。它的南端是全村的"主星"所在。"主星"就是地面上用细卵石镶成的龙凤图案，关系到风水。这三座"苏式店面"居然挟"主星"而与大宗祠相抗衡，反映着商品经济体制与封建宗法制的抗衡。从宗谱里规定不许在街道上造店面，发展到店面作为全村最华丽的建筑与大宗祠抗衡，表明商品经济一旦产生，就必然会突破宗法制度，并且逐渐战胜它。

商品经济的进一步发展是产生了商业街。岩头村丽水街是统一规划、短期内建成的一条商业街。丽水街造在岩头村东缘的蓄水堤上。这道蓄水堤造于明代嘉靖三十五年（丙辰，1556年），长约300米，称为荷垟或者长垟，是桂林公主持的大规模水利和村落建设的一部分。它西侧形成了一个大水库，称为丽水湖或者长湖，与南面的进宦湖、镇南湖等一起，是岩头村农田抗旱的保障。同时它们也是岩头村公园的一部分。岩头村"十景"之一就有"长堤春晓"。

民国年间编写的《岩头金氏宗谱》里说："吾族昔为风水所迷，河堤之上只许种树莳花与建亭点缀风景而已，不与筑屋经商。凡为商贾者，咸于村内街头巷口之住屋为之。"风水关系到宗族的命运，河堤上不许筑屋经商，应该是一个很重要的规定。但是，由于从乐清赴缙云的道路在河堤上经过，"挑盐过缙云，一天一钱银"，盐贩比较富有，同时，岩头盛产稻米，收获季节大批外地农民涌来帮工。

因此，在清代末年，河堤上已经有了零散的商铺，《岩头金氏宗谱》里说："民元以来，商业日渐发达，四处商贩云集，市场扩大，河垟一带自南而北，路之东西，悉已筑为商店"，经济利益冲击之下，"昔日之风水迷梦，今则破除之矣。"宗族的命运，毕竟决定于"一天一钱银"，而不是阴阳八卦。

大约在"悉已筑为商店"之后，丽水街才重新规划，一次重建，成为绵延300米，店面大约九十间的商业街。每间面阔3米上下。

河堤的宽度在13.0米左右。商店建在它的东侧，进深大约10.0米，两层。店前有3.0米宽的小街。街的另一侧就是长湖。整条街都用披檐遮盖，为了尽可能加宽小街，西侧的廊柱都立在一块块向外挑出大约30厘米的天然长条石块的端头上。柱子间设美人靠，美人靠悬空临水。所有的店铺都向小街敞开，顾客在街上选购商品，小街成了商店的扩展。

街面用块石铺。隔不远就有一道台阶下到水面，阶石都是天然长条石，一端砌进堤岸，一端挑出大约60厘米，每块之间分开，块块脱空，非常玲珑轻巧。

店铺都隔成前、后间，前间是店面，后间是卧室、厨房等等。楼上低矮，大多用于储藏。有些店家，向后延伸出一个后院，则院子就在堤外，比店面低许多。

丽水街南段微微向西弯曲成弧形，造成柔和的变化，消除了长街的单调，并且加强了它和湖水的亲切交往。它的南端，是寨墙的南门、门边高台阶上的乘风亭、亭前通向村里的明代嘉靖戊午年（三十七年，1558年）造的三跨石梁桥和桥头一棵数百年的大苦槠树。浓荫蔽天的老树下，几块天然石板随意地搭在水里，身着五彩衣衫的姑娘们在石板上搓洗衣服，雪白的鸭子悠悠地向水底扑啄她们的影子。桥的南侧，就是以琴屿为中心的公园，芙蓉花映着芙蓉峰，溢过桥来。

丽水街的北端是献义门，长湖在这里宽不过10米，西岸就是一座茶馆，方方的，轻巧的美人靠映在水中。闲闲地啜茶吸烟漫话古今的老人们的身影，也在水光中倏忽荡漾。丽水街上虽然熙熙攘攘，但美人靠外，一片水色，秋芦夏荷，看不尽的风光。对岸远处，竹树掩映之下，粉墙黛瓦，参差几户人家。晚风拂来，淡淡的炊烟的苦味，教人心地平和，给奔逐于利途的人一丝温馨。一条商业街而有这样的情境，足见楠溪江文化中人文精神的强有力。

枫林镇的圣旨门街是楠溪江中游最繁华的商业街，风格与岩头村的丽水街完全不同。它以圣旨门为中心，向东西两侧延伸，全长400多米，是横贯全镇的轴线。从清代光绪年间起，圣旨门两边陆陆续续造起了店铺，因为枫林镇是一个山货土产的集中地。店铺造在街道两侧，街道是封闭的，但有小型广场、转弯、交叉路口等造成空间的变化。圣旨门街没有经过统一规划，商店先后建成，形式和形制的变化比较大，但大体仍旧有显著的共同点。店面是开敞式的，一间三间不等。一般为两层，上层往往用牛腿挑出，牛腿前有垂莲柱。

牛腿和垂莲柱等处是雕饰的重点，不过比较简洁，并不华丽。由于街面热闹，店面经济效益高，所以居住部分往往与店铺分离，而造在第二进。一方面是为了增加营业面积，一是便于出租或者出让店面。

圣旨门街平均宽度约为8~10米，这条街充溢着强烈的商业气息，连曾经象征封建宗法制下家族最高荣誉的圣旨门也被商业侵占，只剩下明间的通道了。楠溪江的文化的人文精神，毕竟抵挡不住商品经济的冲击。

English

3. Commercial Architecture

In long historical agricultural society, natural economy dominated Nanxi River; commodity exchange very underdeveloped, no commercial architecture. A few peddler loads satisfied many villagers' demand for small goods.

To Qing dynasty, commodity economy gradually developed. handicraft industry arose in a few Nanxi River villages. Trade using river shipping, exchanging mountain local products for Wenzhou goods also became active; thus some primary commercial centers appeared in Nanxi River middle, among them more important Fenglin, Yantou, Qukou, Huatan villages.

These commercial centers had tens-of-li service radius; surrounding many villages relied on their peddlers, no longer developed own commerce.

These villages mostly on land-water transport lines, suitable distance, were distribution centers for mountain local products and Wenzhou goods. They mostly also必经 places on roads from coastal Yongjia or Yueqing to mountainous Xianju or Jinyun.

Yueqing produced sea salt; salt peddlers going to central Zhejiang, western Zhejiang, even southern Anhui, northern Jiangxi inland areas, continuously on several roads crossing Nanxi River basin. Thus, appropriately located villages, besides shops, also had inns, restaurants, teahouses etc.

To late Qing, especially Republican era, some villages, e.g. Fenglin and Yantou, formed compact concentrated commercial streets.

In these villages, earliest commercial architecture appeared.

Commercial architecture formation, initially blocked by deep-rooted agriculture-valuing-commerce-despising thought under natural economy. "Cangpo Li clan Genealogy" explicitly stipulated street surface not allowed shops; small business only at home.

Later, some families, at front or outside of wing rooms along important streets, opened a hole for business. Later still, converted this front or outside into shop front. shop front actually very simple: nothing but front face open, open-close all using plank boards (pai ban), street-side set a counter.

Some shop fronts, counter equivalent to a section of sill wall, above counter surface using plank boards.

Home selling, street-side opening hole, converted shop front — these three types all exist on main streets of commercially underdeveloped Furong village, Tangwan village etc.

Early shops in primary commercial centers Yantou, Fenglin, Donggao, Langxia etc., form still very simple, and monotonous. They built on village center main streets, especially street intersections, street-facing built; facade one, two, three bays all exist. shop front open, counter close to front edge; customers buy on street.

Two-three bay shops, also allow customers enter shop hall; just turn counter from front edge back, extend into shop hall. Closing shop front, each bay uses six or eight plank boards; also some use counter as sill wall, only above counter surface install plank boards.

Slightly larger shops, shop name carved on wooden plaque, hung at shop front beam center, or carved on wooden board, erected at inner end of L-shaped counter, directly facing outside. Generally no specialized shop sign or "honest to all" type plaques.

This early shop still shop-residence combined. Mostly rear half bay lived in; front-rear either separated by goods counter, or by plank wall. Many shops two-story, but upper floor low, exposed rafters and roof tiles, summer stuffy, winter cold, usually not lived in, only storage. Some handicraft small shops, e.g.

selling scales, selling round-wood furniture, selling bamboo weaving etc., part甚至 most of shop front is workshop. Because sales mostly pre-order, no need display or stack finished products. Products all traditional styles, little innovation, even samples unnecessary.

Inns, restaurants, teahouses neither had nor needed develop own special form. Langxia village front street west end, above several shop fronts has a restaurant; Yantou village east gate, Xianyi Gate inside, has a teahouse; all nothing but relatively spacious space. Teahouse form much like road pavilion, but size larger.

Yantou village center, old jinshi Street (now named Jincang Road) south end intersecting horizontal street T-junction, has three "Suzhou-style shop fronts"; two occupy corners, one south of horizontal street, diagonally facing one at northwest corner. They are most gorgeous shops in entire Nanxi River middle rural area.

Two-story; upper floor sill wall outside fully inlaid with complex-exquisite wooden railing-style decoration. Upper layer slightly projects over lower; below sill wall make hanging-lotus columns (chui lian zhu), carving very detailed. Corner two, fully利用 their position, open to both jinshi Street and horizontal street.

These three shops' style obviously外来, not coordinating with Nanxi River architecture's simplicity; but this destruction of vernacular architecture's simple unity, precisely is commodity economy commercial culture's characteristic.

jinshi Street is Yantou village's most important street. Its north end is village's most important ritual center formed by Rendao Gate, Jin clan Main ancestral hall, jinshi archway, and Chastity archway. Its south end is village's "Main Star" (Zhu Xing) location.

"Main Star" is dragon-phoenix pattern inlaid on ground with fine pebbles, related to fengshui. These three "Suzhou-style shop fronts" actually leverage "Main Star" to contend with main ancestral hall, reflecting commodity economy system contending with feudal patriarchal system.

From genealogy stipulating not allowed build shop fronts on streets, developing to shop fronts as village's most gorgeous buildings contending with main ancestral hall,表明 commodity economy once产生, inevitably will break through patriarchal system, and gradually defeat it.

Commodity economy further development produced commercial streets. Yantou village lishui street is a unified-planned, short-term-built commercial street. lishui street built on Yantou village east edge water-storage dike.

This water-storage dike built Ming Jiajing thirty-five year (Bingchen, 1556), about 300m long, called Heyang or Changyang, part of large-scale water conservancy and village construction presided by Guilin Gong.

Its west side formed a large reservoir, called Lishui Lake or Chang Lake, together with south Jinhuan Lake, Zhennan Lake etc., is Yantou village farmland drought-resistance guarantee. Meanwhile they also part of Yantou village park.

Yantou village "Ten Scenes" one includes "Long Embankment Spring Dawn." Republican-era "Yantou Jin clan Genealogy" says: "Our clan formerly confused by fengshui; river dike only allowed planting trees-flowers and building pavilions to embellish scenery, not building houses for commerce.

All merchants, did business in village street-alley residences." fengshui related to clan fate; dike not allowed build houses-commerce, should be very important stipulation.

However, because road from Yueqing to Jinyun passes over dike, "carrying salt over Jinyun, one qian silver a day," salt peddlers relatively wealthy; meanwhile Yantou abundant rice, harvest season large numbers of outside farmers涌来 help labor.

Therefore, late Qing, dike already had scattered shops; "Yantou Jin clan Genealogy" says: "Since Republic, commerce increasingly developed, merchants from everywhere gathered, market expanded, Heyang area from south to north, both sides of road, all built as shops"; under economic interest impact, "former fengshui dream, now broken." clan fate, after all determined by "one qian silver a day," not yin-yang eight trigrams.

Approximately after "all built as shops," lishui street才 replanned, rebuilt at once, became 300m-long commercial street with about ninety shop fronts. Each bay facade about 3m.

Dike width about 13.0m. shops built on its east side, depth about 10.0m, two-story. Before shops 3.0m wide small street. Street other side is Chang Lake. Entire street covered with shed eave (pi yan); to widen small street as much as possible, west side corridor columns all stand on ends of natural long stone blocks projecting outward about 30cm.

Between columns set meiren-kao; meiren-kao suspended over water. All shops open to small street; customers select goods on street; small street becomes shop extension.

Street surface paved with block stones. Not far apart a step down to water surface; step stones all natural long stones, one end built into dike bank, one end projects about 60cm, each separated, all suspended, very exquisite-light.

shops all divided front-rear rooms; front room shop front, rear room bedroom, kitchen etc. Upper floor low, mostly storage. Some shopkeepers, extend backward a rear courtyard; courtyard then outside dike, much lower than shop front.

lishui street south section slightly curves westward into arc, creating soft variation, eliminating long street monotony, and strengthening its intimate interaction with lake water.

Its south end: stockade wall south gate, gate-side high-step Chengfeng Pavilion, before pavilion three-span stone beam bridge built Ming Jiajing Wuwu year (thirty-seven, 1558) leading to village, and bridgehead several-hundred-year large evergreen chinkapin tree.

Under dense-shade old tree, several natural stone slabs casually placed in water; colorful-dressed girls scrub clothes on stone slabs; white ducks leisurely peck their shadows underwater. bridge south side is park centered on Qin Islet; hibiscus flowers reflecting Hibiscus Peak, overflowing across bridge.

lishui street north end is Xianyi Gate; Chang Lake here no more than 10m wide; west bank is a teahouse, square, light meiren-kao reflected in water. Old people leisurely sipping tea smoking chatting past-present figures, also flit in water light.

lishui street although bustling, but outside meiren-kao,一片 water color, autumn reeds summer lotuses, endless scenery. Opposite bank distance, bamboo-trees shaded, white-walls black-tiles, several households uneven. Evening breeze blows, faint cooking smoke bitterness, teaches heart peaceful, gives profit-chasing people a trace of warmth.

A commercial street with such情境, sufficient shows Nanxi River culture humanistic spirit's strength.

Fenglin Town's Shengzhi Gate Street is Nanxi River middle most prosperous commercial street, style completely different from Yantou village's lishui street. It centers on Shengzhi Gate, extends east-west, total length over 400m, axis crossing entire town.

From Qing Guangxu era, shops gradually built both sides of Shengzhi Gate, because Fenglin Town is a mountain local products concentration place. shops built both sides of street; street enclosed, but small plazas, turns, intersections create spatial variation.

Shengzhi Gate Street not unified planned; shops built successively, form-type variation relatively large, but generally still have significant common points. shop front open-style, one to three bays不等. Generally two-story; upper layer often uses corbel bracket (niu tui) to project; before corbel bracket hanging-lotus column.

corbel bracket and hanging-lotus column etc. are carving focus, but relatively simple, not gorgeous. Due to street lively, shop front economic benefit high, so residential part often separated from shop, built in second进. One reason to increase business area, one convenient for renting or selling shop front.

Shengzhi Gate Street average width about 8-10m; this street充溢 strong commercial atmosphere; even Shengzhi Gate, once symbolizing family highest honor under feudal patriarchal system, also encroached by commerce, only central bay passage left. Nanxi River culture's humanistic spirit, after all cannot withstand commodity economy impact.

第四篇：匠作篇

Part IV: Craftsmanship
1
构架及构件
中文

第四篇 匠作篇

屋顶绝大多数是悬山式的，少数用歇山式。用硬山式或者封火山墙的极少，用了的年代都比较晚近。屋面用青瓦，正中起脊。

台基做法，大致是沿边用条石、块石甚至卵石砌台帮和阶条，里面填夯土。除了蛮石墙外，台基和地面是主要的石作工程所在。

所有木构件，除戏台之外，不论大小木作都不施油漆。

1. 构架及构件

（1）构架分两大类，抬梁式的和穿斗式的。前者多用于宗祠、庙宇等重要公共建筑物的露明部分，后者多用于宗祠、庙宇的不露明部分和住宅。宗祠和庙宇，大多中央三间，即四榀屋架是露明的，因为梢间和尽间常隔成房间甚至做夹层。少数为五间六榀或七间八榀抬梁式露明，这时山墙位置的屋架即边榀，虽是穿斗式的，往往也露明。住宅一般是两层的，楼上主要用于储藏，所以屋架一概不露明，多用穿斗式。

抬梁式屋架的用材和做工都很讲究，构件形式和尺寸比较规范化，而穿斗式的则大多比较随意，是草架，用天然形木料，只有祠堂、庙宇的山墙上露明的边榀很讲究，装饰性处理往往甚至强于中央抬梁式屋架。穿斗式与抬梁式的混合式梁架在祠堂、庙宇的山墙上也很常用。

宗祠和庙宇的正殿，大多是七架梁前后廊，但中央主梁为五架梁，因为按《鲁班经》"三架堂、七架厅，凶"。支承中央五架梁的柱子叫明柱。前檐廊上做卷棚轩，稍简单的做井口轩，后檐廊上都是井口轩。山区山村，所以当中四步，檩子上用大约5厘米×10厘米的枋子密排，既是椽子，又是望板。大宗祠和大庙宇，正殿进深大，明柱间仍为五架梁，而在檐柱和明柱之间再加一棵柱子，称前、后大步柱。大步柱与明柱和檐柱间都为两步。后檐廊置神橱。神橱加宽，在后檐柱外再加一排小柱子，与檐柱相距一步。

两棵明柱之间虽然是五架梁，不过，除明间左右的两榀之外，其馀各榀屋架都是穿斗与抬梁混合式屋架，都有脊柱（当地称正栋柱）落地，所以每缝有七棵柱子，以致殿堂里柱子很多。

宗祠、庙宇、戏台、溪门、牌楼、亭阁等有斗栱，繁简不等。

（2）为了加大正殿的进深，有些宗祠或庙宇采用"双分房"，就是每一缝上用两榀屋架前后相接（即苏州所称鸳鸯厅），中间一棵柱子在前后都支承一个五架梁，在内部看有两个起脊，檩子之上却密排枋子封住。而在它们之间的山谷形空间里，再用草架支起檩子和正脊，形成包容前后两榀屋架的大屋顶。这种做法的优点，一是避免内部空间过于高旷，二是可以少用做脊柱的上好大木料。采用这种"双分房"的有廊下村的朱氏小宗祠、珠岸村的陈氏大宗祠、花坛村的敦睦祠和岩头村的塔湖庙，等等。

扩大内部自由空间的另一种方法是采用"偷柱造"，即不置明间的两棵前明柱，而在这地方架一根横跨三间的大梁，以支承中间两榀屋架上的五架梁。采用这种做法的有岩头村的水亭祠正殿。不过，横跨三间的大梁太长，容易劈裂（按：水亭祠的就已断裂），所以，经常的做法是，把明间开间放大到6米即一间半左右，在这开间里再添两棵后明柱和后檐柱，而前檐不添，用长约6米的梁承托添加的两根主梁。还有个别很复杂的"偷柱造"做法，如苍坡的水门底宗祠，不过基本原则不外乎以梁代柱，支承上面的构架。

甚至住宅也有"偷柱造"的，虽然屋架是穿斗式的。例如花坛村马湾相传建于宋代的老屋，在明间不但照例没有正栋柱，而且减去了两棵前明柱和两棵前檐柱，因此中央的檐檩和代替两棵明柱的梁都长达8.3米。显然，这座老宅的明间有很重要的礼仪性用途，它的梁架构件的尺寸都很大。

English

Part Four: Craftsmanship Chapter

roofs绝大多数 overhanging-gable style,少数 hip-and-gable. Flush-gable or gable-end fire walls extremely few; those used are relatively late. roof uses grey tiles, center ridge.

platform base practice, generally edge uses strip stones, block stones even cobblestones to build platform side and step strips, inside filled rammed earth. Besides rough-stone walls, platform base and floor are main stonework locations.

All timber members, except stage, regardless large-small timber work, no paint applied.

1. frame and members

(1) frames divide two categories: post-and-lintel (tailiang) and column-and-tie (chuandou). former mostly used in exposed parts of important public buildings such as ancestral halls, temples; latter mostly used in unexposed parts of ancestral halls, temples and residences. ancestral halls and temples, mostly central three bays, i.e.

four trusses exposed, because shao-jian and jin-jian often partitioned into rooms even mezzanines.少数 five-bay six-truss or seven-bay eight-truss post-and-lintel exposed; then gable-wall position truss即 side truss, although column-and-tie, often also exposed.

residences generally two-story; upper floor mainly storage, so trusses all unexposed, mostly column-and-tie.

Post-and-lintel truss timber and workmanship very refined; member form and size relatively standardized; column-and-tie mostly casual, rough frame (cao jia), using natural-shape timber; only ancestral hall-temple gable-wall exposed side trusses very refined, decorative treatment often even stronger than central post-and-lintel truss.

Column-and-tie and post-and-lintel hybrid beam frames also commonly used on ancestral hall-temple gable walls.

ancestral hall and temple main hall, mostly seven-purlin beam front-rear corridors, but central main beam five-purlin beam, because according to "Lu Ban Jing" "three-purlin hall, seven-purlin hall, inauspicious." Column supporting central five-purlin beam called ming zhu (bright column).

Front eave corridor makes rolling-roof arcade (juan peng xuan); simpler makes well-head arcade (jing kou xuan); rear eave corridors all well-head arcade. Mountain villages, so central four steps, purlins use about 5cm×10cm beams densely arranged, both rafters and roof boards.

Large ancestral halls and temples, main hall depth large; ming zhu间 still five-purlin beam; between eave column and ming zhu add one column, called front-rear da bu zhu (large-step column). Da bu zhu to ming zhu and eave column both two steps. Rear eave corridor sets shrine cabinet.

shrine cabinet widened, outside rear eave column add one row small columns, one step from eave column.

Between two ming zhu although five-purlin beam, however, except central bay left-right two trusses, remaining trusses all column-and-tie/post-and-lintel hybrid, all have spinal column (local zheng dong zhu) to ground; so each truss has seven columns, causing hall many columns.

ancestral halls, temples, stages, stream gates, archways, pavilions etc. have dougong, complex-simple varying.

(2) To increase main hall depth, some ancestral halls or temples adopt "shuang fen fang" (double-divided hall), i.e.

each truss uses two trusses front-rear joined (Suzhou called yuan yang ting - mandarin duck hall); central one column front-rear both supports a five-purlin beam; internally looks like two ridges; above purlins densely arranged beams seal.

In valley-shaped space between them, again use rough frame to support purlins and main ridge, forming large roof enclosing front-rear two trusses. This practice advantages: one, avoid internal space too high-vast; two, can use less good large timber for spinal column.

Adopting this "double-divided hall": Langxia village Zhu Minor ancestral hall, Zhu'an village Chen Main ancestral hall, Huatan village Dunmu ancestral hall, Yantou village Tahu temple, etc.

Another method to expand internal free space is "tou zhu zao" (column-stealing construction), i.e. not place central bay two front ming zhu, but in this place frame a beam spanning three bays, to support five-purlin beams on middle two trusses. Adopting this: Yantou village Shuiting temple main hall.

However, beam spanning three bays too long, easily splits (note: Shuiting temple's already broken); therefore, common practice, enlarge central bay to 6m i.e. about one-and-half bays, in this bay add two rear ming zhu and rear eave columns, front eave not add, use about 6m long beam to support added two main beams.

There are个别 very complex "column-stealing" practices, e.g. Cangpo Shuimendi ancestral hall, but basic principle nothing more than beam replacing column, supporting above frame.

Even residences have "column-stealing," although truss column-and-tie. E.g. Huatan village Mawan old house legend built Song dynasty; in central bay not only照例 no zheng dong zhu, but also subtracted two front ming zhu and two front eave columns; therefore central eave purlin and beam replacing two ming zhu both 8.3m long.

Obviously, this old house's central bay has very important ceremonial use; its frame members sizes very large.

构件
中文

2. 构件

屋架的主要构件是柱、梁、枋、檩。

（1）楠溪江的大木老司有自己的名称来称呼不同位置上的柱子。前、后檐柱称前、后小步柱，脊柱称正栋柱。在它们之间有前、后大步柱和前、后明柱。

柱子以圆形的居多，方形的也有不少。上下柱径不变，多数有天然木材的弯曲。有一些到柱顶边缘加工成四分之一圆弧。虽然楠溪江打石老司的技巧很高，石料也很丰富，但石柱很少，仅见于牌楼和塘湾郑氏大宗祠，而这座宗祠改建于20世纪30年代。

普通住宅，檐柱高约3米有余，柱径在22厘米左右。宗祠和庙宇，檐柱高可3.5米上下，柱径大约30厘米。乡土建筑，规范化程度比较低，因材施工，这些尺寸的变化幅度很大。

（2）除草架外，梁和枋子（按：当地人不区分梁和枋，概称为梁）大都为月梁（当地称欠梁）。最完全的是，除底面之外，两侧和上面，都呈极柔和、轻快、流畅的双曲面形，底面为单曲面。非常生动，丰满而富有弹性。它上面的曲线，中央曲率大而平缓，渐近两端，曲率渐小，曲线越紧，与宋代官式月梁相同。到头上，又在侧面作一个阴刻的曲线，反卷过去，十分有力，并且使月梁的形式独立而完美。稍简单一些的，底面平直。最简单的，两侧也平直，只有上面作双曲面。

大多数月梁，不论大小，都是由三块材料拼合的。一块在下面，两块在它之上相对，由银锭榫结合。采用这种做法，大约是为了便于加工，因为月梁的形式是很精致的。月梁的形式，既很简洁又很复杂，柔和轻捷，装饰效果很强，尤其是正面的枋子。苍坡村的溪门、花坛村的宪台牌楼，月梁都很灵秀俊逸。

月梁两端的下面有细巧的"鸡舌"作装饰，它们是纯装饰的，不起结构作用。

屋架枋子的高度大致与柱子直径相等。它断面的高宽比约略是2：1。

露明的穿斗架，或穿斗、抬梁混合式屋架有一种斜向构件叫做"大头梁"或"泥鳅梁"。它们本身不对称，一头大一头小，一头圆一头方，轮廓复杂如鱼，圆转而有弹性，雕塑性很强。结构作用不大，但是很有装饰效果。

大木构架的构件和它们的组合形式，最有装饰效果的是前檐廊的卷棚轩。穿插枋，上面的两个坐斗，抱头梁，两棵小蜀柱（或者坐斗交麻叶云），角背，以及蜀柱外侧形式活泼而又华丽的斜向大头梁和泥鳅梁，再加上鹅项形（鹤颈形）的椽子或轩板，它们组成很完整的、紧凑而饱满的、匀称而富于变化的构图。有时，檐柱外侧还有雕刻复杂的牛腿或斜撑支承抱头梁头上的挑檐檩（当地叫方芩桁），又从支承点垂下莲花柱或花篮柱。它们和卷棚轩在一起，构图更加丰富。

椽子是圆形的。两层的住宅，上檐也有用方形椽子的。椽径大，椽档小，很密，上面直接铺仰瓦。祠堂和庙宇，前后明柱间的四步如为露明做法时，用密排的方木代替椽子。

（3）台基上，凡立柱子的地方必有一块柱顶石，方的。柱子脚下，又有一个构件，即柱础。楠溪江建筑的一个重要特色，就是还保存着许多木质的柱础。苍坡、周宅、港头、渡头、霞美、芙蓉、鹤垟、蓬溪、花坛、廊下等村子，都有不少住宅至今还有木质柱础。花坛的朱氏大宗祠和敦睦祠等祠堂也用木柱础。这些木柱础，从老化情况看，有的已经很古旧。不过，据霞美村木匠说，20世纪上半叶还有做木础的。

木础一般横放，以防毛细作用使它易于受潮腐烂，而且也更耐压。所以多取方形而不取圆形，在横放情况下，把木础做成圆形是既麻烦又不结实。木础通常不加雕饰，但如花坛朱氏大宗祠和敦睦祠的有些木础，上面有粗简的半圆线脚，下部略呈凹弧面，叫"大头方础"。住宅的木础，大多高25厘米，长宽各为30~35厘米。宗祠里的，尺寸随柱径而有许多差别。

目前情况，石的柱础多于木的。石柱础式样比较多，而且明显的有等级性。大致是：

第一级：鼓形，刻云龙纹。苍坡村现存六个，是从乾符灵山院取来的。寺院建于唐玄宗先天年间（712-713年），如今已平毁无余。它们高约42.5厘米，直径约52.0厘米。浮雕游龙非常天矫有力，神采飞扬，却又很简洁，是很有价值的艺术品。

第二级：虎爪磉。有圆有方，四周深浮雕作密排的虎爪。大量用于宗祠，但只有出过相当高级的官宦的房派宗祠或大宗祠才可以用。虎爪磉是现存柱础中级别最高的。

第三级：盘磉和篮磉。圆形，后者如花篮，有雕饰，主体常做瓜瓣式，下部有短短一段圆柱体。前者主体作一个圆柱体，也有雕饰。

第四级：有浅浮雕的大头方础。

第五级：碗磉、盆磉、园林磉。外形比较简单，不做雕饰。

第六级：无雕饰的大头方础。

第七级：方磉。有完全的立方体，也有在四棱斜削出一个小小的三角形面，使上表面呈切角正方形。

不同等级的柱础在建筑物里的位置大致是：住宅里，前檐柱（前小步柱）有用盘磉或篮磉的，用碗磉、大头方础等的也有，其他的位置一律用方磉。宗祠里，以正厅的明间里四棵明柱的柱础等级最高，尺寸也大。其次为全部前檐柱，再次为次间的明柱，再次为大步柱，最后是后檐柱。所以在一个正厅里，可以见到多种柱础。虽然它们的位置有一定规矩，但看起来还是显得杂乱。

English

2. members

Truss main members are columns, beams, fang (horizontal beams), purlins.

(1) Nanxi River large-timber carpenters have own names for columns at different positions. Front-rear eave columns called front-rear xiao bu zhu (small-step column); spinal column called zheng dong zhu. Between them have front-rear da bu zhu and front-rear ming zhu.

Columns mostly round; square also不少. Top-bottom column diameter unchanged; most have natural timber curvature. Some at column top edge processed into quarter arc. Although Nanxi River stonecutters skill high, stone material rich, stone columns few, only seen in archways and Tangwan Zheng Main ancestral hall, and this ancestral hall rebuilt 1930s.

Ordinary residences, eave column height about 3m+, column diameter about 22cm. ancestral halls and temples, eave column height can 3.5m上下, column diameter about 30cm. vernacular architecture, standardization degree relatively low, timber-based construction, these sizes variation range large.

(2) Except rough frames, beams and fang (note: locals do not distinguish beam and fang, collectively called beam) mostly moon beams (local qian liang). Most complete: except bottom surface, both sides and top, all extremely soft, light, fluent hyperboloid shape; bottom surface single curved. Very vivid, full and elastic.

Its top curve, central curvature large and gentle, approaching ends curvature gradually smaller, curve tighter, same as Song official-style moon beam. At head, again on side make an incised curve, reverse卷 over, very powerful, and makes moon beam form independent and perfect. Slightly simpler: bottom flat.

Simplest: both sides also flat, only top hyperboloid.

Most moon beams, regardless size, are assembled from three pieces. One below, two above opposite, joined by ingot tenon (yin ding sun). Adopting this practice, approximately for easier processing, because moon beam form very refined. Moon beam form, both very simple and very complex, soft-light, decorative effect strong, especially front fang.

Cangpo village stream gate, Huatan village Xiantai archway, moon beams both very elegant-graceful.

Moon beam both ends below have delicate "ji she" (chicken tongue) decoration; they are pure decorative, no structural function.

Truss fang height roughly equal to column diameter. Its section height-width ratio approximately 2:1.

Exposed column-and-tie frames, or column-and-tie/post-and-lintel hybrid trusses have a diagonal member called "da tou liang" (big-head beam) or "ni qiu liang" (loach beam). They themselves asymmetric, one end big one small, one end round one square, outline complex like fish, round and elastic, very sculptural. Structural function not large, but very decorative.

Large-timber frame members and their combination forms, most decorative is front eave corridor rolling-roof arcade.

Chuancha fang (interlocking horizontal beam), above two zuo dou (seated blocks), bao tou liang (head-holding beam), two small shu zhu (short columns) (or zuo dou jiao ma ye yun - seated block with hemp-leaf cloud), jiao bei (angle back), and outside shu zhu lively-gorgeous diagonal big-head beam and loach beam, plus goose-neck (crane-neck) rafters or arcade boards, they form very complete, compact-full, balanced-varied composition.

Sometimes, outside eave column also has complex-carved niu tui (corbel) or diagonal brace supporting挑檐 purlin (local fang qin heng) on bao tou liang head, and from support point hangs lotus column or flower-basket column. They together with rolling-roof arcade, composition richer.

Rafters round. Two-story residences, upper eave also uses square rafters. Rafter diameter large, rafter gap small, very dense, above directly lays concave tiles. ancestral halls and temples, front-rear ming zhu间 four steps if exposed practice, use densely arranged square timber instead of rafters.

(3) On platform base, wherever column erected必有 a column-top stone, square. Column foot below, again a member,即 column base (zhu chu). An important feature of Nanxi River architecture, is still preserving many wooden column bases. Cangpo, Zhouzhai, Gangtou, Dutou, Xiamei, Furong, Heyang, Pengxi, Huatan, Langxia etc.

villages, all have many residences至今 still wooden column bases. Huatan Zhu Main ancestral hall and Dunmu ancestral hall etc. also use wooden column bases. These wooden column bases, from aging condition, some already very old. However, according to Xiamei village carpenter, first half 20th century still made wooden bases.

Wooden bases generally placed horizontally, to prevent capillary action making them easily damp-rotted, and also more pressure-resistant. So mostly square not round; under horizontal placement, making wooden base round is both troublesome and not strong. Wooden bases usually no carving, but e.g.

Huatan Zhu Main ancestral hall and Dunmu ancestral hall some wooden bases, above have rough semicircular moldings, lower part slightly concave arc, called "da tou fang chu" (big-head square base). residence wooden bases, mostly 25cm high, length-width each 30-35cm. ancestral hall ones, sizes vary with column diameter.

Current situation, stone column bases more than wooden. Stone column base styles relatively many, and obviously hierarchical. Roughly:

First level: drum shape, carved cloud-dragon pattern. Cangpo village现存 six, taken from Qianfu Lingshan temple. temple built Tang Xuanzong Xiantian era (712-713), now completely destroyed. They about 42.5cm high, diameter about 52.0cm. Relief coiling dragon very powerful, spirited, yet very simple, valuable artworks.

Second level: hu zhua sang (tiger-claw base). Round and square,四周 deep relief as dense tiger claws.大量 used in ancestral halls, but only lineage ancestral halls or main ancestral halls that produced fairly high officials can use. Tiger-claw base is highest level among existing column bases.

Third level: pan sang (plate base) and lan sang (basket base). Round; latter like flower basket, has carving, main body often melon-petal style, lower part has short cylinder section. former main body a cylinder, also has carving.

Fourth level: big-head square base with shallow relief.

Fifth level: wan sang (bowl base), pen sang (basin base), yuan lin sang (garden base). Outer shape relatively simple, no carving.

Sixth level: big-head square base without carving.

Seventh level: fang sang (square base). Complete cube, also at four edges bevel a small triangular face, making top surface chamfered square.

Different level column bases in building positions roughly: residences, front eave column (front xiao bu zhu) some use pan sang or lan sang, also use wan sang, big-head square base etc.; other positions all use fang sang. ancestral halls, main hall central bay four ming zhu column bases highest level, size also large.

Next all front eave columns, next side-bay ming zhu, next da bu zhu, finally rear eave columns. So in one main hall, can see multiple column bases. Although their positions have certain rules, but looks still messy.

本篇术语 Key Terms

小步柱 / Xiao bu zhu (small-step column) 月梁 / Moon beam (Yue liang) 欠梁 / Qian liang (local term for moon beam) 银锭榫 / Ingot tenon (Yin ding sun) 鸡舌 / Ji she (chicken tongue decoration) 大头梁 / Da tou liang (big-head beam) 泥鳅梁 / Ni qiu liang (loach beam) 抱头梁 / Bao tou liang (head-holding beam) 蜀柱 / Shu zhu (short column) 牛腿 / Niu tui (corbel bracket) 挑檐檩 / Tiao yan lin (eave-purlin) 莲花柱 / Lotus column 花篮柱 / Flower-basket column 柱顶石 / Column-top stone 柱础 / Column base (Zhu chu) ...等23个
3
斗拱
中文

3. 斗栱

楠溪江流域，宗祠和庙宇大多使用斗栱，尤其在戏台和大门上。溪门和重要的亭阁中多用斗栱，旌表性建筑物如木牌楼，斗栱非常复杂华丽。住宅中用斗栱的也不少，但很简单。

斗栱也有柱头科、角科、平身科三类。平身科只用于戏台和牌楼，以及少数牌楼式的大门，做法与柱头科一样。不同于清代官式，平身科并不密排，各攒之间距离比较大而随意，看上去疏朗。宗祠和庙宇的殿堂只有柱头科而没有平身科。角科只用于歇山顶的建筑物，如各村的戏台和芙蓉村的溪门、苍坡村的望兄亭和方巷的送弟阁，等等。

住宅一般只在柱头上安一个坐斗，坐斗的形式比较古老，有圆的和圆角海棠瓣的。极少用方形的坐斗，檐廊里穿插枋和抱头梁上承接卷棚轩或井口轩的枋子或者檩子的小构架，有方形的坐斗。有些住宅，在柱头上出一翘托住挑檐檩。

少数更讲究一些的，有正心瓜栱和厢栱。不论只出一翘的还是有瓜栱、厢栱的，都有升子（栱身称猫耳朵）。

宗祠的大门、戏台和重要的寨门，常用单翘重昂的斗栱，有时翘被装饰性构件替代，昂嘴也极富装饰性，细长而卷曲，称为象鼻。偶然也用琴面昂，很少。以芙蓉村陈氏大宗祠戏台为例，双重昂直接从坐斗挑出，无翘。平身科在承托挑檐檩的地方挂花篮柱。柱头科和角科下有一个斜撑从柱子上端伸出。角科也只有重昂。斜撑和花篮都作圆雕，斜撑甚至是个人物（仙、神）的雕像，姿态生动，不作建筑化处理。

最华丽的斗栱用于戏台和宗祠的藻井，如芙蓉陈氏大宗祠、廊下村凤南宫、渠口叶氏大宗祠和塘湾郑氏大宗祠的戏台和豫章胡氏小宗祠明间中央等。花坛村东门的三官庙（谯亭），也做这样的藻井。它们的基本做法是，从角科和平身科里拽斜向向上出若干跳的翘，每翘头上有横栱及枋子，最后为中央一块正方形的镜板。渠口、塘湾和豫章村的，上面是八角形藻井，则从角科伸出的翘在转换成八角形后就中止了，由八攒平身科的出翘继续向上。每个翘都是像流云卷草一样俯仰环曲，每个横栱两头都做"元宝斗"，像纱帽翅，而下面的梢子又刻成花篮、覆莲或者流苏。

楠溪江中游有四座建筑物的斗栱很特殊，它们是花坛村宪台牌楼和敦睦祠大门，岩头村进士牌楼和苍坡村溪门。两座门都是牌楼式的，所以可以说是四座牌楼。前三者据记载可以确认是明代建筑，苍坡村溪门虽无确证，但做法与前三者完全一样，可以判定为明代遗构。除了比例尺壮硕雄健，造型简洁，结构脉络清晰之外，它们的斗栱还有两个特点：一是大量使用偷心造的丁头栱，一是用起结构作用的下昂。例如苍坡溪门平身科，从坐斗出三挑丁头栱，上面再架两层下昂。下层下昂前端承托挑檐檩外的一条檩子。后尾压在脊檩之下。

下层下昂前端重复承托那条檩子，后尾直接嵌入上层下昂的体内，二者共同形成一个杠杆结构。花坛敦睦祠大门也是两层下昂，但下层下昂的后尾形同一翘，只给上层下昂起衬垫作用，而上层下昂则是杠杆结构。

多层丁头栱普遍用于小型建筑物上，主要是木构的院门。这种门只有一间，左右各一棵柱子，叫做"二柱门"。柱子前后，对称地跳出四五层偷心的丁头栱，前端承托檩子，上面做坡瓦顶。这些丁头栱，轻捷而有力，条理清晰，在十分简单明了的小门上起着很大的造型作用，使素朴典雅的小门显得高贵。周宅村土地庙前的二柱门，最下层第一跳丁头栱作装饰性的曲线形，使整个小品建筑更添了一分妩媚。

English

3. dougong (bracket sets)

Nanxi River basin, ancestral halls and temples mostly use dougong, especially on stages and gates. Stream gates and important pavilions mostly use dougong; commemorative buildings such as wooden archways, dougong very complex-gorgeous. residences using dougong also不少, but very simple.

dougong also has zhu tou ke (column-head set), jiao ke (corner set), ping shen ke (inter-columnar set) three categories. Ping-shen-ke only used on stages and archways, and少数 archway-style gates; practice same as column-head set.

Different from Qing official style, ping-shen-ke not densely arranged; each set distance relatively large and casual, looks sparse. ancestral hall and temple halls only have column-head sets no ping-shen-ke. Jiao-ke only used on hip-and-gable roof buildings, e.g.

villages' stages and Furong village stream gate, Cangpo village Wangxiong Pavilion and Fangxiang Songdi Ge, etc.

residences generally only install a zuo dou (seated block) on column head; seated block form relatively ancient, round and rounded-corner begonia-petal. Rarely use square seated block; eave corridor chuancha fang and bao tou liang above supporting rolling-roof arcade or well-head arcade fang or purlin small frames, have square seated blocks.

Some residences, on column head出 one qiao (bracket arm) supporting eave purlin.

少数 more refined ones, have zheng xin gua gong (center melon bracket) and xiang gong (side bracket). Regardless only one qiao or with gua gong, xiang gong, all have sheng zi (risers; bracket body called mao er duo - cat ears).

ancestral hall gates, stages, important stockade gates, commonly use single-qiao double-ang dougong; sometimes qiao replaced by decorative member; ang mouth also extremely decorative, slender and curled, called elephant trunk. Occasionally use qin-mian ang (lute-face ang), very few.

Taking Furong village Chen Main ancestral hall stage as example: double ang directly挑出 from seated block, no qiao. Ping-shen-ke at supporting eave purlin place hangs flower-basket column. Column-head and corner sets below have a diagonal brace extending from column upper end. Corner set also only double ang.

Diagonal brace and flower basket both round-carved; diagonal brace even a figure (immortal, deity) statue, posture vivid, not architecturalized.

Most gorgeous dougong used on stage and ancestral hall caisson ceilings (zao jing), e.g. Furong Chen Main ancestral hall, Langxia Fengnan Palace, Qukou Ye Main ancestral hall, Tangwan Zheng Main ancestral hall stages, and Yuzhang Hu Minor ancestral hall central bay center etc.

Huatan village east gate Sanguan temple (watch pavilion), also makes such caisson. Their basic practice: from corner and inter-columnar sets拽 diagonally upward out several jumps of qiao; each qiao head has horizontal bracket and fang; finally central square mirror board.

Qukou, Tangwan, Yuzhang villages, above octagonal caisson, then qiao from corner set after converting to octagon stops; by eight sets inter-columnar qiao continue upward.

Each qiao like flowing clouds curling grass俯仰环曲; each horizontal bracket both ends make "yuan bao dou" (ingot block), like official hat wings; and below tips carved flower baskets, inverted lotus, or tassels.

Nanxi River middle has four buildings' dougong very special: Huatan village Xiantai archway and Dunmu ancestral hall gate, Yantou village jinshi archway, Cangpo village stream gate. Two gates both archway-style, so can say four archways.

First three according to records confirmed Ming buildings; Cangpo stream gate although no确证, but practice exactly same as first three, can判定 Ming remnant.

Besides proportions robust-vigorous,造型 simple, structural脉络 clear, their dougong also has two features: one,大量 use tou-xin-zao (steal-heart) ding-tou-gong (nail-head bracket); one, use structural xia-ang (lower ang). E.g. Cangpo stream gate ping-shen-ke: from seated block out three挑 ding-tou-gong, above again frame two layers xia-ang.

Lower xia-ang front end supports a purlin outside eave purlin. Rear tail presses under ridge purlin. Lower xia-ang front end repeatedly supports that purlin; rear tail directly嵌入 upper xia-ang body; two together form a lever structure.

Huatan Dunmu ancestral hall gate also two layers xia-ang, but lower xia-ang rear tail same as one qiao, only给 upper xia-ang padding作用; upper xia-ang则 lever structure.

Multi-layer ding-tou-gong commonly used on small buildings, mainly wooden courtyard gates. This gate only one bay, left-right each one column, called "er zhu men" (two-column gate). Column front-rear, symmetrically跳 out four-five layers steal-heart ding-tou-gong; front end supports purlin; above makes sloped tile roof.

These ding-tou-gong, light and powerful,条理 clear, on very simple small gate plays large造型 role, making simple-elegant small gate appear noble. Zhouzhai village Earth God temple front two-column gate, lowest layer first跳 ding-tou-gong decorative curved shape, making entire small building add a touch of charm.

举折、生起和侧脚
中文

4. 举折、生起和侧脚

楠溪江的建筑装饰不多，很朴素，匠师们重视房屋整体的造型。为了使房屋整体看起来生动飘逸，在大木构架上作了些特殊的处理，这就是举折、生起和侧脚。

房屋的木构架的外形是很轻快通透的。大面积的屋顶，如果不加处理，就会单调而呆板。因此，首要的问题是处理屋顶，使它生动飘逸起来。

处理的有效办法是使屋顶成为一个双曲面，上下是弯曲的，左右也是弯曲的。上下的弯曲靠举折。左右的弯曲靠生起。当侧脚应用于体形完整地呈现的建筑物如亭子、寨门等的时候，它们的屋顶就更加显得轻扬如鸟翼。

（1）举折决定于每两根檩子的位置，即由下而上每两根檩子间的坡度逐步加大。不论是抬梁式还是穿斗式屋架，这样做是很容易的。确定檩子间相互的高差，在官式建筑有宋式举折和清式举架两种做法。楠溪江的乡土建筑，与官式建筑有所不同，因为檩数多而且间距比较随意，实测数据误差大，所以不能确定它们的做法是否自有一定的规矩。

进深小的，也就是檩数少的建筑物，如木构院门、宗祠廊庑等，有它们的特殊做法。院门通常只有脊檩、檐檩和挑檐檩，也就是只有一步。为了做出上下弧面，采用的办法是，从檐口到脊檩铺一层通长的椽子，然后，再从檐檩到扶脊木铺一层椽子。这样，一坡屋面就有成折线的两个坡度。在椽子上铺瓦的时候，用垫瓦的办法使屋面成为柔和的曲面。檐口的瓦因为有连檐而微微抬起一点。

楠溪江建筑绝大多数用悬山式屋顶，前后两坡弯弯的屋面，给山墙以极优雅的曲线。这些山墙构成了村落主要的面貌。

（2）屋面左右方面的弯曲，即生起，决定于相邻两榀屋架的高差，即由明间两侧的中榀梁架到山墙的边榀，逐间升高，也就是说，除了明间之外，各间的檩子是一头低一头高的。这个坡度，梢间比次间大，尽间又比梢间大。而且，同一间之内，檐檩的升高缓而脊檩的急，中间各檩依次变化。这样，屋面左右的弯曲，檐口平缓而屋脊峻急，屋面中充满张力。

例如苍坡李氏大宗祠，从脊檩往下四根檩（包括脊檩）的生起情况是：左二榀比左一榀，生起27厘米、20厘米、16厘米、10厘米；左三榀比左二榀，生起39厘米、34厘米、29厘米、23厘米（因为尽间有夹层，用草架，无法测量）。四根檩的生起，大致有5厘米的级差。就脊檩来说，七开间的房子，生起竟可达1米以上。

又例如西岸村的大石祠堂，右二榀梁架比右一榀，从脊檩往下三根檩子（包括脊檩）的生起是：20厘米，10厘米，5厘米。仿佛是个等比级数。

由于上、下各檩生起的数值不同，所以生起就不仅仅是逐榀加高柱子的事，而是整榀梁架都有变化。

楠溪江的房屋多用悬山式屋顶，屋顶挑出山墙面很多。为了保持屋面的左右弯曲的完整，在挑出部分继续生起，就使用"升头木"，这是一根一头厚一头薄的木料，附加在挑出来的檩子上。厚的一头朝外，薄的一头伸进山墙之内，大约两三个椽档。升头木依然是脊檩处升得多，往下各檩递根递减，因此有的不得不在脊檩上加两层升头木，夹在脊檩与扶脊木之间，而且把扶脊木顶得弯起。

只有一开间的小品建筑，例如院门、厕所、柴棚、猪舍，仍然坚持做出大屋顶的各种弯曲，它们的生起就全靠升头木。这种情况下，升头木伸入到梁架内侧五至七个椽档。

生起在屋脊和檐口造成的曲线是完整的，圆滑的，没有直线段落，更没有折线。开间越多生起越平缓，在中间部分，生起若有若无，在审美上十分精致。

由生起和举折造成的屋面曲线，看似简单，其实很复杂，它使屋面变得生动如有灵气。村子里屋顶层层叠叠，不但不显得沉闷，反而给村子一种柔和、轻快、丰富的气息。连厕所屋顶都做成这样的曲面，更见得楠溪江人的对美的执著追求和对美的精鉴能力。

三合院的房子，前檐左右出厢（当地称轩），所以前檐檐口是水平的，不作生起。

（3）楠溪江的建筑，多用侧脚。凡小型的、形体完整地呈现出来的建筑物，侧脚是必有的，而且很明显，例如亭子、牌楼、寨门、院门、三官庙等等。侧脚，就是这些建筑物的柱子向它的中心倾斜，在平面投影上，有两个向量。对这些建筑物来说，侧脚的作用是：第一，使结构更稳定；第二，加强形象的整体感；第三，反衬屋顶的舒展飘洒，不论是悬山顶还是歇山顶。

苍坡村的望兄亭，正方形，开间和进深都是三开间，外圈12棵柱子的侧脚平均5.5厘米，柱高320厘米，斜率为1.7%。渡头村东门外路亭，柱高273厘米，侧脚6-7厘米，斜率为2.4%。岩头的进士牌楼，四棵角柱的侧脚是30-35厘米，柱高670厘米，斜率为4.5%。花坛村敦睦祠大门平柱的侧脚斜率为2.9%。都高出于宋式的1%。敦睦祠和进士牌楼为明代建筑，它们的侧脚大于望兄亭很多，可能有时代先后的关系存在。不过。这类小建筑的侧脚的大小确实差别很大，似乎没有一定的规矩。

侧脚对小型的、形体完整地呈现出来的建筑物的意义，在多开间的长条形建筑物中，尤其是在两端伸出厢房（轩）或廊庑的建筑物中，很不明显，甚至几乎没有。住宅的正房，檐柱一般高300厘米左右，向明间中轴倾斜大约2.5-3.5厘米，斜率为0.8%-0.2%。向内的倾斜极少见。大约正是由于这一点侧脚，造成了正房与两厢间阴角上的柱子的困难，于是，这棵柱子经常被减去，而代之以一个垂莲柱或者"冬瓜柱"。也有不少住宅，观察不到侧脚。

祠堂正厅的檐柱，侧脚与住宅的相同，似有似无。

楠溪江建筑大木作，有一些重要特点与宋式建筑相同，如：普遍使用偷心的丁头栱，斗栱有起结构作用的下昂，有圆形坐斗、月梁，做生起、侧脚等。它们在明、清的官式建筑中已经没有。小柱鼓和木抱鼓，也应是比较古老的做法。这些特点，使楠溪江极素朴的建筑显得精致高雅。

楠溪江文化的极盛时期在南宋，元、明以降逐渐衰落，到明末而一蹶不振，以后就很闭塞，所以它的建筑保存了宋代的一些特点，在官式建筑变化的时候并没有跟着变化。这大约也与南宋时它离京城很近，明、清时离京城很远有关系。它的这些特点，有一部分在福建乡土建筑里可以见到。温州与福建毗邻，楠溪江中游几个重要的村落，始祖是从福建长溪迁来的，以后两地又长期保持着商业关系，建筑的影响自然难免。

有这些特点，是一种文化滞后现象，风格古老，建筑物本身并不一定古老。

English

4. ju-zhe (roof Curve), sheng-qi (Corner Rise), and ce-jiao (Column Slant)

Nanxi River architecture decoration not much, very plain; craftsmen value building overall form. To make building overall look lively-elegant, made special treatments on large-timber frame: these are ju-zhe, sheng-qi, ce-jiao.

Building timber frame exterior very light-transparent. Large-area roof, if not treated, will be monotonous-stiff. Therefore, primary issue is treating roof, making it lively-elegant.

Effective treatment method: make roof a hyperboloid, up-down curved, left-right also curved. Up-down curve relies on ju-zhe. Left-right curve relies on sheng-qi. When ce-jiao applied to buildings with complete form such as pavilions, stockade gates etc., their roofs appear even more light as bird wings.

(1) ju-zhe determined by position of every two purlins, i.e. from bottom upward slope between every two purlins gradually increases. Whether post-and-lintel or column-and-tie truss, doing this very easy. Determining purlin mutual height difference, in official architecture has Song-style ju-zhe and Qing-style ju-jia two methods.

Nanxi River vernacular architecture, different from official, because purlin count many and spacing casual, measured data error large, so cannot确定 whether their practice has own rules.

Small depth, i.e. few-purlin buildings, such as wooden courtyard gates, ancestral hall corridors etc., have their special practice. courtyard gate usually only ridge purlin, eave purlin, eave-projecting purlin, i.e. only one step.

To make up-down curved surface, method used: from eave to ridge purlin lay one layer full-length rafters; then, from eave purlin to ridge-supporting timber lay one layer rafters. Thus, one roof slope becomes two slopes forming折 line. When laying tiles on rafters, use tile-padding method to make roof soft curved surface.

Eave tiles because of continuous eave slightly raised a bit.

Nanxi River architecture绝大多数 overhanging-gable roof; front-rear two slopes curved roof, give gable wall extremely elegant curve. These gable walls constitute village main appearance.

(2) roof left-right curve, i.e. sheng-qi, determined by adjacent two trusses height difference, i.e. from central bay both sides middle truss to gable wall side truss, bay by bay rising; that is, except central bay, each bay's purlins one end low one end high.

This slope, shao-jian (second bay from end) larger than ci-jian (side bay), jin-jian (end bay) larger than shao-jian. And, within same bay, eave purlin rise gentle, ridge purlin steep; middle purlins change sequentially. Thus, roof left-right curve, eave gentle ridge steep, roof full of tension.

E.g. Cangpo Li Main ancestral hall, from ridge purlin down four purlins (including ridge purlin) sheng-qi: left second truss vs left first truss, rise 27cm, 20cm, 16cm, 10cm; left third truss vs left second truss, rise 39cm, 34cm, 29cm, 23cm (because end bay has mezzanine, uses rough frame, cannot measure).

Four purlins sheng-qi, roughly 5cm grade difference. As for ridge purlin, seven-bay building, sheng-qi can reach over 1m.

Another e.g. Xi'an village Dashi ancestral hall, right second truss vs right first truss, from ridge purlin down three purlins (including ridge purlin) sheng-qi: 20cm, 10cm, 5cm. As if geometric progression.

Because upper-lower purlins sheng-qi values different, so sheng-qi not merely truss by truss raising columns, but entire truss changes.

Nanxi River houses mostly overhanging-gable roof; roof projects far beyond gable wall. To maintain roof left-right curve completeness, in projected part continue sheng-qi, use "sheng-tou-mu" (raising-head timber); this is a timber one end thick one end thin, attached to projected purlin.

Thick end outward, thin end extends into gable wall, about two-three rafter gaps. Sheng-tou-mu still ridge purlin raises more, downward purlins progressively less; therefore some have to add two layers sheng-tou-mu on ridge purlin, sandwiched between ridge purlin and ridge-supporting timber, and bend ridge-supporting timber upward.

Only one-bay small buildings, e.g. courtyard gates, toilets, firewood sheds, pigsties, still insist making large roof various curves; their sheng-qi entirely relies on sheng-tou-mu. In this case, sheng-tou-mu extends into frame inner side five-seven rafter gaps.

sheng-qi at ridge and eave creates curve complete, smooth, no straight segments, even less折 lines. More bays sheng-qi gentler; in middle part, sheng-qi barely perceptible, aesthetically very refined.

roof curve created by sheng-qi and ju-zhe,看似 simple, actually very complex; makes roof lively as if with spirit. village roofs layer upon layer, not only not appear heavy, but give village soft, light, rich atmosphere. Even toilet roof made such curved surface, further shows Nanxi River people's persistent pursuit of beauty and refined appreciation ability.

Three-courtyard houses, front eave left-right projects wings (local xuan), so front eave edge horizontal, no sheng-qi.

(3) Nanxi River architecture, mostly uses ce-jiao. All small, completely-formed buildings, ce-jiao necessary, and very obvious, e.g. pavilions, archways, stockade gates, courtyard gates, Sanguan temple etc. ce-jiao, i.e. these buildings' columns tilt toward their center; in plan projection, two vectors.

For these buildings, ce-jiao functions: first, make structure more stable; second, strengthen image integrity; third, contrast roof's elegant spread, whether overhanging-gable or hip-and-gable.

Cangpo village Wangxiong Pavilion, square, bay and depth both three bays; outer ring 12 columns ce-jiao average 5.5cm, column height 320cm, slope 1.7%. Dutou village east gate outside road pavilion, column height 273cm, ce-jiao 6-7cm, slope 2.4%. Yantou jinshi archway, four corner columns ce-jiao 30-35cm, column height 670cm, slope 4.5%.

Huatan village Dunmu ancestral hall gate plain columns ce-jiao slope 2.9%. All higher than Song-style 1%. Dunmu Hall and jinshi archway Ming buildings; their ce-jiao much larger than Wangxiong Pavilion, possibly era sequence relation. However, such small buildings ce-jiao size indeed varies greatly, seems no fixed rule.

ce-jiao significance for small, completely-formed buildings, in multi-bay long buildings, especially buildings with wings (xuan) or corridors projecting at both ends, very unobvious, even almost none. residence main hall, eave column generally about 300cm high, tilts toward central bay axis about 2.5-3.5cm, slope 0.8%-0.2%.

Inward tilt extremely rare. Approximately precisely due to this ce-jiao, causes difficulty for column at internal corner between main hall and two wings; therefore, this column often subtracted, replaced by a hanging-lotus column or "winter-melon column." Also many residences, ce-jiao not observable.

ancestral hall main hall eave columns, ce-jiao same as residences, barely perceptible.

Nanxi River architecture large-timber work, has some important features same as Song-style architecture: e.g.普遍 use steal-heart ding-tou-gong, dougong has structural xia-ang, has round seated blocks, moon beams, makes sheng-qi, ce-jiao etc. These in Ming-Qing official architecture already gone.

Small column drums and wooden drum-braces, also should be relatively ancient practices. These features make Nanxi River extremely plain architecture appear refined-elegant.

Nanxi River culture peak period Southern Song; Yuan-Ming onward gradually declined, to late Ming collapsed, later very isolated; so its architecture preserved some Song features, when official architecture changed did not follow. This approximately also related to Southern Song时 close to capital, Ming-Qing far from capital.

Some of these features can be seen in Fujian vernacular architecture. Wenzhou adjacent to Fujian; Nanxi River middle several important villages, founding ancestors migrated from Fujian Changxi; later two places long maintained commercial relations; architectural influence natural.

Having these features, is a cultural lag phenomenon; style ancient, building itself not necessarily ancient.

本篇术语 Key Terms

举折 / Ju-zhe (roof curve) 生起 / Sheng-qi (corner rise) 侧脚 / Ce-jiao (column slant) 举架 / Ju-jia (Qing-style roof slope) 扶脊木 / Ridge-supporting timber 连檐 / Continuous eave (Lian yan) 升头木 / Sheng-tou-mu (raising-head timber) 椽档 / Rafter gap (Chuan dang) 梢间 / Shao-jian (second bay from end) 尽间 / Jin-jian (end bay) 次间 / Ci-jian (side bay) 明间 / Ming-jian (central bay) 垂莲柱 / Hanging-lotus column 冬瓜柱 / Winter-melon column 木抱鼓 / Wooden drum-brace ...等18个
5
槅扇和槛窗
中文

十三、小木装修

在大木构架确立之后，就要进行小木构件的装修。小木装修，即小木构件，如门、窗、槅扇及这些构件上的装饰，是不参与大木结构体系的构件。

楠溪江地区的小木装修，在它的历史文化背景的影响下，有着独特的风格，它们古朴，淡雅，只有重点部位作简单装饰。除少数建筑，如祠堂戏台有彩饰外，木质装修和它们的雕饰多用本色，不施任何彩绘。

小木装修主要分外檐、内檐两大类。外檐装修主要是槅扇。

1. 槅扇和槛窗

楠溪江地区建筑的特点是开敞，前后喜作檐廊。檐廊在湿热的南方是既室内又室外的过渡空间，它不仅通风好，而且遮阳避雨。尤其前廊，面对宽敞的院落，是劳作、闲居以至待客的最好的地方。因此乡民们格外重视前檐廊的装修。檐廊顶常作各式轩棚，梁、枋、瓜柱、大头梁、泥鳅梁等都富有装饰性。外檐装修，主要就是各房间开向这条前檐廊的门和窗。它们位于房屋的正面，对整座住宅的风格起着很大的作用，也对前檐廊下空间的舒适度起很大的作用。

前檐廊里，明间开门，次间和梢间开窗。门和窗，在比较整齐的住宅里，都用槅扇。

槅扇门是楠溪江地区最常见的一种门，明间作六扇三对，中间一双门扇通常每扇宽约73厘米左右，为标准门扇。边上的每扇65厘米左右。中央的门与边上的门之间有馀塞板和腰板，它们的宽度随明间面阔调整。次间和梢间各有四扇槅扇，中央一对为门，平日不开启，左右各一扇为窗。每扇门宽76厘米左右。这些宽度很稳定，家家如此。根据小风水术的"门米"，明间中央门樘的这个宽度主添丁，西边门樘的这个宽度主"财"，而次间、梢间的则主"官"。这大约就是它们的宽度稳定的原因。

在两厢，则次间多为槛窗，明间中央一对槅扇门，两旁为馀塞板和腰板，少数有六扇槅扇，外加馀塞板和腰板。

（1）槅扇门与槅扇窗是一样的，都由边挺、抹头、格心、绦环板、裙板组成，它们的组合形式特殊，不同于清官式做法，也不同于《营造法源》所载的做法。

槅扇高抵梁枋下的上槛，很高，所以门扇格心以上都加出一层或两层绦环板，而在裙板之下也常有做成双层绦环板的。装饰集中于格心和上部绦环板，这与它的总体风格的朴素一致。由于乡民们喜在廊下休闲、做家务，廊下放栏杆椅、捣臼、水磨，因此，位置较低的裙板及下部绦环板不做任何装饰，甚至连线脚都不做。

槅扇门对开。下有门槛。一侧上下各有一段门轴插入连楹、单楹。住宅内使用的连楹用一根木料做成。木料较细，做工精致。把一间中的各扇槅扇门固定在一根贯通的连楹之上，形成一个整体，稳定而不易歪闪走位。但楠溪江的住宅，连楹也有在明间中央门樘上断开而留个缺口的。

槅扇窗固定在上、下槛上，平日不开启，但可以拆卸下来。

槛窗的做法如槅扇的上半部。下半部则由通开间的槛墙代替槅扇的裙板。槛墙上缘是中槛，槛窗就安装在中槛和上槛之间。在楠溪江，槛墙都是拼板的。窗扇有转轴，上、中槛上有轴碗。

在楠溪江地区，门环、锁扣这类构件并不很发育。不过，有一些却也精致大方。锁门，在外侧可将锁扣分分别安在两个门扇上的两个门环上。门里多用门栓。

（2）格心是槅扇和槛窗最有装饰性的部分。它由断面大约为2.5厘米×3.0厘米的细木条拼镶而成透空的格心篦子。细木条都是方棂，向外的一面作或凸或凹的微弧。相互间用榫卯咬合，做工很精致。格心篦子可以整片卸下，它与门窗扇格心篦子之间用竹钉固定。

格心图案大致有五类：

柳条类：垂直的细格，与直棂窗近似，但棂子很细长。靠近上、下两头有横棂三四条，格心很高时，中段也做横棂，以增强刚度。

分格类：步步锦式，方格式。

连锁类：外接纹式，献礼纹式，风车式，楔接纹式，拼合锦式。

波纹类；万字纹式。

回端类：如意纹式。

所有各类，基本都是直线形构图。但都可以嵌花，即用小块木料雕成花卉、蝙蝠、宝瓶等等样式的卡子，嵌到格心的细木图案里去。这样，就在直线图形里增加了曲线的或写实的因素，而且增加了尺度层次，所费不多而艺术效果大大提高。

各类图案中，又大都可以在中央设一个大约20厘米×30厘米的方格，镶上镜子、玻璃、大理石片，或者精雕细刻的历史、戏剧题材的浮雕木板，极为精致。

格心背面裱糊白纸，衬托着细木条的天然深色，非常清新醒目。

在门窗槅扇内侧大都装有一块可上下移动的木板，不用时放下，位于裙板后面。用时提上，封住窗格，插好插销即可。另一些住宅考虑到木制棂格易被损坏及棂格不够严密不利防盗，在格心内侧附加一层竹编层，来增加槅扇门及窗的安全性能。

槛窗格心的形式与槅扇窗上的类似。

（3）门窗槅扇和槛窗的另一个重点装饰位置在格心上方的"抹头板"，因为常以绦环做装饰题材，所以叫绦环板。它位置高，观赏条件并不好，但不易受到损坏。

槅扇中部束腰上的装饰浮雕很丰富，有历代的传统题材，也有在生产、生活中汲取的。题材涉及戏曲小说和传闻人物、动物、植物、器物、自然山水和几何图案，都有吉祥意义，表现出村人们的理想和追求。如埭头村某宅，上房明间中央两扇门的绦环板上分别刻有百鸟之王"凤"和百花之王"牡丹"图案，构成"凤戏牡丹"。牡丹图案上又配以如意云，构成"富贵如意"。在它两侧的馀塞板上以故事人物做装饰；左、右两门四扇槅扇上饰狮、象、鹿、牛装饰；在次间、梢间的槅扇上则采用花鸟装饰，如"喜鹊登梅"、"花开富贵"、"莲（连）生贵子"、"鸳鸯戏水"等喜庆吉祥的图案。把这样众多的装饰浮雕集中于正面一排槅扇之上，体现了人们对生活的热爱和理想。

最能表现楠溪江人文历史背景和它的文化特色的，是住宅的槅扇装饰中大量、普遍地使用"笔墨纸砚"，"琴棋书画"和"渔樵耕读"等题材。前两组用实物形象加上绦带等构图，后一组则借助历史人物故事，如姜太公渭滨垂钓等等。读书取仕，始终是楠溪江人最高的追求，但在艺术上却又表现出散淡无为的格调。

虽然题材多样，应用繁杂，但在绝大多数的建筑中，明间、次间、梢间的装饰并无很大差别，题材花饰运用十分随意。只有极个别的建筑，题材的运用有明显的差别，凡这类建筑都把纹样复杂，做工精细，最具吉祥意义的图案放在中间。这个"中间"，既可以是明间中央两扇门，也可以是整个明间。两侧装饰则逐渐简化。如芙蓉村陈宅槅扇门的绦环板，明间正中两扇是卷草烘托的"寿"字，这是最具传统意味的吉祥图案。左右两侧的门用造型优美的自然形态的卷草，次间使用的是程式化的卷草图案，装饰从繁到简，显示出了装饰纹样的重点和建筑的重点的一致。

在少数质量平常的住宅上，装饰等级明显降低。绦环板上，除在明间用简洁的卷草花饰，其他有用素绦环板的。

绦环板上的装饰多为突起的浅浮雕。底部在同一平面上。在运用人物故事场面做装饰题材时，画面常有透视感。把绦环板雕透的也有，是将底子按装饰性几何图案雕透，衬托花鸟或人物。

轩间槅扇门的装饰做法一般与正房相同。

English

XIII. small-timber joinery (Xiao Mu Zhuangxiu)

After large-timber frame established, proceed to small-timber member joinery. small-timber joinery, i.e. small-timber members such as doors, windows, lattice doors (ge shan) and decorations on these members, are members not participating in large-timber structural system.

Nanxi River region small-timber joinery, under its historical-cultural background influence, has unique style: ancient-plain, elegant, only key positions simple decoration. Except少数 buildings such as ancestral hall stages have colored decoration, wooden joinery and their carvings mostly use natural color, no any painted decoration.

small-timber joinery mainly divided into outer-eave and inner-eave two categories. Outer-eave joinery mainly lattice doors.

1. lattice doors (ge shan) and sill windows (Jian Chuang)

Nanxi River region architecture feature is open; front-rear like to make eave corridors. eave corridor in hot-humid south is both indoor-outdoor transitional space; not only good ventilation, but also sunshade-rain shelter. Especially front corridor, facing spacious courtyard, is best place for labor, leisure, even receiving guests.

Therefore villagers格外 value front eave corridor joinery. eave corridor top often makes various arcade ceilings; beams, fang, melon columns, big-head beams, loach beams etc. all decorative. Outer-eave joinery, mainly is each room's doors and windows opening toward this front eave corridor.

They located on building front, play large role in entire residence style, also play large role in front eave corridor space comfort.

In front eave corridor, central bay opens door, side bays and second-from-end bays open windows. Doors and windows, in relatively neat residences, all use lattice doors.

lattice door is Nanxi River region most common door; central bay makes six leaves three pairs; middle pair door leaves usually each about 73cm wide, standard door leaf. Side leaves each about 65cm. Between central door and side doors have surplus-filler boards and waist boards; their width adjusts with central bay width.

Side bays and second-from-end bays each have four lattice leaves; central pair as door, normally not opened; left-right each one as window. Each door leaf about 76cm wide. These widths very stable, every household same.

According to small fengshui "men mi" (door measure), central bay central door frame this width masters adding sons, west door frame this width masters "wealth", while side bays' master "official". This approximately is reason their widths stable.

In two wings, then side bays mostly sill windows; central bay central pair lattice doors; both sides surplus-filler boards and waist boards;少数 have six lattice leaves, plus surplus-filler boards and waist boards.

(1) lattice door and lattice window same, both composed of side stiles (bian ting), rails (mo tou), lattice core (ge xin), transom panel (tiao huan ban), skirt board (qun ban); their combination form special, different from Qing official practice, also different from "Yingzao Fayan" recorded practice.

lattice door high reaches upper sill under beam-fang, very high; so door leaf above lattice core all add one or two layers transom panels; and below skirt board also often made double-layer transom panels. decoration concentrated on lattice core and upper transom panels; this consistent with its overall plain style.

Because villagers like leisure, housework under corridor; corridor places railing chairs, pounding mortars, water mills; therefore, lower-positioned skirt boards and lower transom panels no any decoration, even no moldings.

lattice door double-leaf. Below has threshold. One side upper-lower each has a door pivot inserted into continuous jamb (lian ying), single jamb. residence used continuous jamb made of one timber. Timber relatively fine, workmanship refined.

Fix each lattice door leaf in one bay on one through continuous jamb, forming a whole, stable not easy to warp-shift. But Nanxi River residences, continuous jamb also has断开 at central bay central door frame leaving a gap.

lattice window fixed on upper-lower sills, normally not opened, but can be disassembled.

sill window practice like lattice door upper half. Lower half then by through-bay sill wall replaces lattice door skirt board. sill wall upper edge is middle sill; sill window installed between middle sill and upper sill. In Nanxi River, sill walls all plank-built. Window sash has pivot; upper-middle sills have pivot cups.

In Nanxi River region, door rings, lock clasps such members not very developed. However, some also refined-generous. Locking door, outside can install lock clasps separately on two door leaves' two door rings. Inside mostly use door bolt.

(2) lattice core is lattice door and sill window most decorative part. It by cross-section approximately 2.5cm×3.0cm thin wood strips assembled into openwork lattice-core screen. Thin wood strips all square mullions; outward face made convex or concave slight arc. Mutually mortise-tenon咬合, workmanship very refined.

Lattice-core screen can whole piece remove; it and door-window leaf lattice-core screen between fixed with bamboo nails.

lattice core patterns roughly five categories:

Willow category: vertical fine grid, similar to straight-mullion window, but mullions very slender. Near upper-lower two ends have horizontal mullions 3-4; when lattice core very high, middle section also makes horizontal mullions to enhance stiffness.

Grid category: step-by-step brocade style, square grid style.

Interlocking category: external-connection pattern, offering pattern, windmill style, wedge-joint pattern, combined brocade style.

Wave category; wan-character pattern.

Return-end category: ruyi pattern.

All categories, basically straight-line composition. But all can inlay flowers, i.e. use small wood blocks carved into flowers, bats, treasure vases etc. style clips, inlay into lattice core fine-wood pattern. Thus, in straight-line graphics increased curved or realistic factors, and increased scale layers; cost not much but artistic effect greatly improved.

Various patterns, again mostly can in center set an approximately 20cm×30cm square grid, inlay mirrors, glass, marble slices, or finely carved history, drama theme relief wood boards, extremely refined.

lattice core back pasted white paper, setting off thin wood strips natural dark color, very fresh-eye-catching.

Inside door-window lattice leaves mostly installed a board movable up-down; when not used put down, located behind skirt board. When used lift up, seal window grid, insert latch即可.

Other residences considering wooden mullion grid easily damaged and mullion grid not tight enough unfavorable anti-theft, inside lattice core add a layer bamboo-woven layer, to increase lattice door and window safety performance.

sill window lattice core form similar to lattice window.

(3) Door-window lattice leaves and sill window another key decorative position is "rail board" above lattice core; because often uses tiao-huan as decorative theme, so called transom panel (tiao huan ban). It position high, viewing conditions not good, but not easily damaged.

lattice door middle waist decorative relief very rich; has历代 traditional themes, also absorbed from production, life. Themes involve drama novels and rumored figures, animals, plants, vessels, natural landscapes and geometric patterns, all auspicious meaning, showing villagers' ideals and pursuits. E.g.

Daitou village某 residence, upper room central bay central two doors' transom panels respectively carved king of birds "phoenix" and king of flowers "peony" patterns, constituting "phoenix plays peony." Peony pattern again matched ruyi clouds, constituting "wealth-ruyi." On its both sides surplus-filler boards use story figures decoration; left-right two doors four lattice leaves decorated lion, elephant, deer, ox; on side bays' lattice leaves then use flower-bird decoration, such as "magpie on plum", "flowers bloom wealth", "lotus (continuous) gives noble sons", "mandarin ducks play water" etc.

festive-auspicious patterns. Concentrating such many decorative reliefs on front row lattice leaves, reflects people's love of life and ideals.

Most can express Nanxi River humanistic historical background and its cultural features, is residence lattice decoration大量,普遍 use "brush-ink-paper-inkstone", "qin-chess-calligraphy-painting" and "fisherman-woodcutter-farmer-scholar" etc. themes. First two groups use physical images plus ribbons etc.

composition; latter group借助 historical figure stories, e.g. Jiang Taigong Wei River fishing etc. Study for officialdom, always Nanxi River people highest pursuit, but in art又表现 detached-inactive style.

Although themes diverse, application complex, but in绝大多数 buildings, central bay, side bays, second-from-end bays decoration no big difference; theme flower decoration use very casual. Only very个别 buildings, theme use obvious difference; all such buildings put pattern complex, workmanship fine, most auspicious-meaning pattern in middle.

This "middle", either central bay central two doors, or entire central bay. Both sides decoration then gradually simplified. E.g. Furong village Chen residence lattice door transom panels, central bay正中 two leaves are scrolling grass foil "shou" (longevity) character, this most traditional-meaning auspicious pattern.

Left-right side doors use beautifully shaped natural-form scrolling grass; side bays use stylized scrolling grass pattern; decoration from complex to simple, shows decorative pattern key and building key consistent.

In少数 ordinary-quality residences, decoration grade明显降低. transom panels, except central bay use simple scrolling grass flower decoration, others use plain transom panels.

transom panel decoration mostly raised low-relief. Bottom on same plane. When using figure story scenes as decorative theme, picture often has perspective. carving transom panel through also has, is carving bottom according to decorative geometric pattern through, setting off flowers-birds or figures.

Wing-room lattice door decoration practice generally same as main hall.

本篇术语 Key Terms

小木装修 / Small-timber joinery (Xiao mu zhuangxiu) 槅扇 / Ge shan (lattice door/window) 槛窗 / Jian chuang (sill window) 边挺 / Bian ting (side stile) 抹头 / Mo tou (rail) 格心 / Ge xin (lattice core) 绦环板 / Tiao huan ban (transom panel) 裙板 / Qun ban (skirt board) 连楹 / Lian ying (continuous jamb) 门簪 / Men zan (door pin) 馀塞板 / Surplus-filler board 腰板 / Waist board 槛墙 / Sill wall (Jian qiang) 步步锦 / Bu bu jin (step-by-step brocade) 万字纹 / Wan-character pattern ...等22个
6
其他门窗
中文

2. 其他门窗

除槅扇门窗和槛窗外，楠溪江建筑常用的门窗形式不多，普遍的只有板门和直棂窗。

（1）直棂窗多用在住宅的背面、山墙面、楼上或者比较简陋的住宅的正面。也常见于明末清初的古老的住宅中，风格很古拙淳朴。

它的做法是，在上槛和中槛之间设两根立颊，立颊之间就是窗洞。槛墙以及立颊外侧都用拼板或者竹笆抹泥封住。窗洞里楞木垂直均匀分布，相隔距离约10厘米，楞木断面形状有正方形、圆柱形和三角形。楞木宽约3.5厘米。用方形楞木时，楞木平面朝外平行排列，用三角形楞木时，尖角朝外，保持室内一面为平面，便于糊窗纸。为保持楞木的整体性和稳固性，常在楞木的上方和下方靠近楞木根部做水平的两排木雕卡子，既美观又牢固。

这种直棂窗可以用木板关闭，木板沿中槛与上槛上的槽口水平滑动，开启时就在窗口两侧的板壁后面。

（2）住宅的大门、侧门、后门和厨房门以及房间之间的门，都用板门。除了院门之外，都是单扇。板门扇是拼板法做的，没有边框，只有腰串。板门的门轴都由一根木料做成，门轴两头上下做成圆柱形，向上插入连楹、向下插入单楹。祠堂、庙宇的大门高抵枋子下，常用门簪把连楹固定在枋子上，门簪向外的一头做成花瓣形，用两个、四个不等，很有装饰效果。

关门的时候，单扇的用插关，双扇的用水平的或垂直的栓杠，也有用插关的，把两扇板门连接起来。

板门一般没有装饰，只有门环、钉销。但庙宇的门上常有大幅神像，一扇一幅，整扇画满。如塘湾的山隍庙和泰石村附近的圣湖庙，庙门上都有色彩极为艳丽浓重的神像画，在山野中，在毛石墙上，它们格外照眼，很有民间美术的粗犷力量。

宅第大门，如是双扇的，则每扇用五块直板拼成；如是单扇的，则每扇用十块较窄的直板拼成，取意"十全十美"。不过，有的把门轴和边框计算在内，有的不计。

English

2. Other Doors and Windows

Except lattice doors-windows and sill windows, Nanxi River architecture commonly used door-window forms not many;普遍 only plank doors and straight-mullion windows.

(1) straight-mullion windows mostly used on residence back, gable wall, upstairs, or relatively simple residence front. Also common in late Ming-early Qing ancient residences, style very ancient-plain.

Its practice: between upper sill and middle sill set two side jambs (li jia); between side jambs is window opening. sill wall and side jamb outside both use planks or bamboo lattice plastered mud sealed. In window opening, mullions vertically evenly distributed, spacing about 10cm; mullion cross-section shapes square, cylindrical, triangular.

Mullion width about 3.5cm. When using square mullions, mullion flat face outward parallel排列; when using triangular mullions, sharp angle outward, keeping indoor side flat, convenient for pasting window paper.

To maintain mullion integrity and stability, often above and below mullions near mullion root make horizontal two rows wood-carving clips, both beautiful and firm.

This straight-mullion window can be closed with wood board; board slides horizontally along grooves on middle sill and upper sill; when opened, it is behind panel walls on both sides of window opening.

(2) residence main gate, side gate, back gate, kitchen gate, and doors between rooms, all use plank doors. Except courtyard gates, all single-leaf. plank door leaf made by plank-joining method, no frame, only waist ties (yao chuan).

plank door pivot all made of one timber; door pivot both ends upper-lower made cylindrical, upward inserted into continuous jamb, downward inserted into single jamb.

ancestral hall, temple main gates high reach under fang; often use door pins (men zan) to fix continuous jamb on fang; door pin outward end made petal shape, using two, four不等, very decorative effect.

When closing door, single-leaf uses latch (cha guan); double-leaf uses horizontal or vertical bolt bar, also uses latch, connecting two plank door leaves.

plank door generally no decoration, only door rings, nail pins. But temple doors often have large deity paintings, one per leaf, whole leaf painted full. E.g. Tangwan Shanhuang temple and near Taishi village Shenghu temple, temple doors all have extremely vivid-rich deity paintings; in mountains, on rubble stone walls, they格外 eye-catching, very folk art rough power.

Mansion main gate, if double-leaf, then each leaf uses five straight planks joined; if single-leaf, then each leaf uses ten narrower straight planks joined, meaning "perfect in every respect." However, some include door pivot and frame in count, some not.

本篇术语 Key Terms

板门 / Plank door (Ban men) 直棂窗 / Straight-mullion window (Zhi ling chuang) 立颊 / Li jia (side jamb) 楞木 / Leng mu (mullion) 腰串 / Yao chuan (waist tie) 插关 / Cha guan (latch) 栓杠 / Shuan gang (bolt bar) 门环 / Door ring (Men huan) 钉销 / Nail pin (Ding xiao) 十全十美 / Perfect in every respect (Shi quan shi mei)

太师壁和神龛
中文

3. 太师壁和神龛

太师壁和神龛属于内檐小木装修。

太师壁大量用于住宅的明间厅堂，其次用于戏台，分隔表演区和后台。神龛用于宗祠和庙宇。

（1）楠溪江地区住宅建筑进深大，室内光线很暗，除了晚间休息，人们绝大多数时间在檐廊下和厅堂中度过。明间厅堂是供奉先入神主和年节祭祀的地方。家庭红白喜事，日常仪礼，会见亲友，也都在厅堂里。因此住宅内檐装修的重点就是厅堂。一般住宅在厅堂后明柱位置做太师壁，这是给神主和祖像做背衬用的，很有庄严的气派。太师壁高度直达楼板梁之下，左右有门通后半间。前面有条案供奉着祖宗牌位。太师壁形式很多，有做成槅扇式的，不能开启，但能卸下。常将它漆成墨色，十分严肃。在太师壁两侧的门上做木作门罩。门洞有花式发券形的，有在上面做镂空雕饰花板的。有的还装上槅扇门，与中央部分一起，一色八扇或十扇槅扇。

有些人家，在太师壁上方挂一个很精致的神橱，奉安神主。神橱甚至有斗栱，也是偷心的丁头栱。少量的有瓜栱、厢栱。

戏台后面的太师壁，在演出的时候悬挂"守旧"，即作为戏班子标志的绣花帷帐。两侧的门，右方为上场门"出将"，左方为下场门"入相"。

（2）在祠堂中，内檐装修着重在神龛，有在正厅明间做神龛的，也有贯通整个正厅3-5开间的。做工十分讲究。

神龛是供奉祖先牌位的。通常有两种做法：一种为砖木结合，下部用砖砌一个台子，上面做木装修罩。为了统一风格，砖台常常隐砌出简单木制构件的形式。也有砌出香案的。木罩仿大木构架的做法而缩小尺度。用小柱子把一个开间分成三开间。上面是华板和楣子。往往加设一层或两层楣子和华板，使神龛有深度感和层次感，有的还在最外层的柱子两侧各做一扇槅扇，不能开启，两扇槅扇之间做花楣子，很像槅扇罩的形式。

龛内后壁面作为神主的背景，有的在墙面上贴一排木槅扇，槅扇上绦环板都有精致的木雕，以实拼板代格心，上面绘有山水，花鸟，人物故事等图案，整齐而富有变化。比较简陋的则在后壁上画出木槅扇的形式。有些庙宇，如圣湖庙，把神像放在神橱里。神橱像一座封闭的阁子，雕镂得过分堆砌。

English

3. taishi wall (Grand Tutor Wall) and shrines (Shen Kan)

taishi wall and shrines belong to inner-eave small-timber joinery.

taishi wall大量 used in residence central-bay hall, secondly used on stages, separating performance area and backstage. shrines used in ancestral halls and temples.

(1) Nanxi River region residence buildings deep depth, indoor light very dark; except evening rest, people绝大多数 time spend under eave corridors and in halls. Central-bay hall is place for enshrining ancestral spirit tablets and festival sacrifices. Family weddings-funerals, daily rituals, receiving relatives-friends, also all in hall.

Therefore residence inner-eave joinery key is hall. General residence at hall rear central-column position makes taishi wall; this is for spirit tablets and ancestor portraits as backdrop, very solemn aura. taishi wall height directly reaches under floor beams; left-right have doors to rear half-room.

Front has altar table enshrining ancestral tablets. taishi wall forms many; some made lattice-door style, cannot open, but can remove. Often painted ink-black, very solemn. On taishi wall both sides doors make wooden door canopies. Door openings have patterned arch forms, some above make openwork carved decorative boards.

Some also install lattice doors, with central part together, uniform eight or ten lattice leaves.

Some households, above taishi wall hang a very exquisite shrine cabinet (shen chu), enshrine spirit tablets. shrine cabinet even has dougong, also steal-heart ding-tou-gong. Few have gua-gong, xiang-gong.

taishi wall behind stage, during performance hangs "shou jiu" (keeping old), i.e. embroidered curtain as troupe标志. Both sides doors, right side as entrance door "chu jiang" (exit general), left side as exit door "ru xiang" (enter minister).

(2) In ancestral halls, inner-eave joinery focuses on shrines; some make shrine in main hall central bay, some through entire main hall 3-5 bays. Workmanship very refined.

shrine is for enshrining ancestral tablets. Usually two practices: one brick-wood combination, lower part uses brick to build a platform, above makes wooden joinery canopy. To unify style, brick platform often implicitly bricks simple wooden-member forms. Also some brick incense tables.

Wooden canopy imitates large-timber frame practice but reduced scale. Use small columns to divide one bay into three bays. Above is decorative boards (hua ban) and lintels (mei zi).

Often add one or two layers lintels and decorative boards, making shrine have depth and layering; some also at outermost layer columns both sides each make one lattice leaf, cannot open; between two lattice leaves make floral lintel, very like lattice-canopy form.

shrine inner rear wall as spirit tablet background; some on wall surface paste a row wooden lattice leaves; lattice leaves transom panels all have exquisite wood carving; use solid joined boards instead of lattice core; above painted landscapes, flowers-birds, figure stories etc. patterns, neat and varied.

Relatively simple ones then on rear wall paint wooden lattice form. Some temples, e.g. Shenghu temple, put deity statues in shrine cabinet. shrine cabinet like a closed pavilion, carving overly piled.

本篇术语 Key Terms

太师壁 / Taishi wall (Grand Tutor wall) 神龛 / Shrine (Shen kan) 神橱 / Shrine cabinet (Shen chu) 内檐装修 / Inner-eave joinery 门罩 / Door canopy (Men zhao) 守旧 / Shou jiu (stage backdrop curtain) 出将 / Chu jiang (stage entrance) 入相 / Ru xiang (stage exit) 华板 / Hua ban (decorative board) 楣子 / Mei zi (lintel) 香案 / Incense table (Xiang an) 圣湖庙 / Shenghu Temple
