装饰题材
中文

4. 装饰题材

楠溪江地区建筑装饰题材多，内容丰富，都是传统的吉祥、喜庆、教化之类。

（1）题材大致有以下各类

人物类：住宅槅扇门上使用的人物题材，多数为小说、戏曲和历史故事，如刘关张桃园三结义、姜太公渭滨垂钓、诸葛亮隆中高卧，以及二十四孝、渔樵耕读等。

祠堂神龛上的人物多为神话传说，如八仙故事、和合二仙等。

动物类：民间不得以龙为装饰题材，但住宅中偶然有变异的龙，如草龙，拐子龙，如意龙，卷草龙、回纹龙等，数量很少。比较多的是狮子、象、鹿、虎、牛、马、蝙蝠等。禽鸟则有孔雀、喜鹊、鸳鸯、鹌鹑，都各有吉祥含意。

植物类：牡丹是最常见的题材。其馀如梅、莲、竹、菊、兰等。

器物类：有文房四宝，琴棋书画，博古，八吉祥（佛教法器，有法螺、法轮、宝伞、白盖、莲花、宝瓶、金鱼、盘子），十宝（珠、锭、方胜、如意、犀角、珊瑚、磬、书、笔、艾叶），暗八仙（八仙所携的神器，有芭蕉扇、剑、渔鼓、玉板、葫芦、箫、花篮、荷花）。其他还有罐、盒、钟、钱、笙、灯笼等。

自然景物：多用于为人物故事造环境，如日、月、山、水、云、石等。

几何形：方格、水波等。

文字：福、寿、万、双喜，还有八卦。

（2）这些题材的喜庆吉祥意义和教化作用的获得大约有以下的方式

寓意：如文房四宝，琴棋书画，渔樵耕读；又如八仙(祝寿)，天官(赐福)，二十四孝，等等。

比德：如岁寒三友（松、竹、梅），四君子（梅、兰、竹、菊）；又如莲（清廉），鸳鸯（夫妻恩爱）等。

象征：如龟鹤（长寿），牡丹（富贵），石榴、瓜（多子），桃（长寿）等等。

谐音：如鹿（禄），蝙蝠（福），花瓶与鹌鹑（平安），猫与蝴蝶（耄耋），鲶鱼（年年有馀），戟与馨（吉庆）等。

转借：如桃园结义（义），隆中高卧（志），季札挂剑（信），封印辞金（忠），竹林七贤（高蹈）等。

综合：由以上的二三种方式综合起作用，含意比较复杂。如，喜鹊与梅花（喜上眉梢），用蝙蝠组成一个福字（福中有福），蝙蝠望着古钱中孔（"福"在眼前），花瓶里插一支戟，旁边放一只馨，一只鹌鹑（平安吉庆），莲花、荔枝、核桃、桂圆（连中三元），等等。

要理解所有这些题材的喜庆、吉祥、教化意义，需要有丰富的知识，需要有相当高的文化素养，还需要有联想能力。

这些题材，全面地反映了楠溪江人的理想和愿望。这些，也都是宗法制度之下全中国世世代代人民的理想和愿望。建筑是人们最基本的生活环境，是人类最基本的创造物之一，他们把自己全部的对生活的热爱，全部的价值观，都熔铸到建筑里面去了。装饰题材，不过是最显露、最容易被人理解的一部分罢了。

English

4. Decorative Themes

Nanxi River region architecture decorative themes many, content rich, all traditional auspicious, festive, moral-education types.

(1) Themes roughly following categories

Figure category: residence lattice doors used figure themes, mostly novels, dramas, historical stories, e.g. Liu-Guan-Zhang Peach Garden Oath, Jiang Taigong Wei River Fishing, Zhuge Liang Longzhong Recluse, and Twenty-four Filial Exemplars, fisherman-woodcutter-farmer-scholar etc.

ancestral hall shrine figures mostly mythological legends, e.g. Eight Immortals stories, He-He Two Immortals etc.

Animal category: folk may not use dragon as decorative theme, but residences occasionally have variant dragons, e.g. grass dragon,拐子 dragon, ruyi dragon, scrolling-grass dragon, meander dragon etc., quantity very few. More common are lions, elephants, deer, tigers, oxen, horses, bats etc. Birds have peacocks, magpies, mandarin ducks, quails, each with auspicious meaning.

plant category: peony is most common theme. Others such as plum, lotus, bamboo, chrysanthemum, orchid etc.

Object category: has Four Treasures of Study, qin-chess-calligraphy-painting, antiquities (bo gu), Eight Auspicious Symbols (Buddhist ritual implements: conch, dharma wheel, precious umbrella, white canopy, lotus, precious vase, goldfish, dish), Ten Treasures (pearl, ingot, interlocking-lozenge, ruyi, rhinoceros horn, coral, chime, book, brush, mugwort), Hidden Eight Immortals (implements carried by Eight Immortals: banana-leaf fan, sword, fish-drum, jade tablet, gourd, flute, flower basket, lotus).

Others also jars, boxes, bells, coins, sheng, lanterns etc.

Natural scenery: mostly used to create environment for figure stories, e.g. sun, moon, mountain, water, cloud, stone etc.

Geometric: square grid, water waves etc.

Characters: fu (blessing), shou (longevity), wan (ten thousand), double happiness, also bagua (eight trigrams).

(2) These themes' festive-auspicious meaning and moral-education function obtained roughly following ways

Allegory (yu yi): e.g. Four Treasures of Study, qin-chess-calligraphy-painting, fisherman-woodcutter-farmer-scholar; also Eight Immortals (longevity blessing), Heavenly Official (bestowing blessing), Twenty-four Filial Exemplars, etc.

Moral analogy (bi de): e.g. Three Friends of Winter (pine, bamboo, plum), Four Gentlemen (plum, orchid, bamboo, chrysanthemum); also lotus (integrity), mandarin ducks (conjugal love) etc.

Symbolism (xiang zheng): e.g. turtle-crane (longevity), peony (wealth), pomegranate, melon (many sons), peach (longevity) etc.

Homophone (xie yin): e.g. deer (official salary), bat (blessing), vase and quail (peace), cat and butterfly (octogenarian-nonagenarian), catfish (yearly surplus), halberd and chime (auspicious celebration) etc.

Borrowed meaning (zhuan jie): e.g. Peach Garden Oath (righteousness), Longzhong Recluse (ambition), Jizi Hanging Sword (trust), Sealing Seal Refusing Gold (loyalty), Seven Sages of Bamboo Grove (lofty detachment) etc.

Combined (zong he): by above two-three methods combined acting, meaning relatively complex. E.g.

magpie and plum (joy on brow), using bats to compose a fu character (blessing within blessing), bat looking at ancient coin center hole ("blessing before eyes"), vase with one halberd, beside one chime, one quail (peace-auspicious-celebration), lotus, lychee, walnut, longan (continuous passing three exams) etc.

To understand all these themes' festive, auspicious, moral-education meaning, requires rich knowledge, requires fairly high cultural accomplishment, also requires associative ability.

These themes, comprehensively reflect Nanxi River people's ideals and aspirations. These, also are all under patriarchal clan system all China generations people's ideals and aspirations.

Architecture is people's most basic living environment, is one of humanity's most basic creations; they cast all their love of life, all their values, into architecture. Decorative themes, merely most exposed, most easily understood part.

本篇术语 Key Terms

装饰题材 / Decorative themes 文房四宝 / Four Treasures of Study 八吉祥 / Eight Auspicious Symbols 暗八仙 / Hidden Eight Immortals 岁寒三友 / Three Friends of Winter 四君子 / Four Gentlemen 二十四孝 / Twenty-four Filial Exemplars 八仙 / Eight Immortals 和合二仙 / He-He Two Immortals 桃园三结义 / Peach Garden Oath 渔樵耕读 / Fisherman-Woodcutter-Farmer-Scholar 喜上眉梢 / Joy on brow (magpie on plum) 连中三元 / Continuous passing three exams 博古 / Bo gu (antiquities)
9
台基与台阶
中文

十四、砖石作

砖石作包括地面、路面、台阶、台基、墙体等等。楠溪江石作中最有特色的是用天然大卵石和蛮石砌筑的墙体，它们使楠溪江的建筑苍健刚挺，好像与山岩一起生成。

1. 台基与台阶

（1）建造房子首先要建一个结实牢固的基座，也就是台基，然后才立柱造屋。楠溪江地区，不论公共建筑还是住宅，台基一般都不高，约45~50厘米，做三步台阶上下。地处低洼或在溪岸边则台基较高，多用毛石或大卵石垒砌。有些台基有台帮和阶条石，有些没有，全用卵石砌筑。庙宇祠堂有散水，而大多数住宅不见有散水。

建筑有等级差别，砖石做法上也同样如此。如祠堂、书院，台基虽与住宅相同，但室内铺地却比较讲究，常用整齐的砖或石。砖有方，有长方。长方形的砖块一般铺成"席"纹，用方砖则铺棋盘格，或两行间错开半块砖。这些砖做工细，质量好。如芙蓉书院讲堂、西岸村大石祠堂、豫章村胡氏大宗祠等，使用方砖铺地，砖块尺寸29.5厘米×29.5厘米×5.0厘米，砖块排列成45°角。砖墁至台基前沿用规整的阶条石收边，内外整齐美观。

住宅中，地面铺设很简单，一般用夯土地，最后在夯土面上淋一层盐卤，再压实，压光，地面很耐磨。在廊步中，地面还有用大小比较整齐的卵石镶铺的，甚至也有用稍大一点的卵石为材料砌筑台帮并取代阶条石的。为了美观还做出一些图案，席纹。

（2）台阶与台基的做法一般是配套的，最常见的大致有下列几种：

①垂带式。使用很普遍。如芙蓉村某祠堂，苍坡村寨门。垂带常有变异形式，如作成琴面式略微卷曲，或再在上部做一个小鼓。琴面上常有浅浮雕。

②如意式。这种形式使用不多，有条石如意和卵石如意。

③如意、垂带混合式。

④自由式。多见于住宅之中，最简便的是用天然卵石叠成。

English

XIV. brick-stone work (Zhuan Shi Zuo)

brick-stone work includes floors, road surfaces, steps, podiums, walls etc. Nanxi River stone work most distinctive is walls built with natural large cobblestones and rough stones; they make Nanxi River architecture vigorous-stiff, as if generated together with mountain rocks.

1. podium (Tai Ji) and Steps

(1) Building a house first must build a solid-firm base, i.e. podium, then erect columns and build roof. Nanxi River region, regardless public buildings or residences, podium generally not high, about 45-50cm, makes three steps up-down. Located low-lying or by stream bank then podium higher, mostly use rubble stone or large cobblestones piled.

Some podiums have podium side walls (tai bang) and step-edge stones (jie tiao shi), some not, all cobblestone built. temples-ancestral halls have drainage apron (san shui), while most residences no drainage apron.

Buildings have grade differences; brick-stone practice also same. E.g. ancestral halls, academies, podium although same as residences, but indoor flooring more refined, commonly use neat bricks or stones. Bricks have square, rectangular.

Rectangular bricks generally laid in "mat" pattern; using square bricks then laid checkerboard, or between two rows offset half brick. These bricks fine workmanship, good quality. E.g.

Furong academy lecture hall, Xi'an village Dashi ancestral hall, Yuzhang village Hu Main ancestral hall etc., use square brick flooring; brick size 29.5cm×29.5cm×5.0cm; bricks arranged at 45° angle. Brick paving to podium front edge uses regular step-edge stones to border, inside-out neat-beautiful.

In residences, floor laying very simple; generally use rammed-earth floor; finally on rammed-earth surface sprinkle a layer of salt brine, then compact, polish, floor very wear-resistant.

In corridor steps, floor also uses relatively neat-sized cobblestones inlaid; even also uses slightly larger cobblestones as material to build podium side walls and replace step-edge stones. For beauty also makes some patterns, mat pattern.

(2) Steps and podium practice generally matching; most common roughly following several:

① chui-dai style (with sloped side walls). Use very common. E.g. Furong village某 ancestral hall, Cangpo village stockade gate. Chui-dai often has variant forms, e.g. made lute-face style slightly curled, or again on upper part make a small drum. Lute-face often has low-relief.

② ruyi style. This form use not much; has strip-stone ruyi and cobblestone ruyi.

③ Ruyi-chui-dai mixed style.

④ Free style. Mostly seen in residences; simplest is using natural cobblestones piled.

本篇术语 Key Terms

砖石作 / Brick-stone work (Zhuan shi zuo) 台基 / Podium (Tai ji) 台阶 / Steps (Tai jie) 台帮 / Podium side wall (Tai bang) 阶条石 / Step-edge stone (Jie tiao shi) 散水 / Drainage apron (San shui) 垂带式 / Chui-dai style (sloped side wall steps) 如意式 / Ruyi style steps 夯土地 / Rammed-earth floor 盐卤 / Salt brine 蛮石 / Rough stone (Man shi) 卵石 / Cobblestone (Luan shi)
10
院落铺地
中文

2. 院落铺地

住宅的院落经常充当晒谷场、晾衣场，因此常把宅院的地面整个铺砌起来，一方面晾晒东西干净；另一方面，大雨时可迅速排出院落，小雨时可由石缝渗入地下，避免院落泥泞。做法分用小卵石或半规则的方石两种。半规则的方石多以45°斜角满铺院落，如苍坡某宅院，方石大小为17.0厘米×17.0厘米。用砖的多为错缝平铺。用小卵石镶铺的有素铺，有做成图案的。如苍坡村某宅用卵石镶成象征喜庆的灯笼图案。卵石的大小约8.0厘米×4.0厘米。比较讲究的住宅，如岩头村的几座"进士第"和芙蓉村、溪口村、廊下村等的一些住宅，院子中央有一道用条石铺的甬路，条石长约150厘米，宽30~40厘米。

祠堂和书院由于等级的需要，礼仪集会的要求，院内地面多用方砖满铺并在院中间铺条石甬路。如芙蓉书院内为方砖平铺，砖的尺寸是30.0厘米×30.0厘米×4.0厘米。甬路的做法是，用120~150厘米长的条石横铺，两侧用条石纵向铺砌收边。

English

2. courtyard paving (Yuan Luo Pu Di)

residence courtyards often serve as grain-drying fields, clothes-drying fields; therefore often pave entire courtyard ground, on one hand drying things clean; on other hand, heavy rain can quickly drain courtyard, light rain can seep underground through stone gaps, avoiding courtyard mud.

Practices divided into using small cobblestones or semi-regular square stones two types. Semi-regular square stones mostly at 45° oblique angle fully pave courtyard, e.g. Cangpo某 residence, square stone size 17.0cm×17.0cm. Using bricks mostly staggered-joint flat paving. Using small cobblestones inlaid has plain paving, has made into patterns. E.g.

Cangpo village某 residence uses cobblestones inlaid into lantern pattern symbolizing festivity. Cobblestone size about 8.0cm×4.0cm. More refined residences, e.g. Yantou village several "jinshi Di" (jinshi mansion) and Furong village, Xikou village, Langxia village etc.

some residences, courtyard center has a strip-stone paved path (yong lu); strip stone length about 150cm, width 30-40cm.

ancestral halls and academies due to grade needs, ritual-gathering requirements, courtyard ground mostly use square bricks fully paved and in courtyard middle pave strip-stone path. E.g. Furong academy inside is square brick flat paving; brick size 30.0cm×30.0cm×4.0cm. path practice: use 120-150cm long strip stones cross-paved; both sides use strip stones longitudinally paved border.

本篇术语 Key Terms

院落铺地 / Courtyard paving 甬路 / Yong lu (courtyard path) 条石 / Strip stone (Tiao shi) 方砖 / Square brick (Fang zhuan) 错缝平铺 / Staggered-joint flat paving 进士第 / Jinshi Di (Jinshi Mansion) 芙蓉书院 / Furong Academy
11
路面铺砌
中文

3. 路面铺砌

楠溪江地区，雨季长达半年之久，因此，一些古老的宗族村落非常重视路面铺砌。大路小路都铺上石头，横断面呈平缓的弧形，向两侧的排水沟或水渠里排水。路面铺砌还有艺术加工，以条石和大小卵石配合或采用一些色彩斑斓的卵石在路面上拼成各种图案。年代久远的道路，经人们一脚一脚地踩踏，石面很光润，石缝里长出短短的野草，草和石的色彩和质地的对比，非常生动。这种路面美观大方，经济实用，渗透性好，有利于水土保持。楠溪江的村落中，路面铺设大致有三种形式。

（1）卵石路面。卵石在楠溪江取之不尽，因此卵石使用极为普通。卵石路中又可分为自然形态卵石路面，席纹路面，以及与条石结合铺设的各种路面。

其中，卵石与条石结合铺砌的路面用在比较重要的干道和商业街上，是最普通、最常见的一类。条石沿道路的中线铺砌，很能强调道路的修长和方向，造成村子空间深远的景观。各式卵石路面也有用在干道上的，但不普遍，常用在次要道路和小路上。路面上所用卵石大都在14.0厘米×14.0厘米上下。为避免卵石错动，在铺设之前，须先做路基。底上素土夯实，再铺一层沙、石、土混合的垫层，然后才铺卵石。卵石平面朝上，棱角向下嵌入垫层土中。依据卵石的形状相互挤住，使整个路面形成一个牢固的整体。

（2）规则石块路面。规则石块即经过人工稍加打凿比较平整的石块。它多用于村中最主要的大道上。铺法有两种，平铺和"人"字斜铺。枫林镇商业街路面采用石块平铺法，芙蓉村东门大道和东皋村中心广场前大道均为斜铺"人"字式，石块尺寸约19.0厘米见方。路面构造做法与卵石路面相同，但大失灵气。

（3）路面装饰。在石路面铺设到一定长度后，常插入一些图案，来丰富单调的路面，如几何图案、花纹、文字等，有些图案则是为配合街巷的风水，表达一种吉祥意义。如芙蓉村如意街上的几何图案，如苍坡村溪门地面的太师帽图案，芙蓉村东门路面的铜钱纹图案，这些反映出人们祈求读书进仕，升官发财的愿望。岩头村的浚水街和进士街的路口。有细卵石镶拼的精致图案。周宅村中心土地庙前的"十八对莲花"则是最华丽的。蓬溪、溪口、枫林也都有这种装饰。

English

3. Road Surface Paving (Lu Mian Pu Qi)

Nanxi River region, rainy season as long as half a year; therefore, some ancient clan villages attach great importance to road surface paving. Main roads and small roads all paved with stones; cross-section gentle arc, draining to both sides drainage ditches or canals.

Road surface paving also has artistic processing; using strip stones and large-small cobblestones combined, or using some colorful cobblestones to拼 various patterns on road surface. Age-old roads, after people step by step trampling, stone surface very smooth; stone gaps grow short wild grass; grass and stone color and texture contrast, very vivid.

This road surface beautiful-generous, economical-practical, good permeability, conducive to soil-water conservation. In Nanxi River villages, road surface paving roughly three forms.

(1) cobblestone road surface. Cobblestones in Nanxi River inexhaustible; therefore cobblestone use extremely common. cobblestone roads again can be divided into natural-form cobblestone road, mat-pattern road, and various roads combined with strip stones.

Among them, cobblestone-strip-stone combined paved road used on relatively important main roads and commercial streets; is most common, most frequently seen type. strip stones paved along road centerline; very can emphasize road's slender and direction, creating village space deep landscape.

Various cobblestone roads also used on main roads, but not common; commonly used on secondary roads and small paths. Road surface used cobblestones mostly about 14.0cm×14.0cm. To avoid cobblestone shifting, before paving, must first make roadbed. Bottom plain soil rammed; then lay a layer sand-stone-soil mixed cushion; then才 pave cobblestones.

Cobblestone flat face upward, edges downward embedded into cushion soil. According to cobblestone shapes mutually squeeze, making entire road surface form a firm whole.

(2) Regular stone-block road surface. Regular stone blocks i.e. stones经过人工 slightly chiseled relatively flat. It mostly used on village most main avenues. Paving methods two: flat paving and "人" (herringbone) oblique paving.

Fenglin Town commercial street road surface uses stone-block flat paving; Furong village east gate avenue and Donggao village central square front avenue all oblique "人" style; stone block size about 19.0cm square. Road surface construction practice same as cobblestone road, but greatly loses vitality.

(3) Road surface decoration. After stone road paved to certain length, often insert some patterns to enrich monotonous road surface, e.g. geometric patterns,花纹, characters etc.; some patterns则 to配合 street-lane fengshui, express an auspicious meaning. E.g. Furong village ruyi street geometric pattern; e.g.

Cangpo village stream gate ground taishi-hat pattern; Furong village east gate road surface copper-coin pattern; these reflect people's desire for study-officialdom, promotion-wealth. Yantou village Junshui Street and jinshi Street intersections. Have fine cobblestone inlaid exquisite patterns.

Zhouzhai village central Earth God temple front "eighteen pairs of lotus"则 is most gorgeous. Pengxi, Xikou, Fenglin also have this decoration.

本篇术语 Key Terms

路面铺砌 / Road surface paving 卵石路面 / Cobblestone road surface 席纹路面 / Mat-pattern road surface 规则石块路面 / Regular stone-block road surface 人字斜铺 / Herringbone oblique paving 路基 / Roadbed (Lu ji) 垫层 / Cushion layer (Dian ceng) 铜钱纹 / Copper-coin pattern 太师帽 / Taishi hat pattern 如意街 / Ruyi Street 丽水街 / Lishui Street
12
墙体做法
中文

4. 墙体做法

楠溪江建筑的墙体，主要有砖墙与石墙两种，它们的用材、构造和审美价值都完全不同，而最有乡土特色的是石墙。

（1）砖墙。公共建筑及较好的住宅，后檐墙和山墙多用砖墙围护。所用砖均为手工制作，规格多、差异大，常用的有28.0厘米×12.5厘米×3.8厘米和28.0厘米×85.0厘米×5.2厘米。由于砖墙只起围护作用，不承重，因此多使用空斗做法，有三顺一丁，二顺一丁、一顺一丁及各种变异形式。使用最多的为三顺一丁。空斗墙不但节省材料，也有良好的保温隔热性能，而且美观。但不很牢固，因此，大多数空斗墙下部砌一段实砖墙或毛石墙，也有不少用条砖平砌双层墙的，一般高度约0.5~0.8米，增强墙体的稳定性和牢固性。

有些墙，自地面起就做空斗，在高约1.5米左右处置一条横枋，使墙体稳定。个别讲究的住宅，如廊下村"进士第"之类，石墙裙有高到1.8米的。

建筑的围护墙大多非常朴素，为美观，常常在砖墙上涂一层0.1~0.2厘米厚的白灰。毛石、素木、白粉对比十分醒目。少数山墙上，还保留着一种古老的装饰，即悬鱼、惹草。这类装饰在宋代使用较多，有实际功能：悬鱼用在悬山两坡博风板的上端结合处，用来保护脊檩头。惹草保护以下的檩头。到清代这两个构件的功能完全由博风板承担，于是它们逐渐退化，后来官式建筑中干脆取消，不再使用。楠溪江民居不用博风板，所以有些房屋的山面依旧采用这种构件。

在硬山山墙上也有这类构件的残迹，但尺度有意夸大，是用灰浆在墙上薄薄地塑出来的，只作为装饰之用，与构件已经没有关系。在零落的装饰上还能隐约看到施彩绘的痕迹，想见在完整时一定有很强的装饰效果。

墀头为山墙的檐部收头，为的是结束硬山屋檐前缘的侧面，也是重要的装饰，装饰特征主要在于线脚进退和方圆的变化。楠溪江地区墀头多用在祠堂和砖门楼上，挑出很大，与建筑本身舒展流畅的风格一致。

（2）石墙。石墙是楠溪江建筑墙体最具特色的一种。石材来源丰富，经济实用。石头有各种各样，砌法也有不同的风格。滩石即卵石，有长圆形、方圆形，有大块，有小块，色泽从深褐色到灰白色变化很多。乡民们顺应和尊重自然的本色，利用它们的大小、形状、色泽，砌筑成厚重结实而各具风采的墙体，使天然石材的美更加显露出来，也使村落仿佛成了自然的一部分。常见做法是：

第一，乱石墙。用大小不等的乱石砌成。有些是上段的石块较小，下段的石块较大。

第二，突变式墙。下部用大而均匀的卵石，砌到高约1.20米左右，全部改用匀等的较小卵石，没有过渡段。

第三，下部为较大的乱石砌，墙体厚重，顶部为扁长形的不十分规矩的"人"字图案，厚度较小，中部为二者的过渡，由下而上，乱砌渐渐趋近"人"字形，这过渡的自然、巧妙，不露痕迹，极其精致，可谓神品。

楠溪江民居石墙中最有特色、最大量、也最美观的一种是第三种。

垒砌卵石墙看似容易，做起来难。首先用大块毛石打好基础，然后分内外两皮砌筑，各自微向后倾，两皮之间自然互相挤紧而稳定。不用砂浆、灰泥或其他填充料。墙体自下而上逐渐减薄，形成缓慢的弧面收分，底部放出很多，造型美观又显得十分稳定。收头或转角，都要使用大块蛮石加强稳定性，为结实牢固，大石块的大小两面，即丁头和长身交替砌筑。

每逢雨天，卵石上的浮土冲去，露出石头的本来色彩，青绿的、柿黄的、灰白的，泛着水光，极其艳丽。梅雨季节，石墙上还长出一层茸茸的绿苔，时间久了，野藤薜荔也悄悄地爬上石墙。一些人家还在墙头栽上葱和萱草，嫩绿中金色的花朵灿烂如阳光，使石墙仿佛有了生命，有了情感。

（3）笆片灰墙。这种墙用竹片编成笆，固定在梁、柱和龙骨枋子上，两面抹上灰泥。这类做法在一般住宅常用于山墙，用于前后檐的也有。它廉价，取材方便，易于施工，轻而且薄。

English

4. wall construction Practices (Qiang Ti Zuo Fa)

Nanxi River architecture walls, mainly brick walls and stone walls two types; their materials, construction, aesthetic values all completely different; and most vernacular characteristic is stone wall.

(1) Brick walls. Public buildings and better residences, rear-eave walls and gable walls mostly use brick walls enclosure. Used bricks all handmade; specifications many, differences large; commonly used 28.0cm×12.5cm×3.8cm and 28.0cm×85.0cm×5.2cm.

Because brick wall only enclosure function, not load-bearing, therefore mostly use hollow-bucket (kong dou) practice; has three-stretch-one-header, two-stretch-one-header, one-stretch-one-header and various variant forms. Most used is three-stretch-one-header.

hollow-bucket wall not only saves materials, also has good thermal insulation performance, and beautiful.

But not very firm; therefore, most hollow-bucket walls lower part build a section solid brick wall or rubble stone wall; also many use strip bricks flat-laid double-layer wall, generally height about 0.5-0.8m, enhancing wall stability and firmness.

Some walls, from ground up make hollow-bucket, at height about 1.5m place a horizontal tie beam, making wall stable. Individual refined residences, e.g. Langxia village "jinshi Di" etc., stone wall plinth as high as 1.8m.

Building enclosure walls mostly very plain; for beauty, often on brick wall apply a layer 0.1-0.2cm thick white lime. Rubble stone, plain wood, white plaster contrast very eye-catching. Few gable walls, still retain an ancient decoration, i.e. xuan yu (hanging fish), re cao (provoking grass).

Such decorations in Song dynasty used more, had practical function: xuan yu used at overhanging-gable two-slope barge-boards upper end junction, used to protect ridge purlin head. Re cao protects below purlin heads.

To Qing dynasty these two members' functions completely borne by barge boards; then they gradually degenerated; later official architecture simply canceled, no longer used. Nanxi River residences do not use barge boards; so some houses' gable sides still use this member.

On flush-gable gable walls also have such member remnants, but scale intentionally exaggerated; is using mortar on wall thinly modeled, only as decoration, no longer related to member. On scattered decoration can faintly see painted traces; imagine when complete must have strong decorative effect.

chi tou (gable wall eave end) is gable wall eave termination, for ending flush-gable eave front edge side, also important decoration; decorative feature mainly in molding advance-retreat and square-round changes. Nanxi River region chi tou mostly used on ancestral halls and brick gate towers, projects large, consistent with building itself elegant-flowing style.

(2) Stone walls. Stone wall is Nanxi River architecture wall most characteristic type. Stone sources abundant, economical-practical. Stones various; masonry also different styles. Beach stones i.e. cobblestones, have oblong, round-square, large blocks, small blocks; colors from dark brown to gray-white vary much.

villagers conform to and respect natural colors; using their size, shape, color, build into thick-firm and distinctive walls, making natural stone beauty more revealed, also making village as if became part of nature. Common practices:

First, random-stone wall. Built with unequal-size random stones. Some are upper section stones smaller, lower section stones larger.

Second, abrupt-change wall. Lower part uses large-uniform cobblestones, built to height about 1.20m, all change to uniform smaller cobblestones, no transition section.

Third, lower part is larger random-stone masonry, wall thick; top is oblong not very regular "人" (herringbone) pattern, thickness smaller; middle is transition of two; from bottom upward, random masonry gradually approaches "人" shape; this transition natural, ingenious, no trace, extremely refined, can be called divine product.

Nanxi River residence stone walls most characteristic, most大量, also most beautiful type is third.

Building cobblestone wall看似 easy, actually difficult. First use large rubble stones lay foundation; then divide inner-outer two skins masonry, each slightly backward-tilt; between two skins naturally mutually squeeze tight and stable. No mortar, plaster or other filler.

Wall from bottom upward gradually thins, forming gentle curved batter; bottom projects much;造型 beautiful and appears very stable. Termination or corner, must use large rough stones to strengthen stability; for firmness, large stones' size two faces, i.e. header and stretcher alternately masonry.

Every rainy day, cobblestones' surface soil washed away, revealing stones' original colors: green-blue, persimmon-yellow, gray-white, shimmering with water, extremely gorgeous. Plum-rain season, stone walls also grow a layer fuzzy green moss; over time, wild vines and climbing figs also quietly climb stone walls.

Some households also plant scallions and daylilies on wall tops; tender green with golden flowers brilliant as sunlight, making stone wall as if with life, with emotion.

(3) bamboo-mat plaster wall. This wall uses bamboo strips woven into mat, fixed on beams, columns and keel tie beams; both sides plaster mud. This practice in general residences commonly used for gable walls; used for front-rear eaves also some. It cheap, materials convenient, easy construction, light and thin.

本篇术语 Key Terms

墙体做法 / Wall construction practices 空斗墙 / Hollow-bucket wall (Kong dou qiang) 三顺一丁 / Three-stretch-one-header bond 悬鱼 / Xuan yu (hanging fish decoration) 惹草 / Re cao (provoking grass decoration) 博风板 / Barge board (Bo feng ban) 墀头 / Chi tou (gable wall eave end) 乱石墙 / Random-stone wall 卵石墙 / Cobblestone wall 笆片灰墙 / Bamboo-mat plaster wall 人字图案 / Herringbone pattern 硬山 / Flush gable (Ying shan) 悬山 / Overhanging gable (Xuan shan) 石墙裙 / Stone wall plinth
13
漏窗与漏墙
中文

5. 漏窗与漏墙

在砖石墙上开窗，形式与构造完全不同于木构架上的小木装修，成为漏窗。漏窗扩大，占了很大的墙面，就产生了漏墙。

（1）漏窗。在楠溪江中游村落里，砖石的围护墙上，为通风采光和装饰墙面而开的窗口，窗棂花式，或用整块石板精凿，或用花砖组合。各种各样的空透图案，朴素简洁，墙面显得生动活泼。漏窗花式数不胜数。有代表性且普遍的几种做法是：

回纹窗。以回纹为基本构图形式并常仿木窗的做法，在每个节点上刻出榫卯的插接线，有的还适当配上一些小花饰，是比较普遍的一种。

三角组合窗。用大小相等的砖条，砌成一个个等边三角形，再组成图案。

六边形组合窗。用砖组成一个个六边形格子，很似龟背纹。

十字矩形窗，以十字和矩形图案共同组成。节点上加梅花朵。

锦纹窗。用简单的几何图案构成，图案常有变化。如矩形纹，圆形纹。

玫瑰窗。这种花窗具有西洋装饰的味道，显然仿温州传来的天主教堂中的玫瑰窗。它常用在砖门楼正面顶上，或用在房屋的山墙部位。

有些漏窗用透雕的石板做花格。

漏窗既没有窗扇，也没有窗纱窗纸。正如唐代诗人皮日休的《石窗》诗所说：

苔染浑成绮，云漫便当纱。

棂中空吐月，扉际不扃霞。

晚近有在砖石花格后面加玻璃窗扇的。

（2）漏砖墙。又叫花墙，用专门烧制的异型花砖制成，楠溪江中游地区使用十分普遍，常用在住宅和祠堂的院墙砖门楼两侧八字墙上部。突出并美化建筑入口。质量比较高的大住宅，是十分讲究入口装饰处理的。也可在砖围墙上部形成一个极华丽的装饰带。少数的，面积较大，如花坛某宅、蓬溪村"近云山舍"院内隔墙，埭头某宗祠院墙，苍坡村的水月堂等。漏砖花墙种类很多，常见有回纹式，十字式、菱花式三种。

第一，回纹式，以回纹图案组成漏砖花墙。有单纯回纹式，也有掺入其他小装饰品的，如小小的花卉、树木、人物、动物、灯笼等砖雕，但主体回纹形式不变。

第二，十字式，薄砖组成空十字形、海棠角，有小小的装饰砖雕点缀。如芙蓉村"大宅"书塾墙面，岩头村某宅院墙。

第三，菱花式，这种花墙图案是用瓦片组合成的。使用不多，如水云村某宅。

English

5. lattice windows and lattice walls (Lou Chuang yu Lou Qiang)

Opening windows on brick-stone walls, form and construction completely different from small-timber joinery on timber frames, become lattice windows (lou chuang). lattice window enlarged, occupying large wall surface, then produces lattice wall (lou qiang).

(1) lattice windows. In Nanxi River middle villages, brick-stone enclosure walls, for ventilation-lighting and wall decoration opened windows; window mullion patterns, either use whole stone slab finely chiseled, or use decorative bricks combined. Various openwork patterns, plain-simple, wall surface appears lively. lattice window patterns countless. Representative and common several practices:

Meander-window (hui wen chuang). Using meander as basic composition form and often imitates wooden window practice; at each node carves mortise-tenon insertion lines; some also appropriately match some small flower decorations; is relatively common type.

Triangle-combination window. Using equal-size brick strips, builds equilateral triangles one by one, then composes patterns.

Hexagon-combination window. Using bricks composes hexagon grids one by one, very like tortoise-shell pattern.

Cross-rectangle window, using cross and rectangle patterns jointly composed. Nodes add plum blossoms.

Brocade-pattern window. Using simple geometric patterns composed; patterns often vary. E.g. rectangle pattern, circle pattern.

rose window. This decorative window has Western decorative flavor; obviously imitates rose window in Catholic church transmitted from Wenzhou. It commonly used on brick gate tower front top, or used on building gable wall position.

Some lattice windows use openwork-carved stone slabs as lattice.

lattice window neither has window sash, nor window screen-paper. Just as Tang dynasty poet Pi Rixiu's "Stone Window" poem says:

Moss dyes entirely into brocade, clouds spread conveniently as gauze.

Mullions hollowly emit moon, door edge does not lock rosy clouds.

Recently some add glass window sashes behind brick-stone lattice.

(2) lattice brick wall. Also called flower wall; made of specially fired special-shaped decorative bricks; Nanxi River middle region use very common; commonly used on residence and ancestral hall courtyard wall brick gate tower both sides splayed-wall upper part. Highlights and beautifies building entrance.

Higher-quality large residences, very particular about entrance decoration treatment. Also can form an extremely gorgeous decorative band on brick enclosure wall upper part. Few, area larger, e.g. Huatan某 residence, Pengxi village "jinyun shan she" courtyard partition wall, Daitou某 ancestral hall courtyard wall, Cangpo village shuiyue hall etc.

Lattice brick flower wall types many; common have meander style, cross style, rhombus-flower style three.

First, meander style, using meander pattern composes lattice brick flower wall. Has pure meander style, also has mixed other small decorations, e.g. tiny flowers, trees, figures, animals, lanterns etc. brick carvings, but main meander form unchanged.

Second, cross style, thin bricks compose hollow cross shape, begonia corners; has small decorative brick carving embellishments. E.g. Furong village "Da Zhai" academy wall, Yantou village某 residence courtyard wall.

Third, rhombus-flower style, this flower wall pattern is composed of tile pieces. Use not much, e.g. Shuiyun village某 residence.

本篇术语 Key Terms

漏窗 / Lattice window (Lou chuang) 漏墙 / Lattice wall (Lou qiang) 漏砖墙 / Lattice brick wall 花墙 / Flower wall (Hua qiang) 回纹窗 / Meander-pattern window 玫瑰窗 / Rose window 龟背纹 / Tortoise-shell pattern 海棠角 / Begonia corner (Hai tang jiao) 八字墙 / Splayed wall (Ba zi qiang) 皮日休 / Pi Rixiu (Tang poet) 近云山舍 / Jinyun Shan She 水月堂 / Shuiyue Hall
14
屋面做法
中文

6. 屋面做法

屋面除了它的实际功能外，还要表现风格，追求一种美。在铺瓦的过程中，可根据各种相关情况对大木构架的举折和生起略作调整，使建筑的屋面更加完整舒展。

（1）屋脊。屋脊是表现建筑精神的重要部分。屋脊做得好，能使建筑大大增色。楠溪江的公共建筑，如祠堂、庙宇、书院，正脊都做得简洁庄重，很少使用花哨的手法。脊端做成与屋脊曲线十分和谐的卷草，翘起，而反曲，完美有力。十分生动有生气。

岩头村进士牌楼是清代嘉靖年间金霞峰考取了进士后建造的，为了装饰和炫耀，做法就接近官式，但装饰题材有地方特色，如正脊正吻虽然做龙头形吻饰，两垂脊上却塑了两只鸡，乡土味十足。

在住宅中，屋脊做法也很简单，但脊的两端装饰性比较强，有的做很轻盈通透卷曲有致的花饰。到了晚期，砖门楼上的脊很华丽，不但有中国传统图案装饰，还融合有西洋风格的图案，如火焰纹，玫瑰纹等。也有在正脊中间做通透图形的。在脊头上，装饰物多为生活中常见的，乡土气息十分浓厚的题材，如花盆葵花、花盆万年青、鱼、鸡、狗等，令人见了耳目一新，很喜兴，但艺术上不很成熟。

（2）瓦面收头及檩椽头的保护。楠溪江地区建筑通常用25厘米×20厘米左右的板瓦，极个别建筑如牌楼才使用筒瓦，直接将瓦叠摞在椽子上面。

瓦垄上部靠近正脊处和下部靠近檐口处的瓦比较密，常压七露三，其他部位的瓦大都压一半露一半，这样就加强了举折的效果。悬山靠边收头处也一样，常叠压两三层瓦，这样，既加强了瓦顶的抗风能力，又使两端结束处逐渐增高，调整和完善屋面弧度，加强生起效果。

为不让椽头直接受日晒雨淋，檐口的滴水和瓦当都很大，一片紧挨一片，完全遮挡住椽头。滴水有长方铲形，25厘米×20厘米，有扁长形，10厘米×20厘米，有如意形，有如意铲形，还有正方形。滴水瓦上常有浅浮雕花纹，如福寿文字、蝙蝠、兽头、水波纹、卷草纹、万年青。雨水盛时，瓦面及滴水瓦上当长出绿色蒙蒙的青苔，十分苍古。

为保护椽头，还用一种很薄的方砖，一块块钉在椽头上，密接成整齐的一排，使椽头不受雨水侵袭。这种做法比起用大滴水瓦来效果好得多。也有用木板做护椽板的，木板易糟朽，效果不如砖的，但更换木板毕竟比更换椽子方便。悬山屋顶，一般都将檩子头露在山面上，很易腐烂，于是把瓦钉在檩头上，加以保护。檩头上有升头木，脊檩上除升头木外还有扶脊木，所以钉在檩头上的瓦比较大，脊檩上往往有上、下两块，将整个暴露的檩头、升头木、扶脊木挡好为止。

English

6. roof construction Practices (Wu Mian Zuo Fa)

roof besides its practical function, also must express style, pursue a kind of beauty. In tile-laying process, can according to various related situations slightly adjust large-timber frame's ju-zhe and sheng-qi, making building roof more complete-elegant.

(1) roof ridge. roof ridge is important part expressing building spirit. roof ridge done well, can greatly enhance building. Nanxi River public buildings, e.g. ancestral halls, temples, academies, main ridge all done simple-dignified, rarely use fancy methods. Ridge end made scrolling grass very harmonious with roof ridge curve, upturned, and reverse-curved, perfect-powerful. Very vivid-lively.

Yantou village jinshi archway was built after Jin Xiafeng passed jinshi exam in Qing Jiajing era; for decoration and炫耀, practice close to official style, but decorative themes have local characteristics, e.g. main ridge zheng wen (ridge ornament) although made dragon-head form ornament, two vertical ridges却 molded two chickens, full of vernacular flavor.

In residences, roof ridge practice also very simple, but ridge both ends decorative relatively strong; some make very light-transparent curled elegant flower decorations. To late period, brick gate tower ridges very gorgeous; not only have Chinese traditional pattern decoration, also integrate Western-style patterns, e.g.

flame pattern, rose pattern etc. Also some make transparent图形 in main ridge middle. On ridge ends, decorations mostly common in life, very strong vernacular atmosphere themes, e.g. flowerpot sunflower, flowerpot evergreen, fish, chicken, dog etc., making people see fresh, very festive, but artistically not very mature.

(2) Tile surface termination and purlin-rafter head protection. Nanxi River region buildings usually use about 25cm×20cm flat tiles (ban wa); extremely individual buildings such as archways才 use cylindrical tiles (tong wa), directly stack tiles on rafters.

tile row upper part near main ridge and lower part near eave tiles relatively dense; often press seven expose three; other parts tiles mostly press half expose half; thus strengthens ju-zhe effect.

Overhanging-gable edge termination also same; often stack two-three layers tiles; thus, both strengthens tile roof wind resistance, and makes both ends termination gradually rise, adjusting and perfecting roof arc, strengthening sheng-qi effect.

To not let rafter head directly sun-rain exposed, eave drip tiles (di shui) and tile-ends (wa dang) both very large; one next to one, completely遮挡 rafter heads. drip tiles have rectangular-shovel shape, 25cm×20cm; have oblong shape, 10cm×20cm; have ruyi shape; have ruyi-shovel shape; also have square.

drip tiles often have low-relief flower patterns, e.g. fu-shou characters, bats, beast heads, water-wave pattern, scrolling-grass pattern, evergreen. When rain abundant, tile surface and drip tiles grow green fuzzy moss, very ancient.

To protect rafter heads, also use a very thin square brick, one by one nailed on rafter heads, closely joined into neat row, making rafter heads not rain attacked. This practice compared to using large drip tiles effect much better.

Also some use wood boards as rafter-protection boards; wood boards easy rot, effect not as good as brick, but replacing wood boards after all more convenient than replacing rafters. Overhanging-gable roof, generally expose purlin heads on gable surface, very easy rot; then nail tiles on purlin heads, to protect.

Purlin heads have sheng-tou-mu; ridge purlin besides sheng-tou-mu also has ridge-supporting timber; so tiles nailed on purlin heads relatively large; ridge purlin often has upper-lower two pieces, until entire exposed purlin head, sheng-tou-mu, ridge-supporting timber well blocked.

本篇术语 Key Terms

屋面做法 / Roof construction practices 屋脊 / Roof ridge (Wu ji) 正脊 / Main ridge (Zheng ji) 垂脊 / Vertical ridge (Chui ji) 正吻 / Zheng wen (ridge ornament) 板瓦 / Flat tile (Ban wa) 筒瓦 / Cylindrical tile (Tong wa) 滴水 / Drip tile (Di shui) 瓦当 / Tile-end (Wa dang) 瓦垄 / Tile row (Wa long) 压七露三 / Press seven expose three (tile overlap) 护椽板 / Rafter-protection board 进士牌楼 / Jinshi Archway 火焰纹 / Flame pattern

Postscript

后记
中文

后记

这次楠溪江中游乡土建筑研究是在1990年10月至1991年12月之间进行的。这期间有调查，有测绘，有摄影，陆陆续续好几次。主持这次研究的是陈志华、楼庆西和李秋香，另外有十一位学生参加了测绘，他们是丘健、张旗、葛志红、何培基、莫军、杨哲怡、潘彤、刘晓梅、张葵、周榕和陈彤。陈志华主要负责研究的整体设计，撰写了本书的前言和第一、二、三篇。楼庆西主要负责摄影。李秋香主要负责测绘，并且撰写了本书的第四篇。研究生舒楠也参加了测绘。建筑学院资料室负责照片的管理和制作。

这次研究的全部经费由台湾龙虎文化基金会支付，由《汉声》杂志社编印出版。下决心放下手边熟悉了的工作，从头做乡土建筑研究，原因之一是急于抢救这些资料。大量珍贵的建筑遗产正在迅速毁灭，永远的失去，不给它们留下一份记录是不可原谅的错误。在研究过程之中，短短一年时间里，楠溪江的乡土建筑就继续破坏了不少。有些是因年久失修而倒塌，如构成坦下村美丽的轮廓的一栋住宅。有些是被拆除，以便在它们的基地上造新房子，如芙蓉村的一栋住宅。有些是遭到意外的灾害，如楠溪江唯一幸存下来的一座书院，被一场大火烧光了明伦堂。

有些被改造成现代建筑，如岩头村丽水街上的老店面。更教人心焦如焚的，是有些建筑被热心的"保护"者修缮得面目全非，如苍坡村的东池上造了钢筋混泥土的"九曲桥"，仁济庙屋脊上塑起了两条五颜六色的游龙，张牙舞爪，与朴素淡雅的楠溪江村落格格不入。这本书里的有些测绘图和照片，刚刚完成，对象就已经没有了，或者彻底改变了。因此，保存了这些资料值得庆幸，而资料的不够全面，不够细致，又成了永远的遗憾。虽然楠溪江乡土建筑还有许多课题待做，但这一次的研究不得不结束了。

这一次工作的收获之一是更深入、更真切地认识到了乡土建筑的研究非做不可，要快做，赶紧做，迫不得已地做，应该以很大的规模去做。这次工作的另一收获是，认识到了工作的一些难点。例如，乡土建筑大多数不能鉴定年代，不容易弄清村落的规划和建筑的变化过程，有时候是追索原状还是着眼于现状，也举棋难定。

本书虽然还是着眼于现状，但详细写了第二章"耕读生活与山水情怀"和第三章"村落规划和建设的工作机制"，以探讨聚落和建筑形成的社会根由以及它们所反映的文化心理，但是，由于缺乏足够的文字资料，农村里又早已没有了了解过去生活的人，这两章写得不很充分。

还有一些通常认为很简单的技术，也成了难题，例如，这些用原木和蛮石建造的房屋，应该怎样制图，本书采用尺规制图，失去了它们粗犷的野性，而如实地画出弯弯曲曲的柱子、梁枋和卵石墙，又几乎是不可能的。采用折中的方法，徒手画直线，恐怕既得不到野性的神韵，又显得草率。以后的每一个课题，怎样捕捉它们各自的特色，大概也会是一个难点。希望在乡土建筑的研究中，每次都有所发现，有所发明，有所创造，有所前进，而不是停留在一个模式上。

1992年春

English

Postscript

This Nanxi River middle vernacular architecture research was conducted between October 1990 and December 1991. During this period there were investigations, surveys, photography, several times intermittently.

Presiding over this research were Chen Zhihua, Lou Qingxi, and Li Qiuxiang; additionally eleven students participated in surveying: Qiu Jian, Zhang Qi, Ge Zhihong, He Peiji, Mo Jun, Yang Zheyi, Pan Tong, Liu Xiaomei, Zhang Kui, Zhou Rong, and Chen Tong.

Chen Zhihua mainly responsible for overall research design, wrote book's preface and Parts One, Two, Three. Lou Qingxi mainly responsible for photography. Li Qiuxiang mainly responsible for surveying, and wrote book's Part Four. Graduate student Shu Nan also participated in surveying.

Architecture School reference room responsible for photo management and production.

All funding for this research paid by Taiwan Longhu Cultural Foundation; published by "Echo" (Hansheng) Magazine. Determined to put aside familiar work at hand, to start vernacular architecture research from scratch, one reason was urgency to rescue these materials.

Large amounts of precious architectural heritage are rapidly being destroyed, forever lost; not leaving a record for them is an unforgivable mistake. During research process, in just one year, Nanxi River vernacular architecture continued to be damaged considerably. Some collapsed due to long-neglected disrepair, e.g.

a residence forming tanxia village's beautiful silhouette. Some were demolished to build new houses on their sites, e.g. a residence in Furong village. Some suffered accidental disasters, e.g. Nanxi River's only surviving academy, its minglun hall burned down by a great fire. Some were transformed into modern buildings, e.g.

old storefronts on Yantou village lishui street. Even more heart-wrenching, some buildings were restored beyond recognition by enthusiastic "protectors", e.g.

Cangpo village's East pond built a reinforced concrete "nine-bend bridge"; renji temple roof ridge molded two colorful roaming dragons, baring fangs and brandishing claws, incompatible with plain-elegant Nanxi River villages. Some survey drawings and photos in this book, just completed, the objects already gone, or completely changed.

Therefore, preserving these materials is fortunate, while materials not comprehensive enough, not detailed enough, became eternal regret. Although Nanxi River vernacular architecture still has many topics to do, this research had to end.

One收获 of this work is more deeply, more truly recognizing that vernacular architecture research must be done, must be done quickly, urgently, necessarily, should be done on a large scale. Another收获 of this work is recognizing some difficulties of the work.

E.g., vernacular architecture most cannot date; not easy to clarify village planning and building change process; sometimes whether to trace original state or focus on current state, also indecisive.

This book although still focuses on current state, but detailed wrote Chapter Two "farming-study Life and landscape sentiment" and Chapter Three "village planning and construction Work Mechanism", to explore social roots of settlement and architecture formation and cultural psychology they reflect, but, due to lack of sufficient written materials, and villages long ago no people who understand past life, these two chapters not written sufficiently.

Also some usually considered simple technologies, became difficult problems, e.g., these houses built with raw logs and rough stones, how should they be drawn; this book uses ruler-compass drawing, losing their rough wildness, while truthfully drawing crooked columns, beams-fang, and cobblestone walls, almost impossible.

Using compromise method, freehand straight lines,恐怕 neither obtains wild charm, nor appears careless. Future each topic, how to capture their respective characteristics, probably also will be a difficulty. Hope in vernacular architecture research, each time have discoveries, inventions, creations, progress, rather than staying in one model.

Spring 1992
